




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


paṭisambhidāmaggapāḷi


1. mahāvaggau

mātikā

1. sautāvadhānai  pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

2. sutvāna saṃvarai pañ‍ñā sīlamayai ñāṇaṃ|

3. saṃvaritvā samādahanai pañ‍ñā samādhibhāvanāmayai ñāṇaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapariggahai pañ‍ñā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ|

5. atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅkhipitvā vavatthānai pañ‍ñā sammasanai ñāṇaṃ|

6. pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ dhammānaṃ vipariṇāmānupassanai pañ‍ñā udayabbayānupassanai ñāṇaṃ|

7. ārammaṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhā bhaṅgānupassanai pañ‍ñā vipassanai ñāṇaṃ|

8. bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ|

9. muñ‍citukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā  pañ‍ñā saṅkhārupaikkhāsu ñāṇaṃ|

10. bahiddhā vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā gautrabhuñāṇaṃ|

11. dubhatau vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ|

12. payaugappaṭippassaddhi pañ‍ñā phalai ñāṇaṃ|

13. chin‍navaṭumānupassanai  chin‍namanupassanai (syā॰), chin‍navaṭṭamanupassanai (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) pañ‍ñā vimuttiñāṇaṃ|

14. tadā samudāgatai samupāgatai (syā॰) dhammai passanai pañ‍ñā pac‍cavaikkhaṇai ñāṇaṃ|

15. ajjhattavavatthānai pañ‍ñā vatthunānattai ñāṇaṃ|

16. bahiddhāvavatthānai pañ‍ñā gaucaranānattai ñāṇaṃ|

17. cariyāvavatthānai pañ‍ñā cariyānānattai ñāṇaṃ|

18. catudhammavavatthānai pañ‍ñā bhūminānattai ñāṇaṃ|

19. navadhammavavatthānai pañ‍ñā dhammanānattai ñāṇaṃ|

20. abhiñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā ñātaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

21. pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā tīraṇaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

22. pahānai pañ‍ñā paric‍cāgaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

23. bhāvanāpañ‍ñā aikarasaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

24. sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñā phassanaṭṭhai phusanaṭṭhai (ka॰) ñāṇaṃ|

25. atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai  ñāṇaṃ|

26. dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

27. niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

28. paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhāṇanānattai (syā॰) paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

29. vihāranānattai pañ‍ñā vihāraṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

30. samāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā samāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

31. vihārasamāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā vihārasamāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

32. avikkhaipaparisuddhattā āsavasamucchaidai pañ‍ñā ānantarikasamādhimhi ñāṇaṃ|

33. dassanādhipataiyyaṃ santau ca vihārādhigamau paṇītādhimuttatā pañ‍ñā araṇavihārai ñāṇaṃ|

34. dvīhi  balaihi saman‍nāgatattā tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi vasibhāvatā pañ‍ñā niraudhasamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ|

35. sampajānassa pavattapariyādānai  pañ‍ñā parinibbānai ñāṇaṃ|

36. sabbadhammānaṃ sammā samucchaidai niraudhai ca anupaṭṭhānatā pañ‍ñā samasīsaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

37. puthunānattataijapariyādānai  pañ‍ñā sal‍laikhaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ|

38. asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ|

39. nānādhammappakāsanatā pañ‍ñā atthasandassanai ñāṇaṃ|

40. sabbadhammānaṃ aikasaṅgahatānānattaikattapaṭivaidhai pañ‍ñā dassanavisuddhiñāṇaṃ|

41. viditattā pañ‍ñā khantiñāṇaṃ|

42. phuṭṭhattā pañ‍ñā pariyaugāhaṇai pariyaugāhanai (syā॰) ñāṇaṃ|

43. samaudahanai pañ‍ñā padaisavihārai ñāṇaṃ|

44. adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

45. nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

46. adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

47. suñ‍ñatai pañ‍ñā ñāṇavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

48. vausaggai vaussaggai (bahūsu) pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

49. tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

50. kāyampi cittampi aikavavatthānatā sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānavasaina ijjhanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā iddhividhai ñāṇaṃ|

51. vitak‍kavipphāravasaina nānattaikattasaddanimittānaṃ pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇaṃ|

52. tiṇṇan‍naṃ  cittānaṃ vipphārattā indriyānaṃ pasādavasaina nānattaikattaviñ‍ñāṇacariyā pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā caitaupariyañāṇaṃ|

53. pac‍cayappavattānaṃ dhammānaṃ nānattaikattakammavipphāravasaina pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ|

54. aubhāsavasaina nānattaikattarūpanimittānaṃ dassanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ|

55. catusaṭṭhiyā ākāraihi tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ vasībhāvatā  pañ‍ñā āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ|

56. pariñ‍ñaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dukkhai ñāṇaṃ|

57. pahānaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā samudayai  ñāṇaṃ|

58. sacchikiriyaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā niraudhai ñāṇaṃ|

59. bhāvanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ|

60. dukkhai ñāṇaṃ|

61. dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ|

62. dukkhaniraudhai  ñāṇaṃ|

63. dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ|

64. atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

65. dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

66. niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

67. paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

68. indriyaparaupariyattai ñāṇaṃ|

69. sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ|

70. yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ|

71. mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ|

72. sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ|

73. anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

imāni taisattati ñāṇāni| imaisaṃ taisattatiyā ñāṇānaṃ sattasaṭṭhi ñāṇāni sāvakasādhāraṇāni; cha ñāṇāni asādhāraṇāni sāvakaihi|

mātikā niṭṭhitā|

1. ñāṇakathā

1. sutamayañāṇaniddaisau

1. kathaṃ  sautāvadhānai pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

‘‘imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā pahātabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā sacchikātabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā hānabhāgiyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā ṭhitibhāgiyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘imai dhammā nibbaidhabhāgiyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘sabbai  saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’’nti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ  dukkhasamudayau (syā॰) ariyasac‍ca’’nti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā ariyasac‍ca’’nti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’’nti  sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

2. kathaṃ  ‘‘imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

aikau dhammau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau – sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā| dvai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – dvai dhātuyau| tayau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – tissau dhātuyau| cattārau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| pañ‍ca dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanāni| cha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – cha anuttariyāni| satta dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – satta niddasavatthūni| aṭṭha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni| nava dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – nava anupubbavihārā| dasa dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā – dasa nij‍jaravatthūni|

3. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ? cakkhu cakkhuṃ (syā॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; cakkhusamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; yampidaṃ yamidaṃ (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.52 passitabbau cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| sautaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saddā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… ghānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; gandhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… jivhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… kāyau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phauṭṭhabbā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… manau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, manausamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|’’

rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; sañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saṅkhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

cakkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; sautaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ghānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jivhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; kāyau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; manau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| rūpā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saddā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gandhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; phauṭṭhabbā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| cakkhusamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sautasamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ghānasamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jivhāsamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; kāyasamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; manausamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; sautasamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ghānasamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; kāyasamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; manausamphassajā vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saddasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gandhasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dhammasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saddasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gandhasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dhammasañ‍caitanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saddataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gandhataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; phauṭṭhabbataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dhammataṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; saddavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; gandhavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rasavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phauṭṭhabbavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammavitak‍kau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| rūpavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; saddavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; gandhavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rasavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phauṭṭhabbavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammavicārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

4. pathavīdhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; āpaudhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; taijaudhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; vāyaudhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ākāsadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; viñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

pathavīkasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; āpaukasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; taijaukasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; vāyaukasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; nīlakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pītakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; lauhitakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; audātakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ākāsakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

kaisā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; laumā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; nakhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dantā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; tacau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau, maṃsaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; nhārū nahārū (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; aṭṭhī abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; aṭṭhimiñ‍jā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā aṭṭhimañ‍jaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ (syā॰ ka॰); vak‍kaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; hadayaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; yakanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; kilaumakaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pihakaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; papphāsaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; antaṃ  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ antaguṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; udariyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; karīsaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pittaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saimhaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pubbau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; lauhitaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saidau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maidau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; assu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; vasā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; khaiḷau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; siṅghāṇikā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; lasikā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; muttaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; matthaluṅgaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

cakkhāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| sautāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saddāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ghānāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; gandhāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jivhāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rasāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| kāyāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| manāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dhammāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

cakkhudhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| sautadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saddadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| ghānadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gandhadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| jivhādhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rasadhātu  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| kāyadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; phauṭṭhabbadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| manaudhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dhammadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

cakkhundriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; sautindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ghānindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jivhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; kāyindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; manindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jīvitindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; itthindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; purisindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; sukhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dukkhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saumanassindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; daumanassindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; upaikkhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saddhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; vīriyindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; satindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; samādhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pañ‍ñindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; añ‍ñindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

5. kāmadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; arūpadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| kāmabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; arūpabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| sañ‍ñābhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; asañ‍ñābhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| aikavaukārabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; catuvaukārabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pañ‍cavaukārabhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

6. paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamajjhānaṃ (syā॰) aivamīdisaisu ṭhānaisu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maittācaitauvimutti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; karuṇācaitauvimutti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; muditācaitauvimutti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; upaikkhācaitauvimutti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

avij‍jā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; saṅkhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; nāmarūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saḷāyatanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; phassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; taṇhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; upādānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; bhavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

7. dukkhaṃ  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dukkhasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… saṅkhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| cakkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jarāmaraṇasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

dukkhassa pariñ‍ñaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhasamudayassa pahānaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhassa sacchikiriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| rūpassa pariñ‍ñaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpasamudayassa pahānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhassa sacchikiriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa… cakkhussa…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa  pariñ‍ñaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇasamudayassa pahānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhassa sacchikiriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhasamudayassa pahānapaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhassa sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| rūpassa pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpasamudayassa pahānapaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhassa sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa… cakkhussa…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇasamudayassa pahānapaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhassa sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

8. dukkhaṃ  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dukkhasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa samudayaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa chandarāganiraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa ādīnavau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa samudayaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa chandarāganiraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa ādīnavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… saṅkhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ… cakkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jarāmaraṇasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa samudayaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa chandarāganiraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa ādīnavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

dukkhaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dukkhasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dukkhassa assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa ādīnavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpassa  assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa ādīnavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| vaidanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… saṅkhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ… cakkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; jarāmaraṇasamudayau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇassa assādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa ādīnavau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; jarāmaraṇassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

9. anic‍cānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dukkhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anattānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; nibbidānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; virāgānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; niraudhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; paṭinissaggānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| rūpai anic‍cānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai dukkhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai anattānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai nibbidānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai virāgānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai niraudhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; rūpai paṭinissaggānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇai dukkhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā;  jarāmaraṇai anattānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇai nibbidānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇai virāgānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇai niraudhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarāmaraṇai paṭinissaggānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

10. uppādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pavattaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; nimittaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; āyūhanā āyuhanā (syā॰) aivamuparipi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; paṭisandhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; gati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; nibbatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; upapatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; jarā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; byādhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyau, maraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saukau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paridaivau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; upāyāsau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

anuppādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; appavattaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; animittaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; anāyūhanā anāyuhanā (syā॰) aivamuparipi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; appaṭisandhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; agati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anibbatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anupapatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ajāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ajarā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; abyādhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; amataṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; asaukau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aparidaivau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anupāyāsau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

uppādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anuppādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pavattaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; appavattaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; animittaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anāyūhanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| paṭisandhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; appaṭisandhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| gati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; agati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| nibbatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anibbatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| upapatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anupapatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| jāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ajāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| jarā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ajarā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā| byādhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; abyādhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| maraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; amataṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; asaukau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| paridaivau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aparidaivau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| upāyāsau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anupāyāsau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

uppādau dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati  dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau dukkhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

anuppādau  sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appavattaṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| animittaṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anāyūhanā sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appaṭisandhi sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| agati sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anibbatti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupapatti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajāti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajarā  sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| abyādhi sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| amataṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| asaukau  sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| aparidaivau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupāyāsau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau dukkhaṃ, anuppādau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ dukkhaṃ, appavattaṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ dukkhaṃ, animittaṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā dukkhaṃ, anāyūhanā sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi dukkhaṃ, appaṭisandhi sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati dukkhaṃ, agati sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti dukkhaṃ, anibbatti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti dukkhaṃ, anupapatti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti dukkhaṃ, ajāti sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā dukkhaṃ, ajarā sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi dukkhaṃ, abyādhi sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ dukkhaṃ, amataṃ sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau dukkhaṃ, asaukau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau dukkhaṃ, aparidaivau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau dukkhaṃ, anupāyāsau sukhanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau bhayanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

anuppādau  khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appavattaṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| animittaṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anāyūhanā khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appaṭisandhi khaimanti  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| agati khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anibbatti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupapatti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajāti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajarā khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| abyādhi khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| amataṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| asaukau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| aparidaivau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupāyāsau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau  bhayaṃ, anuppādau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ bhayaṃ, appavattaṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ bhayaṃ, animittaṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā bhayaṃ, anāyūhanā khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi bhayaṃ, appaṭisandhi khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati bhayaṃ, agati khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti bhayaṃ, anibbatti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti bhayaṃ, anupapatti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti bhayaṃ, ajāti khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā bhayaṃ, ajarā khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi bhayaṃ, abyādhi khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ bhayaṃ, amataṃ khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau bhayaṃ, asaukau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau bhayaṃ, aparidaivau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau bhayaṃ, anupāyāsau khaimanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau sāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

anuppādau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appavattaṃ nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| animittaṃ nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anāyūhanā nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appaṭisandhi nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| agati nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anibbatti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupapatti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajāti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajarā nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| abyādhi nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| amataṃ nirāmisanti  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| asaukau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| aparidaivau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupāyāsau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau  sāmisaṃ, anuppādau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ sāmisaṃ, appavattaṃ nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ sāmisaṃ, animittaṃ nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā sāmisaṃ, anāyūhanā nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi sāmisaṃ, appaṭisandhi nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati sāmisaṃ, agati nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti sāmisaṃ, anibbatti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti sāmisaṃ, anupapatti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti sāmisaṃ, ajāti nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā sāmisaṃ, ajarā nirāmisanti  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi sāmisaṃ, abyādhi nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ sāmisaṃ, amataṃ nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau sāmisaṃ, asaukau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau sāmisaṃ, aparidaivau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau sāmisaṃ, anupāyāsau nirāmisanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau saṅkhārāti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

anuppādau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appavattaṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| animittaṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anāyūhanā nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| appaṭisandhi nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| agati nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anibbatti nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupapatti  nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajāti nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ajaraṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| abyādhi nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| amataṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| asaukau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| aparidaivau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| anupāyāsau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

uppādau saṅkhārā, anuppādau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| pavattaṃ saṅkhārā, appavattaṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nimittaṃ saṅkhārā, animittaṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| āyūhanā saṅkhārā, anāyūhanā nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paṭisandhi saṅkhārā, appaṭisandhi nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| gati saṅkhārā, agati nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| nibbatti saṅkhārā, anibbatti nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upapatti saṅkhārā, anupapatti nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jāti saṅkhārā, ajāti nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| jarā saṅkhārā, ajarā nibbānanti  abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| byādhi saṅkhārā, abyādhi nibbāna’’nti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| maraṇaṃ saṅkhārā, amataṃ nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| saukau saṅkhārā, asaukau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| paridaivau saṅkhārā, aparidaivau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| upāyāsau saṅkhārā, anupāyāsau nibbānanti abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

11. pariggahaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; parivāraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paripūraṭṭhau paripūraṇaṭṭhau (ka॰), paripūriṭṭhau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikaggaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; avisāraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anāvilaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aniñ‍janaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattupaṭṭhānavasaina cittassa ṭhitaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ārammaṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; gaucaraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pahānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paric‍cāgaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vuṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vivaṭṭanaṭṭhau nivattanaṭṭhau (ka॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; santaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paṇītaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vimuttaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anāsavaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; taraṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; animittaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; appaṇihitaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; suñ‍ñataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikarasaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anativattanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; yuganaddhaṭṭhau yuganandhanaṭṭhau (ka॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niyyānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; haituṭṭhau haitaṭṭhau (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

12. samathassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vipassanāya anupassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samathavipassanānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; yuganaddhassa anativattanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sikkhāya samādānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ārammaṇassa gaucaraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; līnassa cittassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; uddhatassa cittassa niggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ubhauvisuddhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; visaisādhigamaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; uttari paṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sac‍cābhisamayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhai patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

saddhindriyassa  adhimaukkhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyindriyassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; satindriyassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samādhindriyassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pañ‍ñindriyassa dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

saddhābalassa assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyabalassa kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; satibalassa pamādai akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samādhibalassa uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pañ‍ñābalassa avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

satisambaujjhaṅgassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa pavicayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pītisambaujjhaṅgassa pharaṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa upasamaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

sammādiṭṭhiyā  dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāsaṅkappassa abhiniraupanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāvācāya pariggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammākammantassa samuṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāājīvassa  vaudānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāvāyāmassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāsatiyā upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammāsamādhissa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

13. indriyānaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; balānaṃ akampiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; baujjhaṅgānaṃ niyyānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maggassa haituṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; satipaṭṭhānānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sammappadhānānaṃ padahanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; iddhipādānaṃ ijjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sac‍cānaṃ tathaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; payaugānaṃ maggānaṃ (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā passitabbā paṭippassaddhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phalānaṃ sacchikiriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

vitak‍kassa abhiniraupanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vicārassa upavicāraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pītiyā pharaṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sukhassa abhisandanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| cittassa aikaggaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| āvaj‍janaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vijānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pajānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sañ‍jānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikaudaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| abhiñ‍ñāya ñātaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pariñ‍ñāya tīraṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pahānassa paric‍cāgaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; bhāvanāya aikarasaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sacchikiriyāya phassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; khandhānaṃ khandhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhātūnaṃ dhātuṭṭhau dhātaṭṭhau (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; āyatanānaṃ āyatanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; saṅkhatānaṃ saṅkhataṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; asaṅkhatassa asaṅkhataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

14. cittaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittānantariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa vuṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa vivaṭṭanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa haituṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa vatthuṭṭhau vatthaṭṭhau (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa bhūmaṭṭhau bhummaṭṭhau (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) (aikattai upanibandhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau) (ka॰) aṭṭhakathā passitabbā abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa ārammaṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa gaucaraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa cariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa gataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa abhinīhāraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa niyyānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

15. aikattai āvaj‍janaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai vijānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai pajānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai sañ‍jānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai aikadaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai upanibandhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai pakkhandanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai pasīdanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai santiṭṭhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai vimuc‍canaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti passanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai yānīkataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai vatthukataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai anuṭṭhitaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paricitaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai susamāraddhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai pariggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai parivāraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paripūraṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai samaudhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai āsaivanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai bhāvanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai bahulīkammaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai susamuggataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai suvimuttaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai bujjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai anubujjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paṭibujjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai sambujjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai baudhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai anubaudhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paṭibaudhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai sambaudhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai baudhipakkhiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai anubaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paṭibaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai sambaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai jautanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai uj‍jautanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai anujautanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai paṭijautanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; aikattai sañ‍jautanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

16. patāpanaṭṭhau pakāsanaṭṭhau (ka॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; viraucanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; kilaisānaṃ santāpanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; amalaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vimalaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; nimmalaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vivaikaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vivaikacariyaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; virāgaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; virāgacariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhacariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vausaggaṭṭhau vaussaggaṭṭhau (syā॰ ka॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vausaggacariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vimuttaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vimutticariyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

chandaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa mūlaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa pādaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa padhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa ijjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; chandassa dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

vīriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa mūlaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa pādaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa padhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa ijjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa  adhimaukkhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīriyassa dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

cittaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa mūlaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa pādaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa padhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa ijjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; cittassa dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

vīmaṃsaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya mūlaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya pādaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya padhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya ijjhanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya adhimaukkhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya paggahaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya avikkhaipaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vīmaṃsāya dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

17. dukkhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa saṅkhataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa santāpaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dukkhassa vipariṇāmaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| samudayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau āyuhanaṭṭhau (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samudayassa nidānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samudayassa sañ‍ñaugaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; samudayassa palibaudhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhassa vivaikaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhassa asaṅkhataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; niraudhassa amataṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau| maggaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maggassa haituṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maggassa dassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; maggassa ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

tathaṭṭhau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anattaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sac‍caṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paṭivaidhaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; abhijānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; parijānanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhātuṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ñātaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sacchikiriyaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phassanaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; abhisamayaṭṭhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

18. naikkhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; abyāpādau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; ālaukasañ‍ñā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; avikkhaipau  abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammavavatthānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pāmauj‍jaṃ pāmuj‍jaṃ (syā॰) abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

anic‍cānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dukkhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anattānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; nibbidānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; virāgānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; niraudhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; paṭinissaggānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; khayānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; vayānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; vipariṇāmānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; animittānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; appaṇihitānupassanā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; suñ‍ñatānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; ādīnavānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; paṭisaṅkhānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; vivaṭṭanānupassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

19. sautāpattimaggau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sautāpattiphalasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; sakadāgāmimaggau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sakadāgāmiphalasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anāgāmimaggau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anāgāmiphalasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; arahattamaggau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; arahattaphalasamāpatti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhaina saddhābalaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhaina vīriyabalaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; pamādai akampiyaṭṭhaina satibalaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhaina samādhibalaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñābalaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pavicayaṭṭhaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pharaṇaṭṭhaina pītisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; upasamaṭṭhaina passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhaina upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

dassanaṭṭhaina  sammādiṭṭhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; akampiyaṭṭhaina balā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; niyyānaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; haituṭṭhaina maggau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satipaṭṭhānā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; padahanaṭṭhaina sammappadhānā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhipādā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; tathaṭṭhaina sac‍cā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samathau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; aikarasaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; muttaṭṭhaina vimaukkhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina vij‍jā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vimutti abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; samucchaidaṭṭhaina khayai ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; paṭippassaddhaṭṭhaina anuppādai ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

20. chandau mūlaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; manasikārau samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; phassau samaudhānaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; vaidanā samausaraṇaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; samādhi pamukhaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyau; sati ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; pañ‍ñā tatuttaraṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; vimutti sāraṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyā; amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

yai yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñātā haunti tai tai dhammā ñātā haunti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇa’’nti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

21. kathaṃ  ‘‘imai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

aikau  dhammau pariñ‍ñaiyyau – phassau sāsavau upādāniyau| dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca| tayau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – tissau vaidanā| cattārau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – cattārau āhārā| pañ‍ca dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| cha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni| satta dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| aṭṭha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā  – aṭṭha laukadhammā| nava dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – nava sattāvāsā| dasa dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā – dasāyatanāni|

‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; rūpā pariñ‍ñaiyyā; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; cakkhusamphassau pariñ‍ñaiyyau; yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ  vā, tampi pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| sautaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; saddā pariñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… ghānaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, gandhā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… jivhā pariñ‍ñaiyyā; rasā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… kāyau pariñ‍ñaiyyau; phauṭṭhabbā pariñ‍ñaiyyā…pai॰… manau pariñ‍ñaiyyau; dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ; manausamphassau pariñ‍ñaiyyau; yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|’’ rūpaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… vaidanā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… sañ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… saṅkhārā pariñ‍ñaiyyā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ… cakkhu pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ… amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| yaisaṃ yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa tai tai dhammā paṭiladdhā haunti, aivaṃ tai dhammā pariñ‍ñātā caiva haunti tīritā ca|

22. naikkhammaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa naikkhammaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ  hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| abyāpādaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa abyāpādau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa ālaukasañ‍ñā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| avikkhaipaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa avikkhaipau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau  pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| dhammavavatthānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa dhammavavatthānaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| ñāṇaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa ñāṇaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| pāmauj‍jaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa pāmauj‍jaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca|

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ  paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa catutthaṃ jhānaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ  paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca|

anic‍cānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa anic‍cānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| dukkhānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa dukkhānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| anattānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa anattānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| nibbidānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa nibbidānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| virāgānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa virāgānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| niraudhānupassanaṃ  paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa niraudhānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva  hauti tīritau ca| paṭinissaggānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa paṭinissaggānupassanā  paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| khayānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa khayānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| vayānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa vayānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| vipariṇāmānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa vipariṇāmānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| animittānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa animittānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| appaṇihitānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa appaṇihitānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| suñ‍ñatānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa suñ‍ñatānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca|

adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ paṭiladdhaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau  caiva hauti tīritau ca| ādīnavānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa ādīnavānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| paṭisaṅkhānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa paṭisaṅkhānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| vivaṭṭanānupassanaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa vivaṭṭanānupassanā paṭiladdhā hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca|

sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa sautāpattimaggau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| sakadāgāmimaggaṃ  paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa sakadāgāmimaggau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| anāgāmimaggaṃ  paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa anāgāmimaggau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca| arahattamaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa arahattamaggau paṭiladdhau hauti| aivaṃ sau dhammau pariñ‍ñātau caiva hauti tīritau ca|

yaisaṃ yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vāyamantassa tai tai dhammā paṭiladdhā haunti| aivaṃ tai dhammā pariñ‍ñātā caiva haunti tīritā ca| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇa’’nti|

23. kathaṃ  ‘‘imai dhammā pahātabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

aikau dhammau pahātabbau – asmimānau| dvai dhammā pahātabbā – avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca| tayau dhammā pahātabbā – tissau taṇhā| cattārau dhammā pahātabbā – cattārau aughā| pañ‍ca dhammā pahātabbā – pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni| cha dhammā pahātabbā – cha  taṇhākāyā| satta dhammā pahātabbā – sattānusayā| aṭṭha dhammā pahātabbā – aṭṭha micchattā| nava dhammā pahātabbā – nava taṇhāmūlakā| dasa dhammā pahātabbā – dasa micchattā|

24. dvai pahānāni – samucchaidappahānaṃ, paṭippassaddhippahānaṃ| samucchaidappahānañ‍ca laukuttaraṃ khayagāmimaggaṃ bhāvayatau; paṭippassaddhippahānañ‍ca phalakkhaṇai| tīṇi pahānāni – kāmānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ; rūpānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ āruppaṃ; yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ, niraudhau tassa nissaraṇaṃ| naikkhammaṃ paṭiladdhassa kāmā pahīnā caiva haunti paric‍cattā ca| āruppaṃ paṭiladdhassa rūpā pahīnā caiva haunti paric‍cattā ca| niraudhaṃ paṭiladdhassa saṅkhārā pahīnā caiva haunti paric‍cattā ca| cattāri pahānāni dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau pajahati; samudayasac‍caṃ  pahānapaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau pajahati; niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau pajahati; maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau  pajahati| pañ‍ca pahānāni – vikkhambhanappahānaṃ, tadaṅgappahānaṃ, samucchaidappahānaṃ, paṭippassaddhippahānaṃ, nissaraṇappahānaṃ| vikkhambhanappahānañ‍ca nīvaraṇānaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvayatau; tadaṅgappahānañ‍ca diṭṭhigatānaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ  samādhiṃ bhāvayatau; samucchaidappahānañ‍ca laukuttaraṃ khayagāmimaggaṃ bhāvayatau; paṭippassaddhippahānañ‍ca phalakkhaṇai; nissaraṇappahānañ‍ca niraudhau nibbānaṃ|

‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pahātabbaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ pahātabbaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, pahātabbaṃ; rūpā pahātabbā; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahātabbaṃ; cakkhusamphassau pahātabbau; yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi pahātabbaṃ| sautaṃ pahātabbaṃ; saddā pahātabbā…pai॰… ghānaṃ pahātabbaṃ; gandhā pahātabbā… jivhā pahātabbā; rasā pahātabbā… kāyau pahātabbau; phauṭṭhabbā pahātabbā… manau pahātabbau; dhammā pahātabbā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahātabbaṃ;… manausamphassau pahātabbau; yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi pahātabbaṃ|’’ rūpaṃ passantau pajahati, vaidanaṃ passantau pajahati, sañ‍ñaṃ passantau pajahati, saṅkhārai passantau pajahati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ passantau pajahati| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ…pai॰… amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina  passantau pajahati| yai yai dhammā pahīnā haunti tai tai dhammā paric‍cattā haunti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina  vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā pahātabbāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃ pajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇa’’nti|

tatiyabhāṇavārau|

25. kathaṃ  ‘‘imai dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

aikau dhammau bhāvaitabbau – kāyagatāsati sātasahagatā| dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā – samathau ca vipassanā ca| tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā – tayau samādhī| cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā – pañ‍caṅgikau samādhi sammāsamādhī (syā॰)| cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā – cha anussatiṭṭhānāni| satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā – satta baujjhaṅgā| aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā – ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā – nava pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni| dasa dhammā bhāvaitabbā – dasa kasiṇāyatanāni|

26. dvai  bhāvanā – laukiyā ca bhāvanā, laukuttarā ca bhāvanā| tissau bhāvanā – rūpāvacarakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā, arūpāvacarakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā, apariyāpan‍nakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā| rūpāvacarakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā atthi hīnā, atthi majjhimā, atthi paṇītā| arūpāvacarakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā atthi hīnā, atthi majjhimā, atthi paṇītā| apariyāpan‍nakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanā paṇītā|

27. catassau bhāvanā – dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau bhāvaiti, samudayasac‍caṃ pahānappaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau bhāvaiti, niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau bhāvaiti, maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhantau bhāvaiti| imā catassau bhāvanā|

aparāpi catassau bhāvanā – aisanābhāvanā, paṭilābhābhāvanā, aikarasābhāvanā, āsaivanābhāvanā| katamā aisanābhāvanā? sabbaisaṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jantānaṃ tattha jātā dhammā aikarasā hauntīti – ayaṃ aisanābhāvanā| katamā paṭilābhābhāvanā? sabbaisaṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nānaṃ tattha jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – ayaṃ paṭilābhābhāvanā| katamā aikarasābhāvanā? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ bhāvayatau saddhindriyassa vasaina cattāri indriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvayatau  vīriyindriyassa vasaina cattāri indriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ bhāvayatau satindriyassa vasaina cattāri indriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ bhāvayatau samādhindriyassa vasaina cattāri indriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvayatau pañ‍ñindriyassa  vasaina cattāri indriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhaina saddhābalaṃ bhāvayatau saddhābalassa vasaina cattāri balāni aikarasā hauntīti – balānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhaina vīriyabalaṃ bhāvayatau vīriyabalassa vasaina cattāri  balāni aikarasā hauntīti – balānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pamādai akampiyaṭṭhaina satibalaṃ bhāvayatau satibalassa vasaina cattāri balāni aikarasā hauntīti – balānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhaina samādhibalaṃ bhāvayatau samādhibalassa vasaina cattāri balāni aikarasā hauntīti – balānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvayatau pañ‍ñābalassa vasaina cattāri balāni aikarasā hauntīti – balānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau satisambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pavicayaṭṭhaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pharaṇaṭṭhaina pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| upasamaṭṭhaina  passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ  aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhaina upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa vasaina cha baujjhaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – baujjhaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvayatau sammādiṭṭhiyā vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvayatau sammāsaṅkappassa vasaina satta  maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācaṃ bhāvayatau sammāvācāya vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantaṃ bhāvayatau sammākammantassa vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvaṃ bhāvayatau sammāājīvassa vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvayatau sammāvāyāmassa vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsatiṃ bhāvayatau sammāsatiyā vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ  aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvayatau sammāsamādhissa vasaina satta maggaṅgā aikarasā hauntīti – maggaṅgānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ayaṃ aikarasābhāvanā|

katamā āsaivanābhāvanā? idha bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ āsaivati, majjhanhikasamayampi majjhantikasamayampi (syā॰ ka॰) āsaivati, sāyanhasamayampi āsaivati, puraibhattampi āsaivati, pacchābhattampi āsaivati, purimaipi yāmai āsaivati, majjhimaipi yāmai āsaivati, pacchimaipi yāmai āsaivati, rattimpi āsaivati, divāpi āsaivati, rattindivāpi rattidivāpi (ka॰) āsaivati, kāḷaipi āsaivati, juṇhaipi āsaivati, vassaipi āsaivati, haimantaipi āsaivati, gimhaipi āsaivati, purimaipi vayaukhandhai āsaivati, majjhimaipi vayaukhandhai āsaivati, pacchimaipi vayaukhandhai āsaivati – ayaṃ āsaivanābhāvanā| imā catassau bhāvanā|

28. aparāpi catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ  anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| kathaṃ tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| byāpādaṃ pajahatau abyāpādavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| thinamiddhaṃ  thīnamiddhaṃ (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) pajahatau ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau avikkhaipavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| vicikicchaṃ pajahatau dhammavavatthānavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| avij‍jaṃ pajahatau ñāṇavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aratiṃ pajahatau pāmauj‍javasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti  – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| nīvaraṇai pajahatau paṭhamajjhānavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| vitak‍kavicārai pajahatau dutiyajjhānavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pītiṃ pajahatau tatiyajjhānavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sukhadukkhai pajahatau catutthajjhānavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

rūpasañ‍ñaṃ paṭighasañ‍ñaṃ nānattasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau anic‍cānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau dukkhānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau anattānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| nandiṃ pajahatau nibbidānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| rāgaṃ pajahatau virāgānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina  bhāvanā| samudayaṃ pajahatau niraudhānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ādānaṃ pajahatau paṭinissaggānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ghanasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau khayānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| āyūhanaṃ pajahatau vayānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti  – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dhuvasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau vipariṇāmānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| nimittaṃ pajahatau animittānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ  anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paṇidhiṃ pajahatau appaṇihitānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| abhinivaisaṃ pajahatau suñ‍ñatānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sārādānābhinivaisaṃ pajahatau adhipañ‍ñā dhammavipassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sammauhābhinivaisaṃ pajahatau yathābhūtañāṇadassanavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| ālayābhinivaisaṃ pajahatau ādīnavānupassanāvasaina  jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| appaṭisaṅkhaṃ pajahatau paṭisaṅkhānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sañ‍ñaugābhinivaisaṃ pajahatau vivaṭṭanānupassanāvasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

diṭṭhaikaṭṭhai kilaisai pajahatau sautāpattimaggavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| auḷārikai kilaisai pajahatau sakadāgāmimaggavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| anusahagatai kilaisai pajahatau anāgāmimaggavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sabbakilaisai pajahatau arahattamaggavasaina jātā dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti  – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

kathaṃ indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| byāpādaṃ pajahatau abyāpādavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰…  sabbakilaisai  pajahatau arahattamaggavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni aikarasā hauntīti – indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

kathaṃ  tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina vīriyaṃ vāhaitīti – tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| byāpādaṃ pajahatau abyāpādavasaina vīriyaṃ vāhaitīti – tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahatau arahattamaggavasaina vīriyaṃ vāhaitīti – tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

kathaṃ āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammaṃ āsaivatīti – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| byāpādaṃ pajahantau abyāpādaṃ āsaivatīti – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggaṃ āsaivatīti – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

imā catassau bhāvanā rūpaṃ passantau bhāvaiti, vaidanaṃ passantau bhāvaiti, sañ‍ñaṃ passantau bhāvaiti, saṅkhārai passantau bhāvaiti, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ passantau bhāvaiti, cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ…pai॰… amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina passantau bhāvaiti| yai yai dhammā bhāvitā haunti tai tai dhammā aikarasā haunti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā bhāvaitabbāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā  sutamayai ñāṇa’’nti|

catutthabhāṇavārau|

29. kathaṃ  ‘‘imai dhammā sacchikātabbā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

aikau dhammau sacchikātabbau – akuppā caitauvimutti| dvai dhammā sacchikātabbā – vij‍jā ca vimutti ca| tayau dhammā sacchikātabbā – tissau vij‍jā| cattārau dhammā sacchikātabbā – cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni| pañ‍ca dhammā sacchikātabbā – pañ‍ca dhammakkhandhā| cha dhammā sacchikātabbā – cha  abhiñ‍ñā| satta dhammā sacchikātabbā – satta khīṇāsavabalāni| aṭṭha dhammā sacchikātabbā – aṭṭha vimaukkhā| nava dhammā sacchikātabbā – nava anupubbaniraudhā| dasa dhammā sacchikātabbā – dasa asaikkhā dhammā|

‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbaṃ; rūpā sacchikātabbā; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ; cakkhusamphassau sacchikātabbau; yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi sacchikātabbaṃ| sautaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ; saddā sacchikātabbā…pai॰… ghānaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ; gandhā sacchikātabbā… jivhā sacchikātabbā; rasā sacchikātabbā… kāyau sacchikātabbau; phauṭṭhabbā sacchikātabbā… manau sacchikātabbau; dhammā sacchikātabbā; manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ; manausamphassau sacchikātabbau; yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi sacchikātabbaṃ’’| rūpaṃ passantau sacchikarauti, vaidanaṃ passantau sacchikarauti, sañ‍ñaṃ passantau sacchikarauti, saṅkhārai passantau sacchikarauti, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ passantau sacchikarauti| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ…pai॰… amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina passantau sacchikarauti| yai yai dhammā sacchikatā haunti tai tai dhammā phassitā phusitā (syā॰) haunti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā sacchikātabbāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇa’’nti|

30. kathaṃ ‘‘imai dhammā hānabhāgiyā, imai dhammā ṭhitibhāgiyā, imai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā, imai dhammā nibbaidhabhāgiyā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

paṭhamassa  jhānassa lābhiṃ kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| avitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti  virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

dutiyassa jhānassa lābhiṃ vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| upaikkhāsukhasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau  dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

tatiyassa jhānassa lābhiṃ pītisukhasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| adukkhamasukhasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

catutthassa  jhānassa lābhiṃ upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa lābhiṃ rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa lābhiṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā  samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau dhammau|

ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa  lābhiṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – hānabhāgiyau dhammau| tadanudhammatā sati santiṭṭhati – ṭhitibhāgiyau dhammau| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti – visaisabhāgiyau dhammau| nibbidāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti virāgūpasaṃhitā – nibbaidhabhāgiyau  dhammau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘imai dhammā hānabhāgiyā, imai dhammā ṭhitibhāgiyā, imai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā, imai dhammā nibbaidhabhāgiyāti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ’’|

31. kathaṃ ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā, sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ? ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhaina, dukkhaṃ bhayaṭṭhaina, anattā asārakaṭṭhainā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhu…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhaina, dukkhaṃ bhayaṭṭhaina, anattā asārakaṭṭhainā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā, sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

32. kathaṃ  ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’’nti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ?

33. tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ; saṅkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi (ka॰) satipaṭṭhānasuttai sac‍cavibhaṅgai ca passitabbaṃ dukkhā|

tattha katamā jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati jāti|

tattha  katamā jarā yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati jarā|

tattha  katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā kālaṃkiriyā (ka॰) khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau jīvitindriyassupacchaidau – idaṃ vuc‍cati maraṇaṃ|

tattha katamau saukau? ñātibyasanaina yau ñātibyasanaina (syā॰) passa sac‍cavibhaṅgai vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina  saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa saukau saucanā saucitattaṃ antausaukau antauparisaukau caitasau parijjhāyanā daumanassaṃ saukasal‍laṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati saukau|

tattha katamau paridaivau? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa ādaivau paridaivau ādaivanā, paridaivanā ādaivitattaṃ paridaivitattaṃ vācā palāpau vippalāpau lālappau lālappanā lālappitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati paridaivau|

tattha  katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ, kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ, caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati daumanassaṃ|

tattha katamau upāyāsau? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina  dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa āyāsau  upāyāsau āyāsanā upāyāsanā āyāsitattaṃ upāyāsitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati upāyāsau|

tattha katamau appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau? idha yassa  tai haunti aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phauṭṭhabbā, yai vā panassa tai haunti anatthakāmā ahitakāmā aphāsukāmā ayaugakkhaimakāmā, yā taihi saddhiṃ saṅgati samāgamau samaudhānaṃ missībhāvau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau|

tattha katamau piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau? idha yassa tai haunti iṭṭhā kantā manāpā rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phauṭṭhabbā, yai vā panassa tai haunti atthakāmā hitakāmā phāsukāmā yaugakkhaimakāmā mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā bhaginī vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā, yā taihi saddhiṃ asaṅgati asamāgamau asamaudhānaṃ amissībhāvau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau|

tattha katamaṃ yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ? jātidhammānaṃ sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘‘ahau vata mayaṃ na jātidhammā assāma, na ca vata nau jāti āgacchaiyyā’’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ – idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| jarādhammānaṃ sattānaṃ…pai॰… byādhidhammānaṃ sattānaṃ… maraṇadhammānaṃ sattānaṃ… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammānaṃ sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati  – ‘‘ahau vata mayaṃ na saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā assāma, na ca vata nau saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā āgacchaiyyu’’nti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ – idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ|

tattha  katamai saṅkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā? saiyyathidaṃ saiyyathīdaṃ (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) – rūpupādānakkhandhau, rūpūpādānakkhandhau (syā॰) aivamuparipi, vaidanupādānakkhandhau sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti saṅkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

34. tattha  katamaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā paunaubbhavikā (syā॰) nandirāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā, sā khau panaisā taṇhā kattha uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, kattha nivisamānā nivisati? yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| kiñ‍ca laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ? cakkhu laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| sautaṃ laukai…pai॰… ghānaṃ laukai… jivhā laukai… kāyau laukai… manau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| rūpā laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā  uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| saddā laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ…pai॰… dhammā laukai… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ laukai…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ laukai… cakkhusamphassau laukai…pai॰… manausamphassau laukai… cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā laukai…pai॰… manausamphassajā vaidanā laukai… rūpasañ‍ñā laukai…pai॰… dhammasañ‍ñā laukai… rūpasañ‍caitanā laukai…pai॰… dhammasañ‍caitanā laukai… rūpataṇhā laukai…pai॰… dhammataṇhā laukai… rūpavitak‍kau laukai…pai॰… dhammavitak‍kau laukai… rūpavicārau laukai…pai॰… dhammavicārau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

35. tattha katamaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau, sā khau panaisā taṇhā kattha pahīyamānā pahīyati, kattha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati? yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā pahīyamānā pahīyati, aittha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati| kiñ‍ca laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ? cakkhulaukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā pahīyamānā pahīyati, aittha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati…pai॰… dhammavicārau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā pahīyamānā pahīyati, aittha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati| idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

36. tattha  katamaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau  aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau  sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammādiṭṭhi|

tattha katamau sammāsaṅkappau? naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāsaṅkappau|

tattha katamā sammāvācā? musāvādā vairamaṇī vairamaṇi (ka॰), pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāvācā|

tattha katamau sammākammantau? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammākammantau|

tattha katamau sammāājīvau? idha ariyasāvakau micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvaina jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāājīvau|

tattha katamau sammāvāyāmau? idha bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya  …pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāvāyāmau|

tattha katamā sammāsati? idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāsati|

tattha katamau sammāsamādhi? idha bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi  savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ  pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina  pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’’nti sautāvadhānaṃ, taṃpajānanā pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ sautāvadhānai pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ|

sutamayañāṇaniddaisau paṭhamau|

2. sīlamayañāṇaniddaisau

37. kathaṃ sutvāna saṃvarai pañ‍ñā sīlamayai ñāṇaṃ? pañ‍ca sīlāni – pariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ, apariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ, paripuṇṇapārisuddhisīlaṃ, aparāmaṭṭhapārisuddhisīlaṃ, paṭippassaddhipārisuddhisīlanti|

tattha katamaṃ pariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ? anupasampan‍nānaṃ pariyantasikkhāpadānaṃ – idaṃ pariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ| katamaṃ apariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ? upasampan‍nānaṃ apariyantasikkhāpadānaṃ – idaṃ apariyantapārisuddhisīlaṃ| katamaṃ paripuṇṇapārisuddhisīlaṃ? puthuj‍janakalyāṇakānaṃ kusaladhammai yuttānaṃ saikkhapariyantai saikhapariyantai (ka॰) paripūrakārīnaṃ kāyai ca jīvitai ca anapaikkhānaṃ paric‍cattajīvitānaṃ – idaṃ paripuṇṇapārisuddhisīlaṃ| katamaṃ aparāmaṭṭhapārisuddhisīlaṃ? sattan‍naṃ saikkhānaṃ – idaṃ aparāmaṭṭhapārisuddhisīlaṃ| katamaṃ  paṭippassaddhipārisuddhisīlaṃ? tathāgatasāvakānaṃ khīṇāsavānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ – idaṃ paṭippassaddhipārisuddhisīlaṃ|

38. atthi  sīlaṃ pariyantaṃ, atthi sīlaṃ apariyantaṃ| tattha katamaṃ taṃ sīlaṃ pariyantaṃ? atthi sīlaṃ lābhapariyantaṃ, atthi sīlaṃ yasapariyantaṃ, atthi sīlaṃ ñātipariyantaṃ, atthi sīlaṃ aṅgapariyantaṃ, atthi sīlaṃ jīvitapariyantaṃ|
