kathaṃ  kāmāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, akusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti? dasa kusalakammapathai kusalatau vavatthaiti, dasa akusalakammapathai akusalatau vavatthaiti, rūpañ‍ca vipākañ‍ca kiriyañ‍ca abyākatatau vavatthaiti – aivaṃ kāmāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, akusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti|

kathaṃ rūpāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti? idhaṭṭhassa cattāri jhānāni kusalatau vavatthaiti, tatrūpapan‍nassa cattāri jhānāni abyākatatau vavatthaiti – aivaṃ rūpāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti|

kathaṃ arūpāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti? idhaṭṭhassa catassau arūpāvacarasamāpattiyau kusalatau vavatthaiti, tatrūpapan‍nassa catassau arūpāvacarasamāpattiyau abyākatatau vavatthaiti – aivaṃ arūpāvacarai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti|

kathaṃ  apariyāpan‍nai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti? cattārau ariyamaggai kusalatau vavatthaiti, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni nibbānañ‍ca abyākatatau vavatthaiti – aivaṃ apariyāpan‍nai dhammai kusalatau vavatthaiti, abyākatatau vavatthaiti| aivaṃ dhammai vavatthaiti|

nava  pāmauj‍jamūlakā dhammā| anic‍catau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati| yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ nibbindati, nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| dukkhatau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… anattatau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… vimuc‍cati|

rūpaṃ anic‍catau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… rūpaṃ dukkhatau manasikarautau…pai॰… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau  manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati| yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ nibbindati, nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| imai nava pāmauj‍jamūlakā dhammā|

74. nava yaunisau manasikāramūlakā dhammā| anic‍catau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitaina cittaina ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| dukkhatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitaina cittaina ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ  dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| anattatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰…|

rūpaṃ anic‍catau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… rūpaṃ dukkhatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… rūpaṃ anattatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau yaunisau manasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ  jāyati, pamuditassa pīti  jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitaina cittaina ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| imai nava yaunisau manasikāramūlakā dhammā|

nava nānattā – dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati lābhanānattaṃ – imai nava nānattā| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘navadhammavavatthānai pañ‍ñā dhammanānattai ñāṇaṃ’’|

dhammanānattañāṇaniddaisau aikūnavīsatimau|

20-24. ñāṇapañ‍cakaniddaisau

75. kathaṃ abhiñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā ñātaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā tīraṇaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, pahānaipañ‍ñā paric‍cāgaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, bhāvanā pañ‍ñā aikarasaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñā phassanaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ? yai yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñātā haunti, tai tai dhammā ñātā  haunti| yai yai dhammā pariñ‍ñātā haunti, tai  tai dhammā tīritā haunti| yai yai dhammā pahīnā haunti, tai tai dhammā paric‍cattā haunti| yai yai dhammā bhāvitā haunti, tai tai dhammā aikarasā haunti| yai yai dhammā sacchikatā haunti, tai tai dhammā phassitā haunti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘abhiñ‍ñā pañ‍ñā ñātaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, pariñ‍ñā pañ‍ñā tīraṇaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, pahānai pañ‍ñā paric‍cāgaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, bhāvanā pañ‍ñā aikarasaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, sacchikiriyā pañ‍ñā phusanaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

ñāṇapañ‍cakaniddaisau catuvīsatimau|

25-28. paṭisambhidāñāṇaniddaisau

76. kathaṃ  atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ? saddhindriyaṃ dhammau, vīriyindriyaṃ dhammau, satindriyaṃ dhammau, samādhindriyaṃ dhammau, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dhammau| añ‍ñau saddhindriyaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau vīriyindriyaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau satindriyaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau samādhindriyaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dhammau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai  ñāṇaṃ’’|

adhimaukkhaṭṭhau atthau, paggahaṭṭhau atthau, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau, dassanaṭṭhau atthau| añ‍ñau adhimaukkhaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau paggahaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau dassanaṭṭhau atthau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

pañ‍ca dhammai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā, pañ‍ca atthai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā| añ‍ñā dhammaniruttiyau, añ‍ñā atthaniruttiyau| yaina ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

pañ‍casu  dhammaisu ñāṇāni, pañ‍casu atthaisu ñāṇāni, dasasu niruttīsu ñāṇāni| añ‍ñāni dhammaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni atthaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

77. saddhābalaṃ  dhammau, vīriyabalaṃ dhammau, satibalaṃ dhammau, samādhibalaṃ dhammau, pañ‍ñābalaṃ dhammau| añ‍ñau saddhābalaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau vīriyabalaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau satibalaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau samādhibalaṃ dhammau, añ‍ñau pañ‍ñābalaṃ dhammau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā  dhammā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina  imai nānā dhammā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| pamādai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| añ‍ñau assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau pamādai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhau atthau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

pañ‍ca dhammai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā, pañ‍ca atthai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā| añ‍ñā dhammaniruttiyau, añ‍ñā atthaniruttiyau| yaina ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau paṭividitāti| taina  vuc‍cati – ‘‘niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

pañ‍casu pañ‍ca (?) dhammaisu ñāṇāni, pañ‍casu pañ‍ca (?) atthaisu ñāṇāni, dasasu dasa (?) niruttīsu ñāṇāni| añ‍ñāni dhammaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni atthaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

satisambaujjhaṅgau  dhammau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau dhammau| añ‍ñau satisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau pītisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau dhammau, añ‍ñau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau dhammau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau  atthau, pavicayaṭṭhau atthau, paggahaṭṭhau atthau, pharaṇaṭṭhau atthau, upasamaṭṭhau atthau, avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau, paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhau atthau| añ‍ñau upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau  pavicayaṭṭhau  atthau, añ‍ñau paggahaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau pharaṇaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau upasamaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhau atthau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

satta dhammai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā, satta atthai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā| añ‍ñā dhammaniruttiyau, añ‍ñā atthaniruttiyau| yaina ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

sattasu dhammaisu ñāṇāni, sattasu atthaisu ñāṇāni, cuddasasu niruttīsu ñāṇāni| añ‍ñāni dhammaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni atthaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

sammādiṭṭhi dhammau, sammāsaṅkappau dhammau, sammāvācā dhammau, sammākammantau dhammau, sammāājīvau dhammau, sammāvāyāmau dhammau, sammāsati dhammau, sammāsamādhi dhammau| añ‍ñau  sammādiṭṭhi dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāsaṅkappau  dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāvācā dhammau, añ‍ñau sammākammantau dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāājīvau dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāvāyāmau dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāsati dhammau, añ‍ñau sammāsamādhi dhammau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā dhammā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

dassanaṭṭhau atthau, abhiniraupanaṭṭhau atthau, pariggahaṭṭhau atthau, samuṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, vaudānaṭṭhau atthau, paggahaṭṭhau atthau, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau| añ‍ñau dassanaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau abhiniraupanaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau pariggahaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau samuṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau vaudānaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau paggahaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau  upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau atthau, añ‍ñau avikkhaipaṭṭhau atthau| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā ñātā tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā atthā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

aṭṭha  dhammai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā, aṭṭha atthai sandassaituṃ byañ‍jananiruttābhilāpā| añ‍ñā dhammaniruttiyau, añ‍ñā atthaniruttiyau| yaina ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imā nānā niruttiyau paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

aṭṭhasu dhammaisu ñāṇāni, aṭṭhasu atthaisu ñāṇāni sauḷasasu niruttīsu ñāṇāni| añ‍ñāni dhammaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni atthaisu ñāṇāni, añ‍ñāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni| yaina ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā ñātā, tainaiva ñāṇaina imai nānā ñāṇā paṭividitāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

paṭisambhidāñāṇaniddaisau aṭṭhavīsatimau|

29-31. ñāṇattayaniddaisau

78. kathaṃ  vihāranānattai pañ‍ñā vihāraṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, samāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā samāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, vihārasamāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā vihārasamāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ? nimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – animittau vihārau| paṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – appaṇihitau vihārau| abhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā  phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – suñ‍ñatau vihārau|

nimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittā samāpatti| paṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā  pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitā samāpatti| abhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai  adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatā samāpatti|

nimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittavihārasamāpatti| paṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitavihārasamāpatti| abhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatavihārasamāpatti|

79. rūpanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – animittau vihārau| rūpapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau  sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – appaṇihitau vihārau| rūpābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – suñ‍ñatau vihārau|

rūpanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittā samāpatti| rūpapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitā samāpatti| rūpābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatā samāpatti|

rūpanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittavihārasamāpatti| rūpapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā phussa phussa  vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitavihārasamāpatti| rūpābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatavihārasamāpatti|

vaidanānimittaṃ  …pai॰… sañ‍ñānimittaṃ… saṅkhāranimittaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇanimittaṃ… cakkhunimittaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – animittau vihārau| jarāmaraṇapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – appaṇihitau vihārau| jarāmaraṇābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati – suñ‍ñatau vihārau|

jarāmaraṇanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittā samāpatti| jarāmaraṇapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitā samāpatti| jarāmaraṇābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatā samāpatti|

jarāmaraṇanimittaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau animittai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ animittaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – animittavihārasamāpatti| jarāmaraṇapaṇidhiṃ bhayatau  sampassamānau appaṇihitai adhimuttattā  phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – appaṇihitavihārasamāpatti| jarāmaraṇābhinivaisaṃ bhayatau sampassamānau suñ‍ñatai adhimuttattā phussa phussa vayaṃ passati, pavattaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ āvaj‍jitvā samāpaj‍jati – suñ‍ñatavihārasamāpatti| añ‍ñau  animittau vihārau, añ‍ñau appaṇihitau vihārau, añ‍ñau suñ‍ñatau vihārau| añ‍ñā animittasamāpatti, añ‍ñā appaṇihitasamāpatti, añ‍ñā suñ‍ñatasamāpatti| añ‍ñā animittā vihārasamāpatti, añ‍ñā appaṇihitā vihārasamāpatti, añ‍ñā suñ‍ñatā vihārasamāpatti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vihāranānattai pañ‍ñā vihāraṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, samāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā samāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ, vihārasamāpattinānattai pañ‍ñā vihārasamāpattaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

ñāṇattayaniddaisau aikatiṃsatimau|

32. ānantarikasamādhiñāṇaniddaisau

80. kathaṃ  avikkhaipaparisuddhattā āsavasamucchaidai pañ‍ñā ānantarikasamādhimhi ñāṇaṃ? naikkhammavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| tassa samādhissa vasaina uppaj‍jati ñāṇaṃ| taina ñāṇaina āsavā khīyanti| iti paṭhamaṃ samathau, pacchā ñāṇaṃ| taina ñāṇaina āsavānaṃ khayau hauti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘avikkhaipaparisuddhattā āsavasamucchaidai pañ‍ñā ānantarikasamādhimhi ñāṇaṃ’’|

āsavāti katamai tai āsavā? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, diṭṭhāsavau, avij‍jāsavau| katthaitai āsavā khīyanti? sautāpattimaggaina anavasaisau diṭṭhāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau kāmāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau bhavāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| anāgāmimaggaina anavasaisau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau  avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| arahattamaggaina anavasaisau bhavāsavau khīyati, anavasaisau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti|

abyāpādavasaina  …pai॰… ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina… avikkhaipavasaina… dhammavavatthānavasaina… ñāṇavasaina… pāmauj‍javasaina… paṭhamajjhānavasaina… dutiyajjhānavasaina… tatiyajjhānavasaina… catutthajjhānavasaina… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina… pathavīkasiṇavasaina… āpaukasiṇavasaina … taijaukasiṇavasaina… vāyaukasiṇavasaina… nīlakasiṇavasaina… pītakasiṇavasaina… lauhitakasiṇavasaina… audātakasiṇavasaina… ākāsakasiṇavasaina… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇavasaina… buddhānussativasaina… dhammānussativasaina… saṅghānussativasaina… sīlānussativasaina… cāgānussativasaina… daivatānussativasaina… ānāpānassativasaina… maraṇassativasaina… kāyagatāsativasaina… upasamānussativasaina… uddhumātakasañ‍ñāvasaina… vinīlakasañ‍ñāvasaina… vipubbakasañ‍ñāvasaina… vicchiddakasañ‍ñāvasaina… vikkhāyitakasañ‍ñāvasaina… vikkhittakasañ‍ñāvasaina… hatavikkhittakasañ‍ñāvasaina … lauhitakasañ‍ñāvasaina… puḷavakasañ‍ñāvasaina… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāvasaina|

81. dīghaṃ assāsavasaina…pai॰… dīghaṃ passāsavasaina… rassaṃ assāsavasaina… rassaṃ passāsavasaina… sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passāsavasaina… pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… pītipaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passāsavasaina… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assāsavasaina… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passāsavasaina… samādahaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… anic‍cānupassī … virāgānupassī… niraudhānupassī… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina… paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina  cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| tassa samādhissa vasaina uppaj‍jati ñāṇaṃ, taina ñāṇaina āsavā khīyanti| iti paṭhamaṃ samathau, pacchā ñāṇaṃ| taina ñāṇaina  āsavānaṃ khayau hauti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘avikkhaipaparisuddhattā āsavasamucchaidai pañ‍ñā ānantarikasamādhimhi ñāṇaṃ’’|

āsavāti katamai tai āsavā? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, diṭṭhāsavau, avij‍jāsavau| katthaitai āsavā khīyanti? sautāpattimaggaina anavasaisau diṭṭhāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau kāmāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau bhavāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| anāgāmimaggaina anavasaisau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| arahattamaggaina anavasaisau bhavāsavau khīyati, anavasaisau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘avikkhaipaparisuddhattā āsavasamucchaidai pañ‍ñā ānantarikasamādhimhi ñāṇaṃ’’|

ānantarikasamādhiñāṇaniddaisau dvattiṃsatimau|

33. araṇavihārañāṇaniddaisau

82. kathaṃ  dassanādhipataiyyaṃ santau ca vihārādhigamau paṇītādhimuttatā pañ‍ñā araṇavihārai ñāṇaṃ? dassanādhipataiyyanti anic‍cānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, dukkhānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, anattānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, rūpai anic‍cānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, rūpai dukkhānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, rūpai anattānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ; vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, jarāmaraṇai dukkhānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ, jarāmaraṇai anattānupassanā dassanādhipataiyyaṃ|

santau  ca vihārādhigamauti suñ‍ñatau vihārau santau vihārādhigamau| animittau vihārau santau vihārādhigamau| appaṇihitau vihārau santau vihārādhigamau|

paṇītādhimuttatāti suñ‍ñatai adhimuttatā paṇītādhimuttatā| animittai adhimuttatā paṇītādhimuttatā| appaṇihitai adhimuttatā paṇītādhimuttatā|

araṇavihārauti paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ araṇavihārau| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ araṇavihārau| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ araṇavihārau| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ araṇavihārau| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti  araṇavihārau…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti araṇavihārau|

araṇavihārauti kainaṭṭhaina araṇavihārau? paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇai haratīti – araṇavihārau| dutiyaina jhānaina vitak‍kavicārai haratīti – araṇavihārau| tatiyaina jhānaina pītiṃ haratīti – araṇavihārau| catutthaina jhānaina sukhadukkhai haratīti – araṇavihārau| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā rūpasañ‍ñaṃ paṭighasañ‍ñaṃ nānattasañ‍ñaṃ haratīti – araṇavihārau| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ haratīti – araṇavihārau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ haratīti – araṇavihārau| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ haratīti – araṇavihārau| ayaṃ araṇavihārau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati  – ‘‘dassanādhipataiyyaṃ santau ca vihārādhigamau paṇītādhimuttatā pañ‍ñā araṇavihārai ñāṇaṃ’’|

araṇavihārañāṇaniddaisau taittiṃsatimau|

34. niraudhasamāpattiñāṇaniddaisau

83. kathaṃ dvīhi balaihi saman‍nāgatattā tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi vasibhāvatā pañ‍ñā niraudhasamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ?

dvīhi  balaihīti dvai balāni – samathabalaṃ, vipassanābalaṃ| katamaṃ samathabalaṃ? naikkhammavasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau  samathabalaṃ| abyāpādavasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalaṃ| ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau  samathabalaṃ| avikkhaipavasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalaṃ…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalaṃ| paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina cittassaikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalaṃ|

samathabalanti kainaṭṭhaina samathabalaṃ? paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇai na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| dutiyaina jhānaina vitak‍kavicārai na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| tatiyaina jhānaina pītiyā na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| catutthaina jhānaina sukhadukkhai na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā rūpasañ‍ñāya paṭighasañ‍ñāya nānattasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – samathabalaṃ| uddhac‍cai ca uddhac‍casahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca na kampati na calati na vaidhatīti – samathabalaṃ| idaṃ samathabalaṃ|

katamaṃ vipassanābalaṃ? anic‍cānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| dukkhānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| anattānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| nibbidānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| virāgānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| niraudhānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| rūpai anic‍cānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ  …pai॰… rūpai paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ|

vipassanābalanti kainaṭṭhaina vipassanābalaṃ? anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| dukkhānupassanāya sukhasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| anattānupassanāya attasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| nibbidānupassanāya nandiyā na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| virāgānupassanāya rāgai na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| niraudhānupassanāya samudayai  na kampatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| paṭinissaggānupassanāya ādānai na kampatīti  – vipassanābalaṃ| avij‍jāya ca avij‍jā sahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca na kampati na calati na vaidhatīti – vipassanābalaṃ| idaṃ vipassanābalaṃ|

tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyāti katamaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā? dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| imaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā|

84. sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhīti katamāhi sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi? anic‍cānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, dukkhānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, anattānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, nibbidānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, virāgānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, niraudhānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, paṭinissaggānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, vivaṭṭanānupassanā ñāṇacariyā, sautāpattimaggau ñāṇacariyā, sautāpattiphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā, sakadāgāmimaggau ñāṇacariyā, sakadāgāmiphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā, anāgāmimaggau ñāṇacariyā, anāgāmiphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā, arahattamaggau ñāṇacariyā, arahattaphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā – imāhi sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi|

85. navahi samādhicariyāhīti katamāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samādhicariyā, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samādhicariyā, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samādhicariyā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samādhicariyā, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti samādhicariyā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya  vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca – imāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi|

vasīti  pañ‍ca vasiyau| āvaj‍janavasī, samāpaj‍janavasī, adhiṭṭhānavasī, vuṭṭhānavasī, pac‍cavaikkhaṇāvasī| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ āvaj‍jati; āvaj‍janāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – āvaj‍janavasī| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ samāpaj‍jati; samāpaj‍janāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – samāpaj‍janavasī| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ adhiṭṭhāti; adhiṭṭhānai dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – adhiṭṭhānavasī| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ vuṭṭhāti; vuṭṭhānai dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – vuṭṭhānavasī| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – pac‍cavaikkhaṇāvasī|

dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ āvaj‍jati; āvaj‍janāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – āvaj‍janavasī| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ samāpaj‍jati…pai॰… adhiṭṭhāti… vuṭṭhāti… pac‍cavaikkhati; pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti – pac‍cavaikkhaṇāvasī| imā pañ‍ca vasiyau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dvīhi balaihi saman‍nāgatattā tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭipassaddhiyā sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi vasībhāvatā pañ‍ñā niraudhasamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ’’|

niraudhasamāpattiñāṇaniddaisau catuttiṃsatimau|

35. parinibbānañāṇaniddaisau

86. kathaṃ  sampajānassa pavattapariyādānai pañ‍ñā parinibbānai ñāṇaṃ? idha sampajānau naikkhammaina kāmacchandassa pavattaṃ pariyādiyati, abyāpādaina byāpādassa pavattaṃ pariyādiyati, ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhassa pavattaṃ pariyādiyati, avikkhaipaina uddhac‍cassa pavattaṃ pariyādiyati, dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchāya…pai॰… ñāṇaina avij‍jāya… pāmauj‍jaina aratiyā … paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇānaṃ pavattaṃ pariyādiyati…pai॰… arahattamaggaina  sabbakilaisānaṃ pavattaṃ pariyādiyati|

atha vā pana sampajānassa anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyantassa idañ‍caiva cakkhupavattaṃ pariyādiyati, añ‍ñañ‍ca cakkhupavattaṃ na uppaj‍jati| idañ‍caiva sautapavattaṃ…pai॰… ghānapavattaṃ… jivhāpavattaṃ… kāyapavattaṃ… manaupavattaṃ pariyādiyati, añ‍ñañ‍ca manaupavattaṃ na uppaj‍jati| idaṃ sampajānassa pavattapariyādānai  pañ‍ñā parinibbānai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sampajānassa pavattapariyādānai pañ‍ñā parinibbānai ñāṇaṃ’’|

parinibbānañāṇaniddaisau pañ‍catiṃsatimau|

36. samasīsaṭṭhañāṇaniddaisau

87. kathaṃ sabbadhammānaṃ sammā samucchaidai niraudhai ca anupaṭṭhānatā pañ‍ñā samasīsaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ? sabbadhammānanti – pañ‍cakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, kusalā dhammā, akusalā dhammā, abyākatā dhammā, kāmāvacarā dhammā, rūpāvacarā dhammā, arūpāvacarā dhammā, apariyāpan‍nā dhammā| sammā samucchaidaiti naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ sammā samucchindati| abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ sammā samucchindati| ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ sammā samucchindati| avikkhaipaina uddhac‍caṃ sammā samucchindati| dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ sammā samucchindati| ñāṇaina avij‍jaṃ sammā samucchindati| pāmauj‍jaina aratiṃ sammā samucchindati| paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇai sammā samucchindati…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai sammā samucchindati|

niraudhaiti naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ niraudhaiti| abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ niraudhaiti| ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ niraudhaiti| avikkhaipaina uddhac‍caṃ niraudhaiti| dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ niraudhaiti| ñāṇaina avij‍jaṃ niraudhaiti| pāmauj‍jaina aratiṃ niraudhaiti| paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇai niraudhaiti…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai niraudhaiti|

anupaṭṭhānatāti  naikkhammaṃ paṭiladdhassa kāmacchandau na upaṭṭhāti| abyāpādaṃ paṭiladdhassa byāpādau  na upaṭṭhāti| ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ paṭiladdhassa thinamiddhaṃ na upaṭṭhāti| avikkhaipaṃ paṭiladdhassa uddhac‍caṃ na upaṭṭhāti| dhammavavatthānaṃ paṭiladdhassa vicikicchā  na upaṭṭhāti| ñāṇaṃ paṭiladdhassa avij‍jā na upaṭṭhāti| pāmauj‍jaṃ paṭiladdhassa arati na upaṭṭhāti| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭiladdhassa nīvaraṇā na upaṭṭhahanti…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ paṭiladdhassa sabbakilaisā na upaṭṭhahanti|

samanti kāmacchandassa pahīnattā naikkhammaṃ samaṃ| byāpādassa pahīnattā abyāpādau samaṃ| thinamiddhassa pahīnattā ālaukasañ‍ñā samaṃ| uddhac‍cassa  pahīnattā avikkhaipau samaṃ| vicikicchāya pahīnattā dhammavavatthānaṃ samaṃ| avij‍jāya pahīnattā ñāṇaṃ samaṃ| aratiyā pahīnattā pāmauj‍jaṃ samaṃ| nīvaraṇānaṃ pahīnattā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahīnattā arahattamaggau samaṃ|

sīsanti tairasa sīsāni – palibaudhasīsañ‍ca taṇhā, vinibandhanasīsañ‍ca mānau, parāmāsasīsañ‍ca diṭṭhi, vikkhaipasīsañ‍ca uddhac‍caṃ, saṃkilaisasīsañ‍ca avij‍jā, adhimaukkhasīsañ‍ca saddhā, paggahasīsañ‍ca vīriyaṃ, upaṭṭhānasīsañ‍ca sati, avikkhaipasīsañ‍ca samādhi, dassanasīsañ‍ca pañ‍ñā, pavattasīsañ‍ca jīvitindriyaṃ, gaucarasīsañ‍ca vimaukkhau, saṅkhārasīsañ‍ca niraudhau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sabbadhammānaṃ sammā samucchaidai niraudhai ca anupaṭṭhānatā pañ‍ñā samasīsaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

samasīsaṭṭhañāṇaniddaisau chattiṃsatimau|

37. sal‍laikhaṭṭhañāṇaniddaisau

88. kathaṃ  puthunānattaikattataijapariyādānai pañ‍ñā sal‍laikhaṭṭhai puthunānattaikattataijapariyādānai (ka॰) ñāṇaṃ? puthūti – rāgau puthu, dausau puthu, mauhau puthu, kaudhau…pai॰… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… issā… macchariyaṃ… māyā… sāṭhaiyyaṃ… thambhau… sārambhau… mānau… atimānau… madau… pamādau… sabbai kilaisā… sabbai duc‍caritā… sabbai abhisaṅkhārā… sabbai bhavagāmikammā|

nānattaikattanti  kāmacchandau nānattaṃ, naikkhammaṃ aikattaṃ| byāpādau nānattaṃ, abyāpādau aikattaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ nānattaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñā aikattaṃ| uddhac‍caṃ nānattaṃ, avikkhaipau aikattaṃ| vicikicchā nānattaṃ, dhammavavatthānaṃ aikattaṃ| avij‍jā nānattaṃ, ñāṇaṃ aikattaṃ| arati nānattaṃ, pāmauj‍jaṃ aikattaṃ| nīvaraṇā nānattaṃ, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ aikattaṃ…pai॰… sabbai kilaisā nānattaṃ, arahattamaggau aikattaṃ|

taijauti pañ‍ca taijā – caraṇataijau, guṇataijau, pañ‍ñātaijau, puñ‍ñataijau, dhammataijau| caraṇataijaina taijitattā dussīlyataijaṃ pariyādiyati| guṇataijaina taijitattā aguṇataijaṃ pariyādiyati| pañ‍ñātaijaina taijitattā duppañ‍ñataijaṃ pariyādiyati| puñ‍ñataijaina taijitattā  apuñ‍ñataijaṃ pariyādiyati| dhammataijaina taijitattā adhammataijaṃ pariyādiyati|

sal‍laikhauti kāmacchandau asal‍laikhau, naikkhammaṃ sal‍laikhau| byāpādau asal‍laikhau, abyāpādau sal‍laikhau| thinamiddhaṃ asal‍laikhau, ālaukasañ‍ñā sal‍laikhau| uddhac‍caṃ asal‍laikhau, avikkhaipau sal‍laikhau| vicikicchā asal‍laikhau, dhammavavatthānaṃ sal‍laikhau| avij‍jā asal‍laikhau, ñāṇaṃ sal‍laikhau| arati asal‍laikhau, pāmauj‍jaṃ sal‍laikhau| nīvaraṇā asal‍laikhau, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ sal‍laikhau…pai॰… sabbakilaisā asal‍laikhau, arahattamaggau sal‍laikhau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘puthunānattataijapariyādānai pañ‍ñā sal‍laikhaṭṭhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

sal‍laikhaṭṭhañāṇaniddaisau sattatiṃsatimau|

38. vīriyārambhañāṇaniddaisau

89. kathaṃ asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ? anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā  vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ|

anuppan‍nassa  kāmacchandassa anuppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa pahānāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| anuppan‍nassa naikkhammassa uppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nassa naikkhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

anuppan‍nānaṃ sabbakilaisānaṃ anuppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai  pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… anuppan‍nassa arahattamaggassa uppādāya asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| uppan‍nassa arahattamaggassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘asal‍līnattapahitattapaggahaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā vīriyārambhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

vīriyārambhañāṇaniddaisau aṭṭhatiṃsatimau|

39. atthasandassanañāṇaniddaisau

90. kathaṃ nānādhammappakāsanatā pañ‍ñā atthasandassanai ñāṇaṃ? nānādhammāti pañ‍cakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, kusalā dhammā, akusalā dhammā, abyākatā dhammā, kāmāvacarā  dhammā, rūpāvacarā dhammā, arūpāvacarā dhammā, apariyāpan‍nā dhammā|

pakāsanatāti rūpaṃ anic‍catau pakāsaiti, rūpaṃ dukkhatau pakāsaiti, rūpaṃ anattatau pakāsaiti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰…  jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau pakāsaiti, jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau pakāsaiti, jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau pakāsaiti|

atthasandassanaiti kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammatthaṃ sandassaiti| byāpādaṃ pajahantau abyāpādatthaṃ sandassaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pajahantau ālaukasañ‍ñatthaṃ sandassaiti| uddhac‍caṃ pajahantau avikkhaipatthaṃ sandassaiti| vicikicchaṃ pajahantau dhammavavatthānatthaṃ sandassaiti| avij‍jaṃ pajahantau ñāṇatthaṃ sandassaiti| aratiṃ pajahantau pāmauj‍jatthaṃ sandassaiti| nīvaraṇai pajahantau paṭhamajhānatthaṃ sandassaiti  …pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggatthaṃ sandassaiti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nānādhammapakāsanatā pañ‍ñā atthasandassanai ñāṇaṃ’’|

atthasandassanañāṇaniddaisau navatiṃsatimau|

40. dassanavisuddhiñāṇaniddaisau

91. kathaṃ  sabbadhammānaṃ aikasaṅgahatānānattaikattapaṭivaidhai pañ‍ñā dassanavisuddhiñāṇaṃ? sabbadhammānanti pañ‍cakkhandhā…pai॰… apariyāpan‍nā dhammā|

aikasaṅgahatāti  dvādasahi ākāraihi sabbai dhammā aikasaṅgahitā| tathaṭṭhaina, anattaṭṭhaina, sac‍caṭṭhaina, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina, abhijānanaṭṭhaina, parijānanaṭṭhaina, dhammaṭṭhaina, dhātuṭṭhaina, ñātaṭṭhaina, sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina, phusanaṭṭhaina, abhisamayaṭṭhaina – imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi sabbai dhammā aikasaṅgahitā|

nānattaikattanti kāmacchandau nānattaṃ, naikkhammaṃ aikattaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisā nānattaṃ, arahattamaggau aikattaṃ|

paṭivaidhaiti dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| samudayasac‍caṃ pahānapaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati|

dassanavisuddhīti sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visujjhati; sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visuddhaṃ| sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visujjhati; sakadāgāmiphalakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visuddhaṃ| anāgāmimaggakkhaṇai  dassanaṃ visujjhati; anāgāmiphalakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visuddhaṃ| arahattamaggakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visujjhati; arahattaphalakkhaṇai dassanaṃ visuddhaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sabbadhammānaṃ aikasaṅgahatānānattaikattapaṭivaidhai pañ‍ñā dassanavisuddhiñāṇaṃ’’|

dassanavisuddhiñāṇaniddaisau cattālīsamau|

41. khantiñāṇaniddaisau

92. kathaṃ  viditattā pañ‍ñā khantiñāṇaṃ? rūpaṃ anic‍catau viditaṃ, rūpaṃ dukkhatau viditaṃ, rūpaṃ anattatau viditaṃ| yaṃ yaṃ viditaṃ taṃ taṃ khamatīti – viditattā pañ‍ñā khantiñāṇaṃ| vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhu…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau viditaṃ, jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau viditaṃ, jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau viditaṃ| yaṃ yaṃ viditaṃ taṃ taṃ khamatīti – viditattā pañ‍ñā khantiñāṇaṃ| taṃ  ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘viditattā pañ‍ñā khantiñāṇaṃ’’|

khantiñāṇaniddaisau aikacattālīsamau|

42. pariyaugāhaṇañāṇaniddaisau

93. kathaṃ phuṭṭhattā pañ‍ñā pariyaugāhaṇai ñāṇaṃ? rūpaṃ anic‍catau phusati, rūpaṃ dukkhatau phusati, rūpaṃ anattatau phusati| yaṃ yaṃ phusati taṃ taṃ pariyaugāhatīti – phuṭṭhattā pañ‍ñā pariyaugāhaṇai ñāṇaṃ| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau phusati, dukkhatau phusati, anattatau phusati| yaṃ yaṃ phusati taṃ taṃ pariyaugāhatīti – phuṭṭhattā pañ‍ñā pariyaugāhaṇai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘phuṭṭhattā pañ‍ñā pariyaugāhaṇai ñāṇaṃ’’|

pariyaugāhaṇañāṇaniddaisau dvaicattālīsamau|

43. padaisavihārañāṇaniddaisau

94. kathaṃ  samaudahanai pañ‍ñā padaisavihārai ñāṇaṃ? micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, micchādiṭṭhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi  vaidayitaṃ, sammādiṭṭhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| micchāsaṅkappapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, micchāsaṅkappavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| sammāsaṅkappapac‍cayāpi  vaidayitaṃ, sammāsaṅkappavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ…pai॰… micchāvimuttipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, micchāvimuttivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| sammāvimuttipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, sammāvimuttivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| chandapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, chandavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| vitak‍kapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, vitak‍kavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| sañ‍ñāpac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, sañ‍ñāvūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ|

chandau ca avūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca avūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca avūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| chandau ca vūpasantau  hauti, vitak‍kau ca avūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca avūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| chandau ca vūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca vūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca avūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| chandau ca vūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca vūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca vūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| appattassa pattiyā atthi āsavaṃ, tasmimpi ṭhānai anuppattai tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina  pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samaudahanai pañ‍ñā padaisavihārai ñāṇaṃ’’|

padaisavihārañāṇaniddaisau taicattālīsamau|

44-49. chavivaṭṭañāṇaniddaisau

95. kathaṃ adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? naikkhammādhipatattā pañ‍ñā kāmacchandatau sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| abyāpādādhipatattā pañ‍ñā byāpādatau sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| ālaukasañ‍ñādhipatattā pañ‍ñā  thinamiddhatau sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| avikkhaipādhipatattā pañ‍ñā uddhac‍catau sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| dhammavavatthānādhipatattā pañ‍ñā vicikicchāya sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| ñāṇādhipatattā pañ‍ñā avij‍jāya sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| pāmauj‍jādhipatattā pañ‍ñā aratiyā sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| paṭhamajjhānādhipatattā pañ‍ñā nīvaraṇaihi sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… arahattamaggādhipatattā pañ‍ñā sabbakilaisaihi sañ‍ñāya vivaṭṭatīti – adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai  ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘adhipatattā pañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

96. kathaṃ nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? kāmacchandau nānattaṃ, naikkhammaṃ aikattaṃ| naikkhammaikattaṃ caitayatau kāmacchandatau cittaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – nānattai  pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| byāpādau nānattaṃ, abyāpādau aikattaṃ| abyāpādaikattaṃ caitayatau byāpādatau cittaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ nānattaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñā aikattaṃ| ālaukasañ‍ñaikattaṃ caitayatau thinamiddhatau cittaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisā nānattaṃ, arahattamaggau aikattaṃ| arahattamaggaikattaṃ caitayatau sabbakilaisaihi cittaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nānattai pañ‍ñā caitauvivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

97. kathaṃ adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| byāpādaṃ  pajahantau abyāpādavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ pajahantau ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā  cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘adhiṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā cittavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

98. kathaṃ suñ‍ñatai pañ‍ñā ñāṇavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? ‘‘cakkhu suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ jānatau pajānatau (syā॰) passatau cakkhābhinivaisatau kāmābhinivaisatau (syā॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā ñāṇaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – suñ‍ñatai pañ‍ñā ñāṇavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘sautaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ… jivhā suñ‍ñā… kāyau suñ‍ñau… manau suñ‍ñau attaina vā attaniyaina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ jānatau passatau manābhinivaisatau ñāṇaṃ vivaṭṭatīti – suñ‍ñatai pañ‍ñā ñāṇavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘suñ‍ñatai pañ‍ñā ñāṇavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

99. kathaṃ vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai  ñāṇaṃ| abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā  vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| avikkhaipaina uddhac‍caṃ vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai  ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai vausaj‍jatīti – vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vausaggai pañ‍ñā vimaukkhavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

100. kathaṃ tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ? dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhaṃ saṅkhataṭṭhaṃ santāpaṭṭhaṃ vipariṇāmaṭṭhaṃ parijānantau vivaṭṭatīti – tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhaṃ nidānaṭṭhaṃ sañ‍ñaugaṭṭhaṃ palibaudhaṭṭhaṃ pajahantau vivaṭṭatīti – tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhaṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ asaṅkhataṭṭhaṃ amataṭṭhaṃ sacchikarauntau vivaṭṭatīti – tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ| maggassa niyyānaṭṭhaṃ haituṭṭhaṃ dassanaṭṭhaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaṃ bhāvaintau vivaṭṭatīti – tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ|

sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau, caitauvivaṭṭau, cittavivaṭṭau, ñāṇavivaṭṭau, vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, sac‍cavivaṭṭau| sañ‍jānantau vivaṭṭatīti – sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau| caitayantau vivaṭṭatīti – caitauvivaṭṭau| vijānantau vivaṭṭatīti – cittavivaṭṭau| ñāṇaṃ karauntau vivaṭṭatīti – ñāṇavivaṭṭau| vausaj‍jantau vivaṭṭatīti – vimaukkhavivaṭṭau| tathaṭṭhai vivaṭṭatīti – sac‍cavivaṭṭau|

yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau, tattha caitauvivaṭṭau| yattha caitauvivaṭṭau, tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau tattha cittavivaṭṭau| yattha cittavivaṭṭau, tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau, tattha ñāṇavivaṭṭau| yattha ñāṇavivaṭṭau, tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau, tattha vimaukkhavivaṭṭau| yattha vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, tattha sac‍cavivaṭṭau| yattha sac‍cavivaṭṭau, tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau vimaukkhavivaṭṭau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘tathaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā sac‍cavivaṭṭai ñāṇaṃ’’|

chavivaṭṭañāṇaniddaisau navacattālīsamau|

50. iddhividhañāṇaniddaisau

101. kathaṃ  kāyampi cittampi aikavavatthānatā sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānavasaina ijjhanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā iddhividhai ñāṇaṃ? idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaiti paridamaiti, muduṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaitvā paridamaitvā muduṃ karitvā kammaniyaṃ kāyampi cittai samaudahati, cittampi kāyai samaudahati, kāyavasaina cittaṃ pariṇāmaiti, cittavasaina kāyaṃ pariṇāmaiti, kāyavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, cittavasaina kāyaṃ adhiṭṭhāti; kāyavasaina cittaṃ pariṇāmaitvā cittavasaina kāyaṃ pariṇāmaitvā kāyavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahitvā cittavasaina kāyaṃ adhiṭṭhahitvā sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca kāyai auk‍kamitvā viharati| sau tathābhāvitaina cittaina parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina iddhividhañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti|

102. aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraukuḍḍaṃ (syā॰) tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati caṅkamati (syā॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.238 passitabbā saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimasati (syā॰) parimaj‍jati; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyampi cittampi aikavavatthānatā sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānavasaina ijjhanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā iddhividhai ñāṇaṃ’’|

iddhividhañāṇaniddaisau pañ‍ñāsamau|

51. sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇaniddaisau

103. kathaṃ  vitak‍kavipphāravasaina nānattaikattasaddanimittānaṃ pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇaṃ? idha bhikkhu chandasamādhi…pai॰… vīriyasamādhi… cittasamādhi… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaiti paridamaiti, muduṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaitvā paridamaitvā, muduṃ karitvā kammaniyaṃ dūraipi saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, santikaipi saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, auḷārikānampi saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, sukhumānampi saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, saṇhasaṇhānampi saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, puratthimāyapi disāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, pacchimāyapi disāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, uttarāyapi disāya saddānaṃ  saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, dakkhiṇāyapi disāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, puratthimāyapi anudisāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, pacchimāyapi anudisāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, uttarāyapi anudisāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, dakkhiṇāyapi anudisāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, haiṭṭhimāyapi disāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti, uparimāyapi disāya saddānaṃ saddanimittaṃ manasi karauti| sau tathābhāvitaina cittaina parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti – dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vitak‍kavipphāravasaina nānattaikattasaddanimittānaṃ pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇaṃ’’|

sautadhātuvisuddhiñāṇaniddaisau aikapañ‍ñāsamau|

52. caitaupariyañāṇaniddaisau

104. kathaṃ  tiṇṇaṃ cittānaṃ vipphārattā indriyānaṃ pasādavasaina nānattaikattaviñ‍ñāṇacariyāpariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā caitaupariyañāṇaṃ? idha bhikkhu  chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaiti paridamaiti, muduṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| sau  imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaitvā paridamaitvā, muduṃ karitvā kammaniyaṃ aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘‘idaṃ rūpaṃ saumanassindriyasamuṭṭhitaṃ, idaṃ rūpaṃ daumanassindriyasamuṭṭhitaṃ, idaṃ rūpaṃ upaikkhindriyasamuṭṭhita’’nti| sau tathābhāvitaina cittaina parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina caitaupariyañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ cittānaṃ vipphārattā indriyānaṃ pasādavasaina nānattaikattaviñ‍ñāṇacariyāpariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā caitaupariyañāṇaṃ’’|

caitaupariyañāṇaniddaisau dvaipañ‍ñāsamau|

53. pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaniddaisau

105. kathaṃ pac‍cayapavattānaṃ dhammānaṃ nānattaikattakammavipphāravasaina  pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ? idha bhikkhu  chandasamādhi…pai॰… muduṃ karitvā kammaniyaṃ aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘‘imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti; aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

sau  tathābhāvitaina cittaina parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau, jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai  anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivama āyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ  anussarati| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘pac‍cayapavattānaṃ dhammānaṃ nānattaikattakammavipphāravasaina pariyaugāhaṇai pañ‍ñā pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ’’|

pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaniddaisau taipañ‍ñāsamau|

54. dibbacakkhuñāṇaniddaisau

106. kathaṃ aubhāsavasaina nānattaikattarūpanimittānaṃ dassanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ? idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaiti paridamaiti, muduṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| sau imaisu catūsu iddhipādaisu cittaṃ paribhāvaitvā paridamaitvā, muduṃ karitvā  kammaniyaṃ ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karauti, divāsañ‍ñaṃ adhiṭṭhāti – ‘‘yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’’| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| sau tathābhāvitaina cittaina parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina, sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai  sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, manauduc‍caritaina  saman‍nāgatā, ariyānaṃ upavādakā, micchādiṭṭhikā, micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā, sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| taṃ  ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘aubhāsavasaina nānattaikattarūpanimittānaṃ dassanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ’’|

dibbacakkhuñāṇaniddaisau catupañ‍ñāsamau|

55. āsavakkhayañāṇaniddaisau

107. kathaṃ catusaṭṭhiyā ākāraihi tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ vasibhāvatā pañ‍ñā āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ? katamaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ? anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa añ‍ñindriyassa añ‍ñātāvindriyassa|

anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati, añ‍ñindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ aikaṃ ṭhānaṃ gacchati – sautāpattimaggaṃ| añ‍ñindriyaṃ cha ṭhānāni gacchati – sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmimaggaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattamaggaṃ| añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ aikaṃ ṭhānaṃ gacchati – arahattaphalaṃ|

sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ paggahaparivāraṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānaparivāraṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipaparivāraṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dassanaparivāraṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ vijānanaparivāraṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ abhisandanaparivāraṃ  hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai jātā dhammā ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ sabbaiva kusalā haunti, sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva niyyānikā haunti, sabbaiva apacayagāminau haunti, sabbaiva laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaparivārā haunti, nissayaparivārā haunti, sampayuttaparivārā haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca|

sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai añ‍ñindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ paggahaparivāraṃ  hauti, satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānaparivāraṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipaparivāraṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ  dassanaparivāraṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ vijānanaparivāraṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ abhisandanaparivāraṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai jātā dhammā sabbaiva abyākatā haunti, ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai añ‍ñindriyassa imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaparivārā haunti, nissayaparivārā haunti, sampayuttaparivārā haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca|

sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇai  …pai॰… sakadāgāmiphalakkhaṇai…pai॰… anāgāmimaggakkhaṇai…pai॰… anāgāmiphalakkhaṇai…pai॰… arahattamaggakkhaṇai añ‍ñindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti…pai॰… jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| arahattamaggakkhaṇai jātā dhammā ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ sabbaiva kusalā haunti, sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva niyyānikā haunti, sabbaiva apacayagāminau haunti, sabbaiva laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| arahattamaggakkhaṇai añ‍ñindriyassa imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaparivārā haunti, nissayaparivārā haunti, sampayuttaparivārā  haunti, sahagatā  haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca|

arahattaphalakkhaṇai añ‍ñātāvindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ paggahaparivāraṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānaparivāraṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipaparivāraṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dassanaparivāraṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ vijānanaparivāraṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ abhisandanaparivāraṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| arahattaphalakkhaṇai jātā dhammā sabbaiva abyākatā haunti, ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| arahattaphalakkhaṇai añ‍ñātāvindriyassa imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaparivārā haunti, nissayaparivārā haunti, sampayuttaparivārā haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca| iti imāni aṭṭhaṭṭhakāni catusaṭṭhi haunti|

āsavāti  katamai tai āsavā? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, diṭṭhāsavau, avij‍jāsavau| katthaitai āsavā khīyanti? sautāpattimaggaina anavasaisau diṭṭhāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau  kāmāsavau  khīyati, apāyagamanīyau bhavāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| anāgāmimaggaina anavasaisau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| arahattamaggaina anavasaisau bhavāsavau khīyati, anavasaisau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘catusaṭṭhiyā ākāraihi tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ vasibhāvatā pañ‍ñā āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ’’|

āsavakkhayañāṇaniddaisau pañ‍capañ‍ñāsamau|

56-63. sac‍cañāṇacatuk‍kadvayaniddaisau

108. kathaṃ  pariñ‍ñaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, pahānaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā samudayai ñāṇaṃ, sacchikiriyaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā niraudhai ñāṇaṃ, bhāvanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ? dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau vipariṇāmaṭṭhau pariñ‍ñātaṭṭhau; samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau nidānaṭṭhau sañ‍ñaugaṭṭhau palibaudhaṭṭhau pahānaṭṭhau; niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau asaṅkhataṭṭhau amataṭṭhau sacchikiriyaṭṭhau; maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau haituṭṭhau dassanaṭṭhau ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau bhāvanaṭṭhau| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘pariñ‍ñaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, pahānaṭṭhai  pañ‍ñā samudayai ñāṇaṃ, sacchikiriyaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā niraudhai ñāṇaṃ, bhāvanaṭṭhai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ’’|

109. kathaṃ dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ? maggasamaṅgissa  ñāṇaṃ dukkhai paitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai paitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai paitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya paitaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ dukkhai ñāṇaṃ? dukkhaṃ ārabbha yā uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau  dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūri maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhai ñāṇaṃ| dukkhasamudayaṃ ārabbha…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhaṃ ārabbha…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ārabbha yā uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ’’|

sac‍cañāṇacatuk‍kadvayaniddaisau taisaṭṭhimau|

64-67. suddhikapaṭisambhidāñāṇaniddaisau

110. kathaṃ  atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ? atthaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttīsu ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| atthanānattai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammanānattai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttinānattai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānanānattai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, atthavavatthānai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammavavatthānai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ niruttivavatthānai  pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānavavatthānai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

atthasal‍lakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammasal‍lakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttisal‍lakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānasal‍lakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| atthūpalakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammūpalakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttūpalakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānūpalakkhaṇai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

atthappabhaidai  pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammappabhaidai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttippabhaidai  pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānappabhaidai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| atthappabhāvanai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammappabhāvanai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttippabhāvanai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānappabhāvanai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ|

atthajautanai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammajautanai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttijautanai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānajautanai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| atthaviraucanai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammaviraucanai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttiviraucanai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānaviraucanai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| atthappakāsanai pañ‍ñā atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammappakāsanai pañ‍ñā dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttippakāsanai pañ‍ñā niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānappakāsanai pañ‍ñā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ| taṃ  ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ’’|

suddhikapaṭisambhidāñāṇaniddaisau sattasaṭṭhimau|

68. indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaniddaisau

111. katamaṃ  tathāgatassa indriyaparaupariyatta ñāṇaṃ? idha tathāgatau sattai passati apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau|

apparajakkhai mahārajakkhaiti saddhau puggalau apparajakkhau, assaddhau puggalau mahārajakkhau| āraddhavīriyau puggalau apparajakkhau, kusītau puggalau mahārajakkhau| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau apparajakkhau, muṭṭhassati puggalau mahārajakkhau| samāhitau puggalau apparajakkhau, asamāhitau puggalau mahārajakkhau| pañ‍ñavā puggalau apparajakkhau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau mahārajakkhau|

tikkhindriyai  mudindriyaiti saddhau puggalau tikkhindriyau, assaddhau puggalau mudindriyau| āraddhavīriyau puggalau tikkhindriyau, kusītau puggalau mudindriyau| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau tikkhindriyau, muṭṭhassati puggalau mudindriyau| samāhitau puggalau tikkhindriyau, asamāhitau puggalau mudindriyau| pañ‍ñavā puggalau tikkhindriyau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau mudindriyau|

svākārai dvākāraiti saddhau puggalau svākārau, assaddhau puggalau dvākārau| āraddhavīriyau puggalau svākārau, kusītau puggalau dvākārau| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau svākārau, muṭṭhassati  puggalau dvākārau| samāhitau puggalau svākārau, asamāhitau puggalau dvākārau| pañ‍ñavā puggalau svākārau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau dvākārau|

suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayaiti saddhau puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, assaddhau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau| āraddhavīriyau puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, kusītau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, muṭṭhassati puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau| samāhitau puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, asamāhitau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau| pañ‍ñavā puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau|

appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinauti saddhau puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, assaddhau puggalau na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī| āraddhavīriyau puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, kusītau puggalau na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, muṭṭhassati puggalau na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī| samāhitau puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, asamāhitau puggalau na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī| pañ‍ñavā puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, duppañ‍ñau puggalau na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī|

112. laukauti – khandhalaukau, dhātulaukau, āyatanalaukau, vipattibhavalaukau, vipattisambhavalaukau, sampattibhavalaukau, sampattisambhavalaukau|

aikau laukau – sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā| dvai laukā – nāmañ‍ca, rūpañ‍ca| tayau laukā – tissau vaidanā| cattārau laukā – cattārau āhārā| pañ‍ca laukā – pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| cha laukā – cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni| satta laukā – satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| aṭṭha laukā – aṭṭha laukadhammā| nava laukā – nava sattāvāsā| dasa laukā – dasāyatanāni| dvādasalaukā – dvādasāyatanāni| aṭṭhārasa laukā – aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau|

vaj‍janti sabbai kilaisā vaj‍jā, sabbai duc‍caritā vaj‍jā, sabbai abhisaṅkhārā vaj‍jā, sabbai bhavagāmikammā vaj‍jā| iti imasmiñ‍ca laukai imasmiñ‍ca vaj‍jai tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti, saiyyathāpi ukkhittāsikai vadhakai| imaihi pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi imāni pañ‍cindriyāni jānāti passati añ‍ñāti paṭivijjhati – idaṃ tathāgatassa indriyaparaupariyattai ñāṇaṃ|

indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaniddaisau aṭṭhasaṭṭhimau|

69. āsayānusayañāṇaniddaisau

113. katamaṃ  tathāgatassa sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ? idha tathāgatau sattānaṃ āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, bhabbābhabbai sattai pajānāti| katamau katamau ca (syā॰ ka॰) sattānaṃ āsayau? ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘asassatau  laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā| iti bhavadiṭṭhisan‍nissitā vā sattā haunti vibhavadiṭṭhisan‍nissitā vā|

aitai vā pana ubhau antai anupagamma idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu anulaumikā khanti paṭiladdhā hauti, yathābhūtaṃ vā ñāṇaṃ| kāmaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau kāmagarukau kāmāsayau kāmādhimuttau’’ti| kāmaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau naikkhammagarukau naikkhammāsayau naikkhammādhimuttau’’ti| naikkhammaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau (naikkhammagarukau naikkhammāsayau naikkhammādhimuttau’’ti| naikkhammaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau kāmagarukau kāmāsayau kāmādhimuttau’’ti| byāpādaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva  jānāti) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhā natthi syāmapautthakai, aivamuparipi – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau byāpādagarukau byāpādāsayau byāpādādhimuttau’’ti| byāpādaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau (abyāpādagarukau abyāpādāsayau abyāpādādhimuttau’’ti| abyāpādaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau abyāpādagarukau abyāpādāsayau abyāpādādhimuttau’’ti| abyāpādaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau byāpādagarukau byāpādāsayau byāpādādhimuttau’’ti| thinamiddhaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti) – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau thinamiddhagarukau thinamiddhāsayau thinamiddhādhimuttau’’ti| thinamiddhaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau ālaukasañ‍ñāgarukau ālaukasañ‍ñāsayau ālaukasañ‍ñādhimuttau’’ti| ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau (ālaukasañ‍ñāgarukau ālaukasañ‍ñāsayau ālaukasañ‍ñādhimuttau’’ti| ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ saivantañ‍ñaiva jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau thinamiddhagarukau thinamiddhāsayau thinamiddhādhimuttau’’ti|) ayaṃ sattānaṃ āsayau|

114. katamau  ca sattānaṃ anusayau? sattānusayā – kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau, mānānusayau, diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau| yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aittha sattānaṃ kāmarāgānusayau anusaiti| yaṃ laukai appiyarūpaṃ asātarūpaṃ, aittha sattānaṃ paṭighānusayau anusaiti| iti imaisu dvīsu dhammaisu avij‍jā anupatitā, tadaikaṭṭhau mānau ca diṭṭhi ca vicikicchā ca daṭṭhabbā| ayaṃ sattānaṃ anusayau|

katamañ‍ca sattānaṃ caritaṃ? puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau parittabhūmakau vā mahābhūmakau vā| idaṃ sattānaṃ caritaṃ|

115. katamā ca sattānaṃ adhimutti? santi sattā hīnādhimuttikā, santi sattā paṇītādhimuttikā| hīnādhimuttikā sattā hīnādhimuttikai sattai saivanti bhajanti payirupāsanti| paṇītādhimuttikā sattā paṇītādhimuttikai sattai saivanti bhajanti payirupāsanti| atītampi addhānaṃ hīnādhimuttikā sattā hīnādhimuttikai sattai saiviṃsu bhajiṃsu payirupāsiṃsu; paṇītādhimuttikā sattā paṇītādhimuttikai sattai saiviṃsu bhajiṃsu payirupāsiṃsu| anāgatampi addhānaṃ hīnādhimuttikā sattā hīnādhimuttikai sattai saivissanti bhajissanti payirupāsissanti; paṇītādhimuttikā sattā paṇītādhimuttikai sattai saivissanti bhajissanti payirupāsissanti| ayaṃ sattānaṃ adhimutti|

katamai  sattā abhabbā? yai tai sattā kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, assaddhā acchandikā duppañ‍ñā, abhabbā niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ – imai tai sattā abhabbā|

katamai sattā bhabbā? yai tai sattā na kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, na kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, na vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā, saddhā chandikā pañ‍ñavantau, bhabbā niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ – imai tai sattā bhabbā| idaṃ tathāgatassa sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ|

āsayānusayañāṇaniddaisau navasaṭṭhimau|

70. yamakapāṭihīrañāṇaniddaisau

116. katamaṃ  tathāgatassa yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ? idha tathāgatau yamakapāṭihīraṃ karauti asādhāraṇaṃ sāvakaihi| uparimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati; haiṭṭhimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, uparimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati; puratthimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, pacchimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati; pacchimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, puratthimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇaakkhitau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmaakkhitau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmaakkhitau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇaakkhitau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇakaṇṇasautatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmakaṇṇasautatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmakaṇṇasautatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇakaṇṇasautatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇanāsikāsautatau aggikkhandhau  pavattati, vāmanāsikāsautatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmanāsikāsautatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇanāsikāsautatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇaaṃsakūṭatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmaaṃsakūṭatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmaaṃsakūṭatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇaaṃsakūṭatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇahatthatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmahatthatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmahatthatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇahatthatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇapassatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmapassatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmapassatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇapassatau udakadhārā pavattati; dakkhiṇapādatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, vāmapādatau udakadhārā pavattati; vāmapādatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, dakkhiṇapādatau udakadhārā pavattati; aṅgulaṅgulaihi aggikkhandhau pavattati, aṅgulantarikāhi udakadhārā pavattati; aṅgulantarikāhi aggikkhandhau pavattati, aṅgulaṅgulaihi udakadhārā pavattati; aikaikalaumatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, aikaikalaumatau udakadhārā pavattati; laumakūpatau laumakūpatau  aggikkhandhau pavattati, laumakūpatau laumakūpatau udakadhārā pavattati|

chan‍naṃ  vaṇṇānaṃ – nīlānaṃ, pītakānaṃ, lauhitakānaṃ, audātānaṃ, mañ‍jiṭṭhānaṃ mañ‍jaiṭṭhānaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), pabhassarānaṃ bhagavā caṅkamati, nimmitau tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā tiṭṭhati, nimmitau caṅkamati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā  nisīdati, nimmitau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, nimmitau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā| nimmitau caṅkamati, bhagavā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau tiṭṭhati, bhagavā caṅkamati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau nisīdati, bhagavā caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau saiyyaṃ kappaiti, bhagavā caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā| idaṃ tathāgatassa yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ|

yamakapāṭihīrañāṇaniddaisau sattatimau|

71. mahākaruṇāñāṇaniddaisau

117. katamaṃ tathāgatassa mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ? bahukaihi ākāraihi passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| ādittau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| uyyuttau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| payātau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ  bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| kummaggappaṭipan‍nau kummaggaṃ paṭipan‍nau (syā॰) laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| upanīyati laukau addhuvauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| atāṇau attāṇau (syā॰) laukau anabhissarauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| assakau laukau, sabbaṃ pahāya gamanīyanti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| ūnau laukau atītau taṇhādāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| atāyanau  laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… alaiṇau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ  …pai॰… asaraṇau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… asaraṇībhūtau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

uddhatau laukau avūpasantauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… sasal‍lau laukasan‍nivāsau, viddhau puthusal‍laihi; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci sal‍lānaṃ uddhatā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jandhakārāvaraṇau laukasan‍nivāsau aṇḍabhūtau kilaisapañ‍jarapakkhittau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci ālaukaṃ dassaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jāgatau laukasan‍nivāsau aṇḍabhūtau pariyaunaddhau tantākulakajātau tantākulajātau (syā॰) kulāgaṇḍikajātau guḷāguṇṭhikajātau (syā॰), gulāguṇḍikajātau (ka॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.95 passitabbā muñ‍japabbajabhūtau apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattatīti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jāvisadausasaṃlittau laukasan‍nivāsau kilaisakalalībhūtauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… rāgadausamauhajaṭājaṭitau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci jaṭaṃ vijaṭaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

taṇhāsaṅghāṭapaṭimuk‍kau  laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhājālaina autthaṭau autthatau (ka॰) laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhāsautaina vuyhati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhāsañ‍ñaujanaina sañ‍ñuttau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhānusayaina anusaṭau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhāsantāpaina santappati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… taṇhāpariḷāhaina pariḍayhati  laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

diṭṭhisaṅghāṭapaṭimuk‍kau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhijālaina autthaṭau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhisautaina vuyhati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhisañ‍ñaujanaina sañ‍ñuttau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhānusayaina anusaṭau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhisantāpaina santappati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhipariḷāhaina pariḍayhati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

jātiyā  anugatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… jarāya anusaṭau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… byādhinā abhibhūtau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ  …pai॰… maraṇaina abbhāhatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dukkhai patiṭṭhitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|
