taṇhāya uḍḍitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… jarāpākāraparikkhittau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mac‍cupāsaina parikkhittau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahābandhanabandhau laukasan‍nivāsau – rāgabandhanaina dausabandhanaina  mauhabandhanaina mānabandhanaina diṭṭhibandhanaina kilaisabandhanaina duc‍caritabandhanaina; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci bandhanaṃ maucaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahāsambādhappaṭipan‍nau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci aukāsaṃ dassaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ… mahāpalibaudhaina palibuddhau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci palibaudhaṃ chaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahāpapātai patitau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci papātā uddhatā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahākantārappaṭipan‍nau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci kantāraṃ tāraitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahāsaṃsārappaṭipan‍nau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci saṃsārā maucaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahāviduggai samparivattati laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci viduggā uddhatā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahāpalipai mahāpal‍laipai (syā॰) palipan‍nau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci palipā uddhatā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

abbhāhatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… ādittau laukasan‍nivāsau – rāgagginā dausagginā mauhagginā jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci nibbāpaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… un‍nītakau laukasan‍nivāsau hañ‍ñati nic‍camatāṇau pattadaṇḍau tak‍karauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… vaj‍jabandhanabaddhau laukasan‍nivāsau āghātanapac‍cupaṭṭhitau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci bandhanaṃ maucaitā, añ‍ñatra  mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… anāthau  laukasan‍nivāsau paramakāruñ‍ñappattau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci tāyaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dukkhābhitun‍nau dukkhābhituṇṇau (ka॰) laukasan‍nivāsau cirarattaṃ pīḷitauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… gadhitau laukasan‍nivāsau nic‍caṃ pipāsitauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

andhau laukasan‍nivāsau acakkhukauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… hatanaittau laukasan‍nivāsau apariṇāyakauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… vipathapakkhandau vipathaṃ pakkhantau (syā॰) laukasan‍nivāsau añ‍jasāparaddhau; tassa natthañ‍ñau  kauci ariyapathaṃ ānaitā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… mahaughapakkhandau laukasan‍nivāsau; tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci aughā uddhatā, añ‍ñatra mayāti – passantānaṃ…pai॰…|

118. dvīhi diṭṭhigataihi pariyuṭṭhitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… tīhi duc‍caritaihi vippaṭipan‍nau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… catūhi yaugaihi yuttau laukasan‍nivāsau catuyaugayaujitauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… catūhi ganthaihi gaṇṭhaihi (syā॰) ganthitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… catūhi upādānaihi upādiyati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍cagatisamāruḷhau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi raj‍jati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi autthaṭau  laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… chahi vivādamūlaihi vivadati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… chahi taṇhākāyaihi raj‍jati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… chahi diṭṭhigataihi pariyuṭṭhitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ  …pai॰… sattahi anusayaihi anusaṭau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… sattahi sañ‍ñaujanaihi sañ‍ñuttau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… sattahi mānaihi un‍natau uṇṇatau (syā॰ ka॰) laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhahi laukadhammaihi samparivattati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhahi micchattaihi niyyātau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhahi purisadausaihi dussati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… navahi āghātavatthūhi āghātitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ  …pai॰… navavidhamānaihi un‍natau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… navahi taṇhāmūlakaihi dhammaihi raj‍jati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasahi kilaisavatthūhi kilissati laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasahi āghātavatthūhi āghātitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasahi akusalakammapathaihi saman‍nāgatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasahi sañ‍ñaujanaihi sañ‍ñuttau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasahi micchattaihi niyyātau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasavatthukāya micchādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… dasavatthukāya antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhasatataṇhāpapañ‍casataihi papañ‍citau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ  sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| dvāsaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigataihi pariyuṭṭhitau laukasan‍nivāsauti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati|

ahañ‍camhi  tiṇṇau, laukau ca atiṇṇau ahaṃ camhi muttau, laukau ca amuttau; ahañ‍camhi dantau, laukau ca adantau; ahaṃ camhi santau, laukau  ca asantau; ahaṃ camhi assatthau, laukau ca anassatthau; ahaṃ camhi parinibbutau, laukau ca aparinibbutau; pahaumi khvāhaṃ tiṇṇau tāraituṃ, muttau maucaituṃ, dantau damaituṃ, santau samaituṃ, assatthau assāsaituṃ, parinibbutau parai ca parinibbāpaitunti – passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati| idaṃ tathāgatassa mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ|

mahākaruṇāñāṇaniddaisau aikasattatimau|

72-73. sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaniddaisau

119. katamaṃ tathāgatassa sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ? sabbaṃ saṅkhatamasaṅkhataṃ anavasaisaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ anāvaraṇaṃ ñāṇaṃ (syā॰) aivamuparipi|

120. atītaṃ  sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| anāgataṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| pac‍cuppan‍naṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

cakkhu caiva rūpā ca, aivaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| sautañ‍caiva saddā ca…pai॰… ghānañ‍caiva gandhā ca… jivhā caiva rasā ca… kāyau caiva phauṭṭhabbā ca… manau caiva  dhammā ca, aivaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā anic‍caṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṭṭhaṃ anattaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā rūpassa anic‍caṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṭṭhaṃ  anattaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā vaidanāya…pai॰… yāvatā sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… yāvatā saṅkhārānaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… yāvatā cakkhussa…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa anic‍caṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṭṭhaṃ anattaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā abhiñ‍ñāya abhiñ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā pariñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā pahānassa pahānāya (syā॰) pahānaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā bhāvanāya bhāvanaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā sacchikiriyāya sacchikiriyaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā khandhānaṃ khandhaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā dhātūnaṃ dhātuṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā āyatanānaṃ āyatanaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā saṅkhatānaṃ saṅkhataṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā asaṅkhatassa asaṅkhataṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā  kusalai dhammai, sabbaṃ sabbai (aṭṭhakathā) jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā akusalai dhammai…pai॰… yāvatā abyākatai dhammai… yāvatā kāmāvacarai dhammai… yāvatā rūpāvacarai  dhammai… yāvatā arūpāvacarai dhammai… yāvatā apariyāpan‍nai dhammai, sabbaṃ sabbai (aṭṭhakathā) jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā samudayassa samudayaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā maggassa maggaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā atthapaṭisambhidāya atthapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā dhammapaṭisambhidāya dhammapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā niruttipaṭisambhidāya niruttipaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya  paṭibhānapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā  indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā  sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, taṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

121.

 na tassa addiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci, athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ, tathāgatau taina samantacakkhūti mahāni॰ 156; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 85||

samantacakkhūti kainaṭṭhaina samantacakkhu? cuddasa buddhañāṇāni| dukkhai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā  paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, atthapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, dhammapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, niruttipaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, indriyaparaupariyattai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ – imāni cuddasa buddhañāṇāni| imaisaṃ cuddasan‍naṃ buddhañāṇānaṃ aṭṭha ñāṇāni sāvakasādhāraṇāni; cha ñāṇāni asādhāraṇāni sāvakaihi|

yāvatā  dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau, sabbau ñātau; añ‍ñātau dukkhaṭṭhau natthīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau, sabbau ñātau, sabbau diṭṭhau, sabbau viditau, sabbau sacchikatau, sabbau phassitau pañ‍ñāya; aphassitau pañ‍ñāya dukkhaṭṭhau natthīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ| yāvatā samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau…pai॰… yāvatā niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau… yāvatā maggassa maggaṭṭhau…pai॰… yāvatā atthapaṭisambhidāya atthapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā dhammapaṭisambhidāya dhammapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā niruttipaṭisambhidāya niruttipaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya paṭibhānapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau, sabbau ñātau, sabbau diṭṭhau, sabbau viditau, sabbau sacchikatau, sabbau phassitau pañ‍ñāya; aphassitau pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau natthīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaṃ…pai॰… yāvatā sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ; sabba ñātaṃ, sabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ, sabbaṃ viditaṃ, sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ, sabbaṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya; aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ natthīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

yāvatā sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya  diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, sabbaṃ ñātaṃ, sabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ, sabbaṃ viditaṃ, sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ, sabbaṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya; aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya natthīti – sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha āvaraṇaṃ natthīti – anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ|

na  tassa addiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci, athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ, tathāgatau taina samantacakkhūti||

sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaniddaisau taisattatimau|

ñāṇakathā niṭṭhitā|

2. diṭṭhikathā

122. kā  diṭṭhi, kati diṭṭhiṭṭhānāni, kati diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānāni, kati diṭṭhiyau, kati diṭṭhābhinivaisā, katamau diṭṭhiṭṭhānasamugghātauti?

[1] kā diṭṭhīti abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| [2] kati diṭṭhiṭṭhānānīti aṭṭha diṭṭhiṭṭhānāni| [3] kati diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānānīti aṭṭhārasa diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānāni| [4] kati diṭṭhiyauti sauḷasa diṭṭhiyau| [5] kati diṭṭhābhinivaisāti tīṇi sataṃ diṭṭhābhinivaisā| [6] katamau diṭṭhiṭṭhānasamugghātauti sautāpattimaggau diṭṭhiṭṭhānasamugghātau|

123. kathaṃ abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi? kati abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhīti (syā॰) rūpaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| vaidanaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… saṅkhārai aitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| cakkhuṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sautaṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānaṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhaṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyaṃ aitaṃ mama… manaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| rūpai rūpaṃ (syā॰) tathā pañ‍casu ārammaṇaisu aikavacanaina aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddai aitaṃ mama… gandhai aitaṃ mama… rasai aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbai aitaṃ mama… dhammai aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| cakkhusamphassaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sautasamphassaṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānasamphassaṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhāsamphassaṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyasamphassaṃ  aitaṃ mama… manausamphassaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sautasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… ghānasamphassajaṃ  vaidanaṃ… jivhāsamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… kāyasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… manausamphassajaṃ  vaidanaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

rūpasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… rasasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammasañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| rūpasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama… rasasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammasañ‍caitanaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| rūpataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama… rasataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammataṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| rūpavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama… rasavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammavitak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| rūpavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saddavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama… rasavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammavicāraṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

pathavīdhātuṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… taijaudhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… vāyaudhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… ākāsadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| pathavīkasiṇaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… āpaukasiṇaṃ… taijaukasiṇaṃ… vāyaukasiṇaṃ … nīlakasiṇaṃ… pītakasiṇaṃ… lauhitakasiṇaṃ… audātakasiṇaṃ… ākāsakasiṇaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

kaisaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰…  laumaṃ aitaṃ mama… nakhaṃ aitaṃ mama… dantaṃ aitaṃ mama… tacaṃ aitaṃ mama… maṃsaṃ aitaṃ mama… nhāruṃ aitaṃ mama… aṭṭhiṃ aitaṃ mama… aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aitaṃ mama… vak‍kaṃ aitaṃ mama… hadayaṃ aitaṃ mama… yakanaṃ aitaṃ mama… kilaumakaṃ aitaṃ mama… pihakaṃ aitaṃ mama… papphāsaṃ aitaṃ mama… antaṃ aitaṃ mama… antaguṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… udariyaṃ aitaṃ mama… karīsaṃ aitaṃ mama… pittaṃ aitaṃ mama… saimhaṃ aitaṃ mama… pubbaṃ aitaṃ mama  … lauhitaṃ aitaṃ mama… saidaṃ aitaṃ mama… maidaṃ aitaṃ mama… assuṃ aitaṃ mama… vasaṃ aitaṃ mama … khaiḷaṃ aitaṃ mama… siṅghāṇikaṃ aitaṃ mama… lasikaṃ aitaṃ mama… muttaṃ aitaṃ mama… matthaluṅgaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

cakkhāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… rūpāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… sautāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… saddāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… rasāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… manāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

cakkhudhātuṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… rūpadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… sautadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… saddadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… gandhadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhādhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… rasadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… phauṭṭhabbadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… manaudhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… dhammadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau  diṭṭhi|

cakkhundriyaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sautindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… ghānindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… jivhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… kāyindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… manindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… jīvitindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… itthindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… purisindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… sukhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… dukkhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… saumanassindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… daumanassindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… upaikkhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… saddhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… vīriyindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… satindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… samādhindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

kāmadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… rūpadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… arūpadhātuṃ aitaṃ mama… kāmabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… rūpabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… arūpabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… sañ‍ñābhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… asañ‍ñābhavaṃ  aitaṃ mama… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… aikavaukārabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… catuvaukārabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… pañ‍cavaukārabhavaṃ aitaṃ mama… paṭhamajjhānaṃ aitaṃ mama… dutiyajjhānaṃ aitaṃ mama… tatiyajjhānaṃ aitaṃ mama… catutthajjhānaṃ aitaṃ mama… maittaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ aitaṃ mama… karuṇaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ aitaṃ mama… muditaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ aitaṃ mama… upaikkhaṃ  caitauvimuttiṃ aitaṃ mama… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ aitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ  aitaṃ mama… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ aitaṃ mama… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

avij‍jaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… saṅkhārai aitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitaṃ mama… nāmarūpaṃ aitaṃ mama… saḷāyatanaṃ aitaṃ mama… phassaṃ aitaṃ mama… vaidanaṃ aitaṃ mama… taṇhaṃ aitaṃ mama… upādānaṃ aitaṃ mama… bhavaṃ aitaṃ mama … jātiṃ aitaṃ mama… jarāmaraṇaṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| aivaṃ abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi|

124. katamāni aṭṭha diṭṭhiṭṭhānāni? khandhāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, avij‍jāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, phassaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, sañ‍ñāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, vitak‍kaupi vitak‍kāpi (syā॰) diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, ayaunisau manasikāraupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, pāpamittaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ, parataughausaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ|

khandhā haitu khandhā pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ khandhāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| avij‍jā haitu avij‍jā pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ avij‍jāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| phassau haitu phassau pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ phassaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| sañ‍ñā haitu sañ‍ñā pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ sañ‍ñāpi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| vitak‍kau haitu vitak‍kau pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya, samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ vitak‍kaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| ayaunisau manasikārau haitu ayaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ ayaunisau manasikāraupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| pāpamittau haitu pāpamittau pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya, samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ pāpamittaupi diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| parataughausau haitu parataughausau pac‍cayau diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ upādāya samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina – aivaṃ parataughausaupi  diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ| imāni aṭṭha diṭṭhiṭṭhānāni|

125. katamāni aṭṭhārasa diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānāni? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ, diṭṭhigahanaṃ, diṭṭhikantāraṃ, diṭṭhivisūkaṃ, diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ, diṭṭhisañ‍ñaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisal‍laṃ, diṭṭhisambādhau, diṭṭhipalibaudhau, diṭṭhibandhanaṃ, diṭṭhipapātau, diṭṭhānusayau, diṭṭhisantāpau, diṭṭhipariḷāhau, diṭṭhiganthau, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, diṭṭhābhinivaisau, diṭṭhiparāmāsau – imāni aṭṭhārasa diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānāni|

126. katamā  sauḷasa diṭṭhiyau?  assādadiṭṭhi, attānudiṭṭhi, micchādiṭṭhi, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, sak‍kāyavatthukā sassatadiṭṭhi, sak‍kāyavatthukā ucchaidadiṭṭhi, antaggāhikādiṭṭhi, pubbantānudiṭṭhi, aparantānudiṭṭhi, sañ‍ñaujanikā diṭṭhi, ahanti mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi, mamanti mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi, attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi, laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi, bhavadiṭṭhi, vibhavadiṭṭhi – imā sauḷasa diṭṭhiyau|

127. katamai tīṇi sataṃ diṭṭhābhinivaisā? assādadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? attānudiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? micchādiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sak‍kāyavatthukāya sassatadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sak‍kāyavatthukāya ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? pubbantānudiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? aparantānudiṭṭhiyā  katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sañ‍ñaujanikāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? ahanti mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? mamanti mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? attavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? bhavadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? vibhavadiṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti?

assādadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍catiṃsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| attānudiṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| micchādiṭṭhiyā dasahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| sak‍kāyavatthukāya sassatadiṭṭhiyā pan‍narasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| sak‍kāyavatthukāya ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| pubbantānudiṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| aparantānudiṭṭhiyā catucattālīsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| sañ‍ñaujanikāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| ahanti mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| mamanti mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| attavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya  diṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya  diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| bhavadiṭṭhiyā aikaina ākāraina aikūnavīsatiyā ākāraina (syā॰) abhinivaisau hauti| vibhavadiṭṭhiyā aikaina ākāraina abhinivaisau hauti|

1. assādadiṭṭhiniddaisau

128. assādadiṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍catiṃsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? yaṃ rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ rūpassa assādauti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na assādau, assādau na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñau assādau| yā ca diṭṭhi yau ca assādau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati assādadiṭṭhi|

assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti| tāya diṭṭhivipattiyā saman‍nāgatau puggalau diṭṭhivipan‍nau| diṭṭhivipan‍nau puggalau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā| yau diṭṭhiyā rāgau yā diṭṭhi yau rāgau (syā॰), sau na diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na rāgau| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñau rāgau| yā ca diṭṭhi yau ca rāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati diṭṭhirāgau| tāya ca diṭṭhiyā taina ca rāgaina saman‍nāgatau puggalau diṭṭhirāgarattau| diṭṭhirāgarattai puggalai din‍naṃ dānaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi|

micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa dvaiva gatiyau – nirayau vā tiracchānayauni vā| micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa yañ‍caiva  kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai  tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā| saiyyathāpi nimbabījaṃ vā kausātakībījaṃ vā tittakālābubījaṃ vā al‍lāya pathaviyā nikkhittaṃ yaṃ caiva pathavirasaṃ upādiyati, yañ‍ca āpaurasaṃ  upādiyati, sabbaṃ taṃ tittakattāya kaṭukattāya asātattāya asāratāya (syā॰) saṃvattati| taṃ kissa haitu? bījaṃ hissa pāpikaṃ| aivamaivaṃ micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi|

micchādiṭṭhi  diṭṭhigataṃ, diṭṭhigahanaṃ, diṭṭhikantāraṃ, diṭṭhivisūkaṃ, diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ, diṭṭhisañ‍ñaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisal‍laṃ, diṭṭhisambādhau, diṭṭhipalibaudhau, diṭṭhibandhanaṃ, diṭṭhipapātau, diṭṭhānusayau, diṭṭhisantāpau, diṭṭhipariḷāhau, diṭṭhiganthau, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, diṭṭhābhinivaisau, diṭṭhiparāmāsau – imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi pariyuṭṭhitacittassa sañ‍ñaugau|

129. atthi sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca, atthi sañ‍ñaujanāni na ca diṭṭhiyau| katamāni sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, sīlabbataparāmāsau – imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca| katamāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau? kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, mānasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ, bhavarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, issāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ, macchariyasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, anunayasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, avij‍jāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ – imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ saṅkhārai yai saṅkhārai (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.28 passitabbā paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ cakkhuṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ sautaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ ghānaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ jivhaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ manaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ rūpai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ saddai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ gandhai paṭic‍ca … yaṃ rasai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ phauṭṭhabbai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ dhammai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca … yaṃ cakkhusamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ sautasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ ghānasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ jivhāsamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ kāyasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ manausamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ sautasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ ghānasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ jivhāsamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ manausamphassajāya vaidanāya assādauti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na assādau, assādau na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñau assādau| yā ca diṭṭhi yau ca assādau – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati assādadiṭṭhi|

assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti| tāya diṭṭhivipattiyā saman‍nāgatau puggalau diṭṭhivipan‍nau| diṭṭhivipan‍nau puggalau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā| yau diṭṭhiyā rāgau, sau na diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na rāgau| añ‍ñā  diṭṭhi, añ‍ñau rāgau| yā ca diṭṭhi yau ca rāgau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati diṭṭhirāgau| tāya ca diṭṭhiyā taina ca rāgaina saman‍nāgatau puggalau diṭṭhirāgarattau| diṭṭhirāgarattai puggalai  din‍naṃ dānaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi|

micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa dvaiva gatiyau – nirayau vā tiracchānayauni vā| micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā| saiyyathāpi nimbabījaṃ vā kausātakībījaṃ vā tittakālābubījaṃ vā al‍lāya pathaviyā nikkhittaṃ yañ‍caiva pathavirasaṃ upādiyati yañ‍ca āpaurasaṃ upādiyati, sabbaṃ taṃ tittakattāya kaṭukattāya asātattāya saṃvattati| taṃ kissa haitu? bījaṃ hissa pāpikaṃ| aivamaivaṃ micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa yañ‍caiva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ yañ‍ca vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… yañ‍ca manaukammaṃ yathādiṭṭhi samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ, yā ca caitanā yā ca patthanā yau ca paṇidhi yai ca saṅkhārā, sabbai tai dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya  ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhi hissa pāpikā assādadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi|

micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhābhinivaisau diṭṭhiparāmāsau  – imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi pariyuṭṭhitacittassa sañ‍ñaugau|

atthi  sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca, atthi sañ‍ñaujanāni na ca diṭṭhiyau| katamāni sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, sīlabbataparāmāsau – imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni caiva diṭṭhiyau ca| katamāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau? kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, mānasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ, bhavarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, issāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ, macchariyasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, anunayasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, avij‍jāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ – imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| assādadiṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍catiṃsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

assādadiṭṭhiniddaisau paṭhamau|

2. attānudiṭṭhiniddaisau

130. attānudiṭṭhiyā  katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ|

131. kathaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau pathavīkasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati – ‘‘yaṃ pathavīkasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ pathavīkasiṇa’’nti| pathavīkasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi  tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci sau  vaṇṇau, yau vaṇṇau sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau pathavīkasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati – ‘‘yaṃ pathavīkasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ pathavīkasiṇa’’nti| pathavīkasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā rūpavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa dvaiva gatiyau…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

idhaikac‍cau āpaukasiṇaṃ… taijaukasiṇaṃ… vāyaukasiṇaṃ… nīlakasiṇaṃ… pītakasiṇaṃ… lauhitakasiṇaṃ… audātakasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati – ‘‘yaṃ audātakasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ audātakasiṇa’’nti| audātakasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci, sau vaṇṇau; yau vaṇṇau, sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaiva idhaikac‍cau…pai॰… audātakasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā rūpavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ  rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā iminā rūpaina rūpavā’’ti| rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā| añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau imāya chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana ayaṃ attā iminā rūpaina rūpavā’’ti| rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dutiyā rūpavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ  attani rūpaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani idaṃ rūpa’’nti| attani rūpaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi pupphaṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ pupphaṃ, ayaṃ gandhau; añ‍ñaṃ pupphaṃ, añ‍ñau gandhau| sau khau panāyaṃ gandhau imasmiṃ pupphai’’ti| pupphasmiṃ gandhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani idaṃ rūpa’’nti| attani rūpaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ tatiyā rūpavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ attani rūpaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imasmiṃ rūpai’’ti| rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi maṇi karaṇḍakai pakkhittau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ maṇi, ayaṃ karaṇḍakau| añ‍ñau maṇi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍakau| sau khau panāyaṃ maṇi imasmiṃ karaṇḍakai’’ti| karaṇḍakasmiṃ maṇiṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imasmiṃ rūpai’’ti| rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ catutthā rūpavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na  ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

132. kathaṃ vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ sautasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ ghānasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ jivhāsamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yā manausamphassajā  vaidanā sau ahaṃ, yau ahaṃ sā manausamphassajā vaidanā’’ti – manausamphassajaṃ vaidanañ‍ca attañ‍ca  advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci sau vaṇṇau, yau vaṇṇau sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yā manausamphassajā vaidanā sau ahaṃ, yau ahaṃ sā manausamphassajā vaidanā’’ti – manausamphassajaṃ vaidanañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā vaidanāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ vaidanāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya vaidanāya vaidanāvā’’ti| vaidanāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau  chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā| añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau imāya chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya vaidanāya vaidanāvā’’ti| vaidanāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dutiyā vaidanāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi  diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ vaidanāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ attani vaidanaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani ayaṃ vaidanā’’ti| attani vaidanaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi pupphaṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ pupphaṃ, ayaṃ gandhau; añ‍ñaṃ pupphaṃ, añ‍ñau gandhau| sau khau panāyaṃ gandhau imasmiṃ pupphai’’ti| pupphasmiṃ gandhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani ayaṃ vaidanā’’ti| attani vaidanaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau  diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ tatiyā vaidanāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ attani vaidanaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ vaidanāya attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya vaidanāyā’’ti| vaidanāya attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi maṇi karaṇḍakai pakkhittau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ maṇi, ayaṃ karaṇḍakau| añ‍ñau maṇi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍakau| sau khau panāyaṃ maṇi imasmiṃ karaṇḍakai’’ti| karaṇḍakasmiṃ maṇiṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya vaidanāyā’’ti| vaidanāya attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ catutthā vaidanāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ vaidanāya attānaṃ samanupassati|

133. kathaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau cakkhusamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… sautasamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… ghānasamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… jivhāsamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… kāyasamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| yā manausamphassajā sañ‍ñā sau ahaṃ, yau  ahaṃ  sā manausamphassajā sañ‍ñā’’ti| manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci sau vaṇṇau, yau vaṇṇau sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati – ‘‘yā manausamphassajā sañ‍ñā sau ahaṃ, yau ahaṃ sā manausamphassajā sañ‍ñā’’ti| manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā sañ‍ñāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi  micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni,  na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ sañ‍ñāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai  ayaṃ attā imāya sañ‍ñāya sañ‍ñāvā’’ti| sañ‍ñāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā| añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau imāya chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya sañ‍ñāya sañ‍ñāvā’’ti| sañ‍ñāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dutiyā sañ‍ñāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ attani sañ‍ñaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani ayaṃ sañ‍ñā’’ti| attani sañ‍ñaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi pupphaṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ pupphaṃ, ayaṃ gandhau| añ‍ñaṃ pupphaṃ, añ‍ñau gandhau| sau khau panāyaṃ gandhau imasmiṃ pupphai’’ti| pupphasmiṃ gandhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani ayaṃ sañ‍ñā’’ti| attani sañ‍ñaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau  diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ tatiyā sañ‍ñāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ attani sañ‍ñaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ sañ‍ñāya attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai  …pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya sañ‍ñāyā’’ti| sañ‍ñāya  attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi maṇi karaṇḍakai pakkhittau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ maṇi, ayaṃ karaṇḍakau| añ‍ñau maṇi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍakau| sau khau panāyaṃ maṇi imasmiṃ karaṇḍakai’’ti| karaṇḍakasmiṃ maṇiṃ samanupassati| aivamaiva idhaikac‍cau saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imāya sañ‍ñāyā’’ti| sañ‍ñāya attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ catutthā sañ‍ñāvatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāya attānaṃ samanupassati|

134. kathaṃ saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau cakkhusamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ sautasamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ ghānasamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ jivhāsamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ manausamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yā manausamphassajā caitanā, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ sā manausamphassajā caitanā’’ti – manausamphassajaṃ caitanañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci sau  vaṇṇau, yau vaṇṇau sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau manausamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yā manausamphassajā caitanā sau ahaṃ, yau ahaṃ sā manausamphassajā caitanā’’ti – manausamphassajaṃ caitanañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā saṅkhāravatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ  saṅkhāravantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imaihi saṅkhāraihi saṅkhāravā’’ti| saṅkhāravantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā| añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau  imāya chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaiva idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā| imaihi saṅkhāraihi saṅkhāravā’’ti| saṅkhāravantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na  diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dutiyā saṅkhāravatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ saṅkhāravantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ  attani saṅkhārai samanupassati?  idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani imai saṅkhārā’’ti| attani saṅkhārai samanupassati| saiyyathāpi pupphaṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ pupphaṃ, ayaṃ gandhau; añ‍ñaṃ pupphaṃ, añ‍ñau gandhau| sau khau panāyaṃ gandhau imasmiṃ pupphai’’ti| pupphasmiṃ gandhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani imai saṅkhārā’’ti| attani saṅkhārai samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ tatiyā saṅkhāravatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ attani saṅkhārai samanupassati|

kathaṃ saṅkhāraisu attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imaisu saṅkhāraisū’’ti| saṅkhāraisu attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi maṇi karaṇḍakai pakkhittau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ maṇi, ayaṃ karaṇḍakau| añ‍ñau maṇi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍakau| sau khau panāyaṃ maṇi imasmiṃ karaṇḍakai’’ti| karaṇḍakasmiṃ maṇiṃ samanupassati| aivamaiva idhaikac‍cau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imaisu saṅkhāraisū’’ti| saṅkhāraisu attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu  – ayaṃ catutthā saṅkhāravatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ saṅkhāraisu attānaṃ samanupassati|

135. kathaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇa’’nti – manauviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci sau vaṇṇau, yau vaṇṇau sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ taṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇa’’nti – manauviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā viñ‍ñāṇavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā iminā viñ‍ñāṇaina viñ‍ñāṇavā’’ti| viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā| añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau imāya chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā iminā viñ‍ñāṇaina viñ‍ñāṇavā’’ti| viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dutiyā viñ‍ñāṇavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi  micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ  attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti| attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi pupphaṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ pupphaṃ, ayaṃ gandhau; añ‍ñaṃ pupphaṃ, añ‍ñau gandhau| sau khau panāyaṃ gandhau imasmiṃ pupphai’’ti| pupphasmiṃ gandhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| imasmiñ‍ca pana attani idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti| attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ tatiyā viñ‍ñāṇavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanupassati|

kathaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇai’’ti| viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ  attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi maṇi karaṇḍakai  pakkhittau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ maṇi, ayaṃ karaṇḍakau| añ‍ñau maṇi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍakau| sau khau panāyaṃ maṇi imasmiṃ karaṇḍakai’’ti| karaṇḍakasmiṃ maṇiṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā imasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇai’’ti| viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ catutthā viñ‍ñāṇavatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| attānudiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| attānudiṭṭhiyā imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

attānudiṭṭhiniddaisau dutiyau|

3. micchādiṭṭhiniddaisau

136. micchādiṭṭhiyā  katamaihi dasahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? ‘‘natthi din‍na’’nti – vatthu vatthuṃ (syā॰) aivamuparipi| aivaṃvādau micchābhinivaisaparāmāsau micchādiṭṭhābhinivaisaparāmāsau (syā॰) diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi  na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā micchāvatthukā micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘natthi yiṭṭha’’nti – vatthu…pai॰… ‘‘natthi huta’’nti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau’’ti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi ayaṃ laukau’’ti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi parau laukau’’ti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi mātā’’ti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi pitā’’ti  – vatthu… ‘‘natthi sattā aupapātikā’’ti – vatthu… ‘‘natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā samaggatā (ka॰) sammāpaṭipan‍nā, yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ, parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’’ti – vatthu| aivaṃvādau micchābhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ dasamā micchāvatthukā micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa dvaiva gatiyau…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| micchādiṭṭhiyā imaihi dasahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

micchādiṭṭhiniddaisau tatiyau|

4. sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiniddaisau

137. sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ|

kathaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau pathavīkasiṇaṃ…pai॰… audātakasiṇaṃ attatau  samanupassati| ‘‘yaṃ audātakasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ audātakasiṇa’’nti – audātakasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau…pai॰… aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau audātakasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi…pai॰… ayaṃ paṭhamā rūpavatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiniddaisau catutthau|

5. sassatadiṭṭhiniddaisau

138. sak‍kāyavatthukāya  sassatadiṭṭhiyā katamaihi pan‍narasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ… saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ|

kathaṃ rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai attā| sau khau pana mai ayaṃ attā iminā rūpaina rūpavā’’ti| rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi rukkhau chāyāsampan‍nau assa| tamainaṃ purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘ayaṃ rukkhau, ayaṃ chāyā; añ‍ñau rukkhau, añ‍ñā chāyā| sau khau panāyaṃ rukkhau imāya  chāyāya chāyāvā’’ti| chāyāvantaṃ rukkhaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau vaidanaṃ…pai॰… ayaṃ paṭhamā sak‍kāyavatthukā sassatadiṭṭhi| sassatadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati…pai॰… sak‍kāyavatthukāya sassatadiṭṭhiyā imaihi pan‍narasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

sassatadiṭṭhiniddaisau pañ‍camau|

6. ucchaidadiṭṭhiniddaisau

139. sak‍kāyavatthukāya  ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati, saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati|

kathaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau  pathavīkasiṇaṃ…pai॰… audātakasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yaṃ audātakasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ audātakasiṇa’’nti – audātakasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau…pai॰… ayaṃ paṭhamā sak‍kāyavatthukā ucchaidadiṭṭhi| ucchaidadiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… sak‍kāyavatthukāya ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

ucchaidadiṭṭhiniddaisau chaṭṭhau|

7. antaggāhikādiṭṭhiniddaisau

140. antaggāhikāya  diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā… ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā… ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā… ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā… ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti… ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti… ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katihākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti?

‘‘sassatau  laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ka] ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ laukau caiva sassataṃ cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi  …pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā laukau caiva sassatā cāti…pai॰… sañ‍ñā laukau caiva sassatā cāti…pai॰… saṅkhārā laukau caiva sassatā cāti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  laukau caiva sassatañ‍cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ pañ‍camī  sassatau laukauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[kha] ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ laukau caiva asassatañ‍cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhivipatti…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā laukau caiva asassatā cāti…pai॰… sañ‍ñā laukau caiva asassatā cāti…pai॰… saṅkhārā laukau caiva asassatā cāti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ laukau caiva asassatañ‍cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi  micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ga] ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idhaikac‍cau parittaṃ aukāsaṃ nīlakatau pharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’’ti| antasañ‍ñī hauti| yaṃ pharati, taṃ vatthu caiva laukau ca| yaina pharati, sau attā caiva laukau cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

idhaikac‍cau  parittaṃ aukāsaṃ pītakatau pharati… lauhitakatau pharati… audātakatau pharati… aubhāsakatau pharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’’ti| antasañ‍ñī hauti| yaṃ pharati taṃ vatthu caiva laukau ca| yaina pharati sau attā caiva laukau cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… antavā  laukauti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[gha] ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi  abhinivaisau hauti? idhaikac‍cau vipulaṃ aukāsaṃ nīlakatau pharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘anantavā ayaṃ laukau apariyantau’’ti| anantasañ‍ñī hauti| yaṃ pharati taṃ vatthu caiva laukau ca; yaina pharati sau attā caiva laukau cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

idhaikac‍cau vipulaṃ aukāsaṃ pītakatau pharati… lauhitakatau pharati… audātakatau pharati… aubhāsakatau pharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘anantavā ayaṃ laukau apariyantau’’ti| anantasañ‍ñī hauti| yaṃ pharati taṃ vatthu caiva laukau ca; yaina pharati sau attā  caiva laukau cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… anantavā laukauti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ṅa] ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ jīvañ‍caiva sarīrañ‍ca; yaṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, yaṃ sarīraṃ taṃ jīvanti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā  ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā  jīvā caiva sarīraṃ ca… sañ‍ñā jīvā caiva sarīraṃ ca… saṅkhārā jīvā caiva sarīraṃ ca… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ jīvañ‍caiva sarīrañ‍ca; yaṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, yaṃ sarīraṃ taṃ jīvanti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ca] ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ sarīraṃ, na jīvaṃ; jīvaṃ na sarīraṃ| añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā sarīraṃ, na jīvaṃ… sañ‍ñā sarīraṃ, na jīvaṃ… saṅkhārā sarīraṃ, na jīvaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sarīraṃ, na jīvaṃ; jīvaṃ na sarīraṃ| añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[cha] ‘‘hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā hautipi tiṭṭhatipi uppaj‍jatipi nibbattatipīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā idhaiva maraṇadhammā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… saṅkhārā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā hautipi tiṭṭhatipi uppaj‍jatipi nibbattatipīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… ‘‘hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’’ti  – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ja] ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgataupi kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati; na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ  paṭhamā ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā idhaiva maraṇadhammā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… saṅkhārā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgataupi kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati| ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[jha] ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā hauti  ca na ca hautīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya  diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā idhaiva maraṇadhammā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… saṅkhārā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā hauti ca na ca hautīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi…pai॰… ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

[ña] ‘‘naiva  hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya  diṭṭhiyā katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? rūpaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā naiva hauti na na hautīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi, añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

vaidanā idhaiva maraṇadhammā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… saṅkhārā idhaiva maraṇadhammā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ idhaiva maraṇadhammaṃ| tathāgatau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā naiva hauti na na hautīti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau diṭṭhi| tāya diṭṭhiyā sau antau gahitauti – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ pañ‍camī ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti  – antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| antaggāhikā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti| antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

antaggāhikādiṭṭhiniddaisau sattamau|

8. pubbantānudiṭṭhiniddaisau

141. pubbantānudiṭṭhiyā  katamaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? cattārau sassatavādā, cattārau aikac‍casassatikā, cattārau antānantikā, cattārau amarāvikkhaipikā, dvai adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā – pubbantānudiṭṭhiyā imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

pubbantānudiṭṭhiniddaisau aṭṭhamau|

9. aparantānudiṭṭhiniddaisau

142. aparantānudiṭṭhiyā katamaihi catucattālīsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sauḷasa sañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha asañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā, satta ucchaidavādā, pañ‍ca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā – aparantānudiṭṭhiyā imaihi catucattālīsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

aparantānudiṭṭhiniddaisau navamau|

10-12. sañ‍ñaujanikādidiṭṭhiniddaisau

143. sañ‍ñaujanikāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhābhinivaisau diṭṭhiparāmāsau – sañ‍ñaujanikāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

144. ‘‘aha’’nti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? cakkhu ahanti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau| ahanti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā  ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ  paṭhamā ‘‘aha’’nti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

sautaṃ  ahanti…pai॰… ghānaṃ ahanti…pai॰… jivhā ahanti…pai॰… kāyau ahanti…pai॰… manau ahanti…pai॰… rūpā ahanti…pai॰… dhammā ahanti… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ahanti…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ahanti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau| ahanti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ aṭṭhārasamī ‘‘aha’’nti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘aha’’nti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

145. ‘‘mama’’nti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? cakkhu mamanti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau| mamanti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā ‘‘mama’’nti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

sautaṃ mamanti…pai॰… ghānaṃ mamanti…pai॰… jivhā mamanti…pai॰… kāyau mamanti…pai॰… manau mamanti…pai॰… rūpā mamanti…pai॰… dhammā mamanti…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ mamanti…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ mamanti abhinivaisaparāmāsau| mamanti  mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na  diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ aṭṭhārasamī ‘‘mama’’nti – mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi| mānavinibandhā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| ‘‘mama’’nti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

sañ‍ñaujanikādidiṭṭhiniddaisau dvādasamau|

13. attavādapaṭisaṃyuttadiṭṭhiniddaisau

146. attavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau  samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ…pai॰… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ…pai॰…|

kathaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati? idhaikac‍cau pathavīkasiṇaṃ…pai॰… audātakasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| ‘‘yaṃ audātakasiṇaṃ, sau ahaṃ; yau ahaṃ, taṃ audātakasiṇa’’nti – audātakasiṇañ‍ca attañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ‘‘yā ac‍ci, sau vaṇṇau; yau vaṇṇau, sā ac‍cī’’ti – ac‍ciñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca advayaṃ samanupassati| aivamaivaṃ idhaikac‍cau audātakasiṇaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰…  ayaṃ paṭhamā rūpavatthukā attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi| attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| aivaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… attavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

attavādapaṭisaṃyuttadiṭṭhiniddaisau tairasamau|

14. laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttadiṭṭhiniddaisau

147. laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā katamaihi aṭṭhahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti? sassatau attā ca laukau cāti – abhinivaisaparāmāsau laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā, diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ paṭhamā laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi| laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau|

asassatau  attā ca laukau cāti…pai॰… sassatau ca asassatau ca attā ca laukau cāti…pai॰… naiva sassatau nāsassatau attā ca laukau cāti… antavā attā ca laukau cāti… anantavā attā ca laukau cāti… antavā ca anantavā ca attā ca laukau cāti… naiva antavā na anantavā attā ca laukau cāti abhinivaisaparāmāsau laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi| diṭṭhi na vatthu, vatthu na diṭṭhi| añ‍ñā diṭṭhi, añ‍ñaṃ vatthu| yā ca diṭṭhi yañ‍ca vatthu – ayaṃ aṭṭhamī laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi| laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhi  …pai॰… imāni sañ‍ñaujanāni, na ca diṭṭhiyau| laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā imaihi aṭṭhahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau hauti|

laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttadiṭṭhiniddaisau cuddasamau|

15-16. bhava-vibhavadiṭṭhiniddaisau

148. aulīyanābhinivaisau  bhavadiṭṭhi| atidhāvanābhinivaisau vibhavadiṭṭhi| assādadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍catiṃsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau kati bhavadiṭṭhiyau, kati vibhavadiṭṭhiyau? attānudiṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau kati bhavadiṭṭhiyau kati vibhavadiṭṭhiyau…pai॰… laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau kati bhavadiṭṭhiyau kati vibhavadiṭṭhiyau?

assādadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍catiṃsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| attānudiṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau pan‍narasa bhavadiṭṭhiyau, pañ‍ca vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| micchādiṭṭhiyā dasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau pan‍narasa bhavadiṭṭhiyau, pañ‍ca vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| sak‍kāyavatthukāya sassatadiṭṭhiyā pan‍narasahi  ākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā bhavadiṭṭhiyau| sak‍kāyavatthukāya ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau|

‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā bhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau  sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā bhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā bhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā pañ‍cahākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau|

pubbantānudiṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| aparantānudiṭṭhiyā catucattārīsāya ākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| sañ‍ñaujanikāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| ahanti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| mamanti – mānavinibandhāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhārasahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau sabbāva tā bhavadiṭṭhiyau| attavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā vīsatiyā ākāraihi abhinivaisau pan‍narasa bhavadiṭṭhiyau, pañ‍ca vibhavadiṭṭhiyau| laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttāya diṭṭhiyā aṭṭhahi ākāraihi abhinivaisau siyā bhavadiṭṭhiyau, siyā vibhavadiṭṭhiyau|

sabbāva tā diṭṭhiyau assādadiṭṭhiyau| sabbāva tā diṭṭhiyau attānudiṭṭhiyau| sabbāva tā diṭṭhiyau micchādiṭṭhiyau| sabbāva  tā diṭṭhiyau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyau| sabbāva tā diṭṭhiyau antaggāhikā diṭṭhiyau| sabbāva tā  diṭṭhiyau sañ‍ñaujanikā diṭṭhiyau| sabbāva tā diṭṭhiyau attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā diṭṭhiyau|

bhavañ‍ca  diṭṭhiṃ vibhavañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ, aitaṃ dvayaṃ tak‍kikā nissitāsai|

taisaṃ niraudhamhi na hatthi ñāṇaṃ, yatthāyaṃ laukau viparītasañ‍ñīti||

149. ‘‘dvīhi, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhigataihi pariyuṭṭhitā daivamanussā aulīyanti auliyanti (syā॰ ka॰) itivu॰ 49 passitabbā aikai, atidhāvanti aikai; cakkhumantau ca passanti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aulīyanti aikai? bhavārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā bhavaratā bhavasammuditā| taisaṃ bhavaniraudhāya dhammai daisiyamānai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aulīyanti aikai|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atidhāvanti aikai? bhavainaiva khau panaikai aṭṭīyamānā aṭṭiyamānā (syā॰ ka॰) itivu॰ 49 passitabbā harāyamānā jigucchamānā vibhavaṃ abhinandanti – ‘‘yatau kira, bhau, ayaṃ attā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ucchij‍jati vinassati na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ aitaṃ yāthāvanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atidhāvanti aikai|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhumantau ca passanti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhūtaṃ bhūtatau passati| bhūtaṃ bhūtatau disvā bhūtassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhumantau ca  passanti|

‘‘yau ayaṃ gāthā itivu॰ 49 dissati bhūtaṃ bhūtatau disvā, bhūtassa ca atik‍kamaṃ|

yathābhūtaidhimuc‍cati, bhavataṇhā parikkhayā||

‘‘sa vai bhūtapariñ‍ñātau, vītataṇhau bhavābhavai|

bhūtassa vibhavā bhikkhu, nāgacchati punabbhava’’nti||

150. tayau  puggalā vipan‍nadiṭṭhī, tayau puggalā sampan‍nadiṭṭhī| katamai tayau puggalā vipan‍nadiṭṭhī? titthiyau ca, titthiyasāvakau ca, yau ca micchādiṭṭhikau – imai tayau puggalā vipan‍nadiṭṭhī|

katamai tayau puggalā sampan‍nadiṭṭhī? tathāgatau ca, tathāgatasāvakau ca, yau ca sammādiṭṭhikau – imai tayau puggalā sampan‍nadiṭṭhī|

‘‘kaudhanau  upanāhī ca, pāpamakkhī ca yau narau|

vipan‍nadiṭṭhi māyāvī, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti’’ imai (syā॰) su॰ ni॰ 116 passitabbā||

ak‍kaudhanau anupanāhī, visuddhau amakkhī (syā॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā suddhataṃ gatau|

sampan‍nadiṭṭhi maidhāvī, taṃ jañ‍ñā ariyau itīti||

tissau vipan‍nadiṭṭhiyau, tissau sampan‍nadiṭṭhiyau| katamā tissau vipan‍nadiṭṭhiyau? aitaṃ mamāti  – vipan‍nadiṭṭhi| aisauhamasmīti – vipan‍nadiṭṭhi| aisau mai attāti – vipan‍nadiṭṭhi| imā tissau  vipan‍nadiṭṭhiyau|

katamā tissau sampan‍nadiṭṭhiyau? naitaṃ mamāti – sampan‍nadiṭṭhi| naisauhamasmīti – sampan‍nadiṭṭhi| na maisau attāti – sampan‍nadiṭṭhi| imā tissau sampan‍nadiṭṭhiyau|

aitaṃ mamāti – kā diṭṭhi, kati diṭṭhiyau, katamantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau? aisauhamasmīti – kā diṭṭhi, kati diṭṭhiyau, katamantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau? aisau mai attāti – kā diṭṭhi, kati diṭṭhiyau, katamantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau?

aitaṃ mamāti – pubbantānudiṭṭhi| aṭṭhārasa diṭṭhiyau| pubbantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau| aisauhamasmīti – aparantānudiṭṭhi| catucattārīsaṃ diṭṭhiyau| aparantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau| aisau  mai attāti – vīsativatthukā attānudiṭṭhi| vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippamukhāni sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippamukhaina (syā॰) dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni| pubbantāparantānuggahitā tā diṭṭhiyau|

151. ‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi niṭṭhaṃ gatā, sabbai tai diṭṭhisampan‍nā| taisaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā? sattakkhattuparamassa, kaulaṃkaulassa, aikabījissa, sakadāgāmissa, yau ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai arahā – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā? antarāparinibbāyissa, upahac‍caparinibbāyissa, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, uddhaṃsautassa, akaniṭṭhagāminau – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā|

‘‘yai  kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi niṭṭhaṃ gatā, sabbai tai diṭṭhisampan‍nā| taisaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā|

‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi avaic‍cappasan‍nā, sabbai tai sautāpan‍nā| taisaṃ sautāpan‍nānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā, pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā? sattakkhattuparamassa, kaulaṃkaulassa, aikabījissa, sakadāgāmissa, yau ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai arahā – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā? antarāparinibbāyissa, upahac‍caparinibbāyissa, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyissa, uddhaṃsautassa, akaniṭṭhagāminau – imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhā|

‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, mayi avaic‍cappasan‍nā, sabbai tai sautāpan‍nā| taisaṃ sautāpan‍nānaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha niṭṭhā| imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ idha vihāya niṭṭhāti’’|

bhavavibhavadiṭṭhiniddaisau sauḷasamau|

diṭṭhikathā niṭṭhitā|

3. ānāpānassatikathā

1. gaṇanavāra

152. sauḷasavatthukaṃ  ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ ānāpānasatisamādhiṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) bhāvayatau samādhikāni dvai ñāṇasatāni uppaj‍janti – aṭṭha paripanthai paribandhai (ka॰) ñāṇāni, aṭṭha ca upakārai ñāṇāni, aṭṭhārasa upak‍kilaisai ñāṇāni, tairasa vaudānai ñāṇāni, bāttiṃsa sataukārissa sataukārīsu (syā॰) ñāṇāni, catuvīsati samādhivasaina ñāṇāni, dvaisattati vipassanāvasaina ñāṇāni, aṭṭha nibbidāñāṇāni, aṭṭha nibbidānulaumañāṇāni, aṭṭha nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇāni, aikavīsati vimuttisukhai ñāṇāni|

katamāni aṭṭha paripanthai ñāṇāni, aṭṭha ca upakārai ñāṇāni? kāmacchandau samādhissa paripanthau, naikkhammaṃ samādhissa upakāraṃ| byāpādau samādhissa  paripanthau, abyāpādau samādhissa upakāraṃ| thinamiddhaṃ samādhissa paripanthau, ālaukasañ‍ñā samādhissa upakāraṃ| uddhac‍caṃ samādhissa paripanthau, avikkhaipau samādhissa upakāraṃ| vicikicchā samādhissa paripanthau, dhammavavatthānaṃ samādhissa upakāraṃ| avij‍jā samādhissa paripanthau, ñāṇaṃ samādhissa upakāraṃ| arati samādhissa paripanthau, pāmauj‍jaṃ samādhissa upakāraṃ| sabbaipi akusalā dhammā samādhissa paripanthā, sabbaipi kusalā dhammā samādhissa upakārā| imāni aṭṭha paripanthai  ñāṇāni, aṭṭha ca upakārai ñāṇāni|

gaṇanavārau paṭhamau|

2. sauḷasañāṇaniddaisau

153. imaihi  sauḷasahi ākāraihi uducittaṃ cittaṃ samuducitaṃ cittaṃ aikattai santiṭṭhati, nīvaraṇaihi visujjhati| katamai  tai aikattā? naikkhammaṃ aikattaṃ, abyāpādau aikattaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñā aikattaṃ, avikkhaipau aikattaṃ, dhammavavatthānaṃ aikattaṃ, ñāṇaṃ aikattaṃ, pāmauj‍jaṃ aikattaṃ, sabbaipi kusalā dhammā aikattā|

nīvaraṇāti, katamai tai nīvaraṇā? kāmacchandau nīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādau nīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ nīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchā nīvaraṇaṃ, avij‍jā nīvaraṇaṃ, arati nīvaraṇaṃ, sabbaipi akusalā dhammā nīvaraṇā|
