nīvaraṇāti, kainaṭṭhaina nīvaraṇā? niyyānāvaraṇaṭṭhaina nīvaraṇā| katamai tai niyyānā? naikkhammaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca naikkhammaina ariyā niyyanti| kāmacchandau niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| taina ca kāmacchandaina nivutattā naikkhammaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – kāmacchandau niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| abyāpādau ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca abyāpādaina ariyā niyyanti| byāpādau niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| taina ca byāpādaina nivutattā abyāpādaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – byāpādau niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| ālaukasañ‍ñā ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| tāya ca ālaukasañ‍ñāya ariyā niyyanti| thinamiddhaṃ niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| taina ca thinamiddhaina nivutattā ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – thinamiddhaṃ niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| avikkhaipau ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca avikkhaipaina ariyā niyyanti| uddhac‍caṃ niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| taina ca uddhac‍caina nivutattā avikkhaipaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – uddhac‍caṃ niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| dhammavavatthānaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca dhammavavatthānaina ariyā niyyanti| vicikicchā niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| tāya ca vicikicchāya nivutattā dhammavavatthānaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – vicikicchā niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| ñāṇaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca ñāṇaina ariyā niyyanti| avij‍jā niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| tāya ca avij‍jāya nivutattā ñāṇaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – avij‍jā niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| pāmauj‍jaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taina ca pāmauj‍jaina ariyā niyyanti| arati niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| tāya ca aratiyā nivutattā pāmauj‍jaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – arati niyyānāvaraṇaṃ| sabbaipi kusalā dhammā ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ| taihi  ca kusalaihi dhammaihi ariyā niyyanti| sabbaipi akusalā dhammā niyyānāvaraṇā| taihi ca akusalaihi dhammaihi nivutattā kusalai dhammai ariyānaṃ niyyānaṃ nappajānātīti – sabbaipi akusalā dhammā niyyānāvaraṇā| imaihi ca pana nīvaraṇaihi visuddhacittassa sauḷasavatthukaṃ  ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ bhāvayatau khaṇikasamaudhānā|

sauḷasañāṇaniddaisau dutiyau|

3. upak‍kilaisañāṇaniddaisau

paṭhamacchak‍kaṃ

154. imaihi ca pana nīvaraṇaihi visuddhacittassa sauḷasavatthukaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ bhāvayatau khaṇikasamaudhānā katamai  aṭṭhārasa upak‍kilaisā uppaj‍janti? assāsādimajjhapariyausānaṃ satiyā anugacchatau ajjhattavikkhaipagataṃ cittaṃ samādhissa paripanthau| passāsādimajjhapariyausānaṃ satiyā anugacchatau bahiddhāvikkhaipagataṃ cittaṃ samādhissa paripanthau| assāsapaṭikaṅkhanā nikanti taṇhācariyā samādhissa paripanthau| passāsapaṭikaṅkhanā nikanti taṇhācariyā samādhissa paripanthau| assāsainābhitun‍nassa passāsapaṭilābhai mucchanā samādhissa paripanthau| passāsainābhitun‍nassa assāsapaṭilābhai mucchanā samādhissa paripanthau|

anugacchanā  ca assāsaṃ, passāsaṃ anugacchanā|

sati ajjhattavikkhaipā, kaṅkhanā bahiddhāvikkhaipapatthanā vikkhaipapanthanā (syā॰)||

assāsainābhitun‍nassa, passāsapaṭilābhai mucchanā|

passāsainābhitun‍nassa, assāsapaṭilābhai mucchanā||

cha aitai upak‍kilaisā, ānāpānassatisamādhissa|

yaihi vikkhippamānassa vikampamānassa (syā॰), nau ca cittaṃ vimuc‍cati|

vimaukkhaṃ appajānantā, tai haunti parapattiyāti||

dutiyacchak‍kaṃ

155. nimittaṃ āvaj‍jatau assāsai cittaṃ vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau| assāsaṃ  āvaj‍jatau nimittai cittaṃ vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau| nimittaṃ āvaj‍jatau passāsai cittaṃ vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau| passāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau nimittai cittaṃ vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau| assāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau passāsai cittaṃ vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau| passāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau assāsai cittaṃ  vikampati – samādhissa paripanthau|

nimittaṃ  āvaj‍jamānassa, assāsai vikkhipatai manau|

assāsaṃ āvaj‍jamānassa, nimittai cittaṃ vikampati||

nimittaṃ āvaj‍jamānassa, passāsai vikkhipatai manau|

passāsaṃ āvaj‍jamānassa, nimittai cittaṃ vikampati||

assāsaṃ āvaj‍jamānassa, passāsai vikkhipatai manau|

passāsaṃ āvaj‍jamānassa, assāsai cittaṃ vikampati||

cha aitai upak‍kilaisā, ānāpānassatisamādhissa|

yaihi vikkhippamānassa, nau ca cittaṃ vimuc‍cati|

vimaukkhaṃ appajānantā, tai haunti parapattiyāti||

tatiyacchak‍kaṃ

156. atītānudhāvanaṃ cittaṃ vikkhaipānupatitaṃ – samādhissa paripanthau| anāgatapaṭikaṅkhanaṃ cittaṃ vikampitaṃ – samādhissa paripanthau| līnaṃ cittaṃ kausaj‍jānupatitaṃ  – samādhissa paripanthau| atipaggahitaṃ cittaṃ uddhac‍cānupatitaṃ – samādhissa paripanthau| abhinataṃ cittaṃ rāgānupatitaṃ – samādhissa paripanthau| apanataṃ cittaṃ byāpādānupatitaṃ – samādhissa paripanthau|

atītānudhāvanaṃ cittaṃ, anāgatapaṭikaṅkhanaṃ līnaṃ|

atipaggahitaṃ abhinataṃ, apanataṃ cittaṃ na samādhiyati||

cha aitai upak‍kilaisā, ānāpānassatisamādhissa|

yaihi  upak‍kiliṭṭhasaṅkappau, adhicittaṃ nappajānātīti||

157. assāsādimajjhapariyausānaṃ  satiyā anugacchatau ajjhattaṃ vikkhaipagataina cittaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| passāsādimajjhapariyausānaṃ satiyā anugacchatau bahiddhāvikkhaipagataina cittaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| assāsapaṭikaṅkhanāya nikantiyā taṇhācariyāya kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| passāsapaṭikaṅkhanāya nikantiyā taṇhācariyāya kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā  ca| assāsainābhitun‍nassa passāsapaṭilābhai mucchitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| passāsainābhitun‍nassa assāsapaṭilābhai mucchitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| nimittaṃ āvaj‍jatau assāsai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| assāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau nimittai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| nimittaṃ āvaj‍jatau passāsai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| passāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau nimittai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā  ca phanditā ca| assāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau passāsai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| passāsaṃ āvaj‍jatau assāsai cittaṃ vikampitattā kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| atītānudhāvanaina cittaina vikkhaipānupatitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| anāgatapaṭikaṅkhanaina cittaina vikampitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| līnaina cittaina  kausaj‍jānupatitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| atipaggahitaina cittaina uddhac‍cānupatitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| abhinataina cittaina rāgānupatitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca| apanataina cittaina byāpādānupatitaina kāyaupi cittampi sāraddhā ca haunti iñ‍jitā ca phanditā ca|

ānāpānassati yassa, paripuṇṇā abhāvitā|

kāyaupi iñ‍jitau hauti, cittampi hauti iñ‍jitaṃ|

kāyaupi phanditau hauti, cittampi hauti phanditaṃ||

ānāpānassati  yassa, paripuṇṇā subhāvitā|

kāyaupi aniñ‍jitau hauti, cittampi hauti aniñ‍jitaṃ|

kāyaupi  aphanditau hauti, cittampi hauti aphanditanti||

imaihi ca pana nīvaraṇaihi visuddhacittassa sauḷasavatthukaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ bhāvayatau khaṇikasamaudhānā imai aṭṭhārasa upak‍kilaisā uppaj‍janti|

upak‍kilaisañāṇaniddaisau tatiyau|

4. vaudānañāṇaniddaisau

158. katamāni tairasa vaudānai ñāṇāni? atītānudhāvanaṃ cittaṃ vikkhaipānupatitaṃ; taṃ vivaj‍jayitvā aikaṭṭhānai samādahati – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| anāgatapaṭikaṅkhanaṃ cittaṃ vikampitaṃ; taṃ vivaj‍jayitvā tatthaiva adhimaucaiti – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| līnaṃ cittaṃ kausaj‍jānupatitaṃ; taṃ paggaṇhitvā kausaj‍jaṃ pajahati – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| atipaggahitaṃ cittaṃ uddhac‍cānupatitaṃ; taṃ viniggaṇhitvā uddhac‍caṃ pajahati – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| abhinataṃ cittaṃ rāgānupatitaṃ; taṃ sampajānau hutvā rāgaṃ pajahati – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| apanataṃ cittaṃ byāpādānupatitaṃ; taṃ sampajānau hutvā byāpādaṃ pajahati – aivampi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati| imaihi chahi ṭhānaihi parisuddhaṃ cittaṃ pariyaudātaṃ aikattagataṃ hauti|

katamai  tai aikattā? dānavausaggupaṭṭhānaikattaṃ, samathanimittupaṭṭhānaikattaṃ, vayalakkhaṇupaṭṭhānaikattaṃ, niraudhupaṭṭhānaikattaṃ| dānavausaggupaṭṭhānaikattaṃ cāgādhimuttānaṃ, samathanimittupaṭṭhānaikattañ‍ca  adhicittamanuyuttānaṃ, vayalakkhaṇupaṭṭhānaikattañ‍ca vipassakānaṃ, niraudhupaṭṭhānaikattañ‍ca ariyapuggalānaṃ – imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi aikattagataṃ cittaṃ paṭipadāvisuddhipakkhandañ‍caiva hauti, upaikkhānubrūhitañ‍ca, ñāṇaina ca sampahaṃsitaṃ|

paṭhamassa jhānassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? paṭhamassa jhānassa paṭipadāvisuddhi ādi, upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai, sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| paṭhamassa jhānassa paṭipadāvisuddhi  ādi| ādissa kati lakkhaṇāni? ādissa tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| yau tassa paripanthau tatau cittaṃ visujjhati, visuddhattā cittaṃ majjhimaṃ samathanimittaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, paṭipan‍nattā tattha cittaṃ pakkhandati| yañ‍ca paripanthatau cittaṃ visujjhati, yañ‍ca visuddhattā cittaṃ majjhimaṃ samathanimittaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, yañ‍ca paṭipan‍nattā tattha cittaṃ pakkhandati| paṭhamassa  jhānassa paṭipadāvisuddhi ādi| ādissa imāni tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ ādikalyāṇañ‍caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nañ‍ca’’|

paṭhamassa jhānassa upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai| majjhassa kati lakkhaṇāni? majjhassa tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| visuddhaṃ cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, samathapaṭipan‍naṃ ajjhupaikkhati, aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| yañ‍ca visuddhaṃ cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, yañ‍ca samathapaṭipan‍naṃ  ajjhupaikkhati, yañ‍ca aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhati – paṭhamassa jhānassa upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai| majjhassa imāni tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ majjhaikalyāṇañ‍caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nañ‍ca’’|

paṭhamassa jhānassa sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| pariyausānassa kati lakkhaṇāni? pariyausānassa cattāri lakkhaṇāni| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā| paṭhamassa jhānassa sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| pariyausānassa imāni cattāri lakkhaṇāni| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇañ‍caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nañ‍ca’’| aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ vitak‍kasampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti vicārasampan‍nañ‍ca pītisampan‍nañ‍ca sukhasampan‍nañ‍ca cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

dutiyassa  jhānassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? dutiyassa jhānassa paṭipadāvisuddhi ādi, upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai, sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ pītisampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti sukhasampan‍nañ‍ca cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca  vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

tatiyassa  jhānassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ sukhasampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

catutthassa jhānassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍nañ‍ca upaikkhāsampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ upaikkhāsampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍naṃ ca…pai॰… pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

anic‍cānupassanāya kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ  vitak‍kasampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti vicārasampan‍nañ‍ca pītisampan‍nañ‍ca sukhasampan‍nañ‍ca cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca| dukkhānupassanāya…pai॰… anattānupassanāya… nibbidānupassanāya… virāgānupassanāya… niraudhānupassanāya… paṭinissaggānupassanāya… khayānupassanāya… vayānupassanāya… vipariṇāmānupassanāya… animittānupassanāya… appaṇihitānupassanāya… suñ‍ñatānupassanāya… adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya… yathābhūtañāṇadassanāya… ādīnavānupassanāya… paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya… vivaṭṭanānupassanāya… pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

sautāpattimaggassa…pai॰… sakadāgāmimaggassa… anāgāmimaggassa… arahattamaggassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? arahattamaggassa paṭipadāvisuddhi  ādi upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai, sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| arahattamaggassa paṭipadāvisuddhi ādi| ādissa kati lakkhaṇāni? ādissa tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| yau tassa paripanthau tatau cittaṃ visujjhati, visuddhattā cittaṃ majjhimaṃ samathanimittaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, paṭipan‍nattā tattha cittaṃ pakkhandati| yañ‍ca paripanthatau cittaṃ visujjhati, yañ‍ca visuddhattā cittaṃ majjhimaṃ samathanimittaṃ  paṭipaj‍jati, yañ‍ca paṭipan‍nattā tattha cittaṃ pakkhandati – arahattamaggassa paṭipadāvisuddhi ādi| ādissa imāni tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| taina  vuc‍cati – ‘‘arahattamaggau ādikalyāṇau caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nau ca’’|

arahattamaggassa upaikkhānubrūhanā majjhai| majjhassa kati lakkhaṇāni? majjhassa tīṇi lakkhaṇāni| visuddhaṃ cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, samathapaṭipan‍naṃ ajjhupaikkhati, aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| yañ‍ca visuddhaṃ cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, yañ‍ca samathapaṭipan‍naṃ ajjhupaikkhati, yañ‍ca aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘arahattamaggau majjhaikalyāṇau caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nau ca’’|

arahattamaggassa sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| pariyausānassa kati lakkhaṇāni? pariyausānassa cattāri lakkhaṇāni| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina sampahaṃsanā| arahattamaggassa sampahaṃsanā pariyausānaṃ| pariyausānassa imāni cattāri lakkhaṇāni| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘arahattamaggau pariyausānakalyāṇau caiva hauti lakkhaṇasampan‍nau ca’’| aivaṃ tivattagataṃ cittaṃ tividhakalyāṇakaṃ dasalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ vitak‍kasampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti vicārasampan‍nañ‍ca pītisampan‍nañ‍ca sukhasampan‍nañ‍ca cittassa adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nañ‍ca saddhāsampan‍nañ‍ca vīriyasampan‍nañ‍ca satisampan‍nañ‍ca samādhisampan‍nañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nañ‍ca|

159.

 nimittaṃ assāsapassāsā, anārammaṇamaikacittassa|

ajānatau ca tayau dhammai, bhāvanā nupalabbhati||

nimittaṃ  assāsapassāsā, anārammaṇamaikacittassa|

jānatau ca tayau dhammai, bhāvanā upalabbhatīti||

kathaṃ imai tayau dhammā aikacittassa ārammaṇā na haunti, na cimai tayau dhammā aviditā haunti, na ca cittaṃ vikkhaipaṃ gacchati, padhānañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyati, payaugañ‍ca sādhaiti, visaisamadhigacchati? saiyyathāpi rukkhau  samai bhūmibhāgai nikkhittau| tamainaṃ purisau kakacaina chindaiyya| rukkhai phuṭṭhakakacadantānaṃ vasaina purisassa sati upaṭṭhitā hauti; na āgatai vā gatai vā kakacadantai manasi karauti| na āgatā vā gatā vā kakacadantā aviditā haunti, padhānañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyati, payaugañ‍ca sādhaiti| yathā rukkhau samai bhūmibhāgai nikkhittau| aivaṃ upanibandhanā nimittaṃ| yathā kakacadantā, aivaṃ assāsapassāsā| yathā rukkhai phuṭṭhakakacadantānaṃ vasaina purisassa sati upaṭṭhitā hauti, na āgatai vā gatai vā kakacadantai manasi karauti, na āgatā vā gatā vā kakacadantā aviditā haunti, padhānañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyati, payaugañ‍ca sādhaiti| aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu nāsikaggai vā mukhanimittai vā satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā nisin‍nau hauti, na āgatai vā  gatai vā assāsapassāsai manasi karauti, na āgatā vā gatā vā assāsapassāsā aviditā haunti, padhānañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyati, payaugañ‍ca sādhaiti| visaisamadhigacchati padhānañ‍ca|

katamaṃ padhānaṃ? āraddhavīriyassa kāyaupi cittampi kammaniyaṃ hauti – idaṃ padhānaṃ| katamau payaugau? āraddhavīriyassa upak‍kilaisā pahīyanti, vitak‍kā vūpasamanti – ayaṃ payaugau| katamau visaisau? āraddhavīriyassa sañ‍ñaujanā pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti anusayā byāsanti (syā॰)| ayaṃ visaisau| aivaṃ imai tayau dhammā aikacittassa ārammaṇā na haunti, na cimai tayau dhammā aviditā haunti, na ca cittaṃ vikkhaipaṃ gacchati, padhānañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyati, payaugañ‍ca sādhaiti, visaisamadhigacchati|

160.

 ānāpānassati yassa, paripuṇṇā subhāvitā|

anupubbaṃ paricitā, yathā buddhaina daisitā|

sau imaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimāti||

ānanti assāsau, nau passāsau| apānanti apānanti (ka॰) passāsau, nau assāsau| assāsavasaina upaṭṭhānaṃ sati, passāsavasaina upaṭṭhānaṃ sati|

yau assasati tassupaṭṭhāti, yau passasati tassupaṭṭhāti| paripuṇṇāti pariggahaṭṭhaina paripuṇṇā, parivāraṭṭhaina paripuṇṇā, paripūraṭṭhaina paripuṇṇā| subhāvitāti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina  bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| tassimai cattārau bhāvanaṭṭhā yānīkatā haunti vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā|

yānīkatāti yattha yattha ākaṅkhati tattha tattha vasippattau hauti balappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau| tassa mai tai dhammā āvaj‍janapaṭibaddhā āvaj‍janapaṭibandhā (ka॰) aivamīdisaisu padaisu haunti ākaṅkhapaṭibaddhā manasikārapaṭibaddhā cittuppādapaṭibaddhā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘yānīkatā’’ti| vatthukatāti yasmiṃ yasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ cittaṃ svādhiṭṭhitaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ sati supaṭṭhitā hauti| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ vā pana vatthusmiṃ sati sūpaṭṭhitā supaṭṭhitā (ka॰) hauti, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ cittaṃ svādhiṭṭhitaṃ hauti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vatthukatā’’ti| anuṭṭhitāti vatthusmiṃ yaina yaina cittaṃ abhinīharati taina taina sati anuparivattati| yaina yaina vā pana sati anuparivattati taina taina cittaṃ abhinīharati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘anuṭṭhitā’’ti| paricitāti pariggahaṭṭhaina paricitā, parivāraṭṭhaina paricitā, paripūraṭṭhaina paricitā| satiyā pariggaṇhantau jināti  pāpakai akusalai dhammai| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paricitā’’ti| susamāraddhāti  cattārau susamāraddhā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina susamāraddhā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina susamāraddhā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina susamāraddhā, tappac‍canīkānaṃ kilaisānaṃ susamūhatattā susamuhatattā (ka॰) susamāraddhā|

161. susamanti atthi samaṃ, atthi susamaṃ| katamaṃ samaṃ? yai tattha jātā anavaj‍jā kusalā baudhipakkhiyā – idaṃ samaṃ| katamaṃ susamaṃ? yaṃ taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ niraudhau nibbānaṃ – idaṃ susamaṃ| iti idañ‍ca samaṃ idañ‍ca susamaṃ ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammūḷā apamuṭṭhā (syā॰), passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘susamāraddhā’’ti|

anupubbaṃ paricitāti dīghaṃ assāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā| rassaṃ assāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā| rassaṃ passāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā| paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina purimā purimā paricitā, pacchimā pacchimā anuparicitā| sabbāpi sauḷasavatthukā ānāpānassatiyau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paricitā  caiva haunti anuparicitā ca| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘anupubbaparicitā’’ti|

yathāti dasa yathatthā – attadamathatthau yathatthau, attasamathatthau yathatthau, attaparinibbāpanatthau yathatthau, abhiñ‍ñatthau yathatthau, pariñ‍ñatthau yathatthau, pahānatthau yathatthau, bhāvanatthau yathatthau, sacchikiriyatthau  yathatthau, sac‍cābhisamayatthau yathatthau, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpakatthau yathatthau|

buddhauti  yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhi, tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇi, balaisu ca vasībhāvaṃ|

162. buddhauti kainaṭṭhaina buddhau? bujjhitā sac‍cānīti – buddhau| baudhaitā pajāyāti – buddhau| sabbañ‍ñutāya buddhau| sabbadassāvitāya buddhau| anañ‍ñanaiyyatāya buddhau| visavitāya vikatitāya (syā॰) buddhau| khīṇāsavasaṅkhātaina buddhau| nirupalaipasaṅkhātaina nirupak‍kilaisasaṅkhātaina (syā॰) buddhau| aikantavītarāgauti – buddhau| aikantavītadausauti – buddhau| aikantavītamauhauti – buddhau| aikantanik‍kilaisauti – buddhau| aikāyanamaggaṃ gatauti – buddhau| aikau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti – buddhau| abuddhivihatattā buddhipaṭilābhā – buddhau| buddhauti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi  kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ| vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ – buddhauti| daisitāti attadamathatthau yathatthau yathā buddhaina daisitau, attasamathatthau yathatthau yathā buddhaina daisitau, attaparinibbāpanatthau yathatthau yathā buddhaina daisitau…pai॰… niraudhai patiṭṭhāpakatthau yathatthau yathā buddhaina daisitau|

sauti  gahaṭṭhau vā hauti pabbajitau vā| laukauti| khandhalaukau dhātulaukau āyatanalaukau vipattibhavalaukau vipattisambhavalaukau sampattibhavalaukau sampattisambhavalaukau| aikau laukau – sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā…pai॰… aṭṭhārasa laukā – aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau| pabhāsaitīti attadamathatthaṃ yathatthaṃ abhisambuddhattā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ aubhāsaiti bhāsaiti pabhāsaiti| attasamathatthaṃ yathatthaṃ abhisambuddhattā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ aubhāsaiti bhāsaiti pabhāsaiti| attaparinibbāpanatthaṃ yathatthaṃ abhisambuddhattā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ aubhāsaiti bhāsaiti pabhāsaiti…pai॰… niraudhai patiṭṭhāpakatthaṃ yathatthaṃ abhisambuddhattā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ aubhāsaiti bhāsaiti pabhāsaiti|

abbhā muttauva candimāti yathā abbhā, aivaṃ kilaisā| yathā candau, aivaṃ ariyañāṇaṃ| yathā candimā daivaputtau, aivaṃ bhikkhu| yathā  candau abbhā muttau mahikā muttau dhūmarajā muttau rāhugahaṇā rāhupāṇā (syā॰) vippamuttau bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucatai viraucati (syā॰) ca, aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu sabbakilaisaihi vippamuttau  bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucatai ca| taina vuc‍cati – abbhā muttauva candimāti| imāni tairasa vaudānai ñāṇāni|

vaudānañāṇaniddaisau catutthau|

bhāṇavārau|

5. sataukāriñāṇaniddaisau

163. katamāni bāttiṃsa sataukārissa ñāṇāni? idha bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati satau passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’’ti pajānāti| dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’’ti pajānāti| rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘‘rassaṃ assasāmī’’ti pajānāti| rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘‘rassaṃ passasāmī’’ti pajānāti| ‘‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘passambhayaṃ  kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| pītipaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī … passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ… samādahaṃ cittaṃ… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… anic‍cānupassī… virāgānupassī… niraudhānupassī… ‘‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati|

164. idhāti imissā diṭṭhiyā, imissā khantiyā, imissā ruciyā, imasmiṃ ādāyai, imasmiṃ dhammai, imasmiṃ vinayai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, imasmiṃ pāvacanai, imasmiṃ brahmacariyai, imasmiṃ satthusāsanai| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘idhā’’ti| bhikkhūti  puthuj‍janakalyāṇakau vā hauti bhikkhu saikkhau vā arahā vā akuppadhammau| arañ‍ñanti nikkhamitvā bahi indakhīlā sabbamaitaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ| rukkhamūlanti yattha bhikkhunau āsanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti mañ‍cau vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisi vā taṭṭikā vā cammakhaṇḍau vā tiṇasantharau tiṇasaṇṭharau (syā॰) vā paṇṇasantharau vā palālasantharau palāsasaṇṭharau (syā॰) vā, tattha bhikkhu caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| suñ‍ñanti kainaci anākiṇṇaṃ hauti gahaṭṭhaihi vā pabbajitaihi vā| agāranti āgāranti (syā॰) vihārau aḍḍhayaugau pāsādau hammiyaṃ guhā| nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvāti nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā| ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāyāti ujukau hauti kāyau ṭhitau panidhitau (syā॰) supaṇihitau| parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvāti| parīti pariggahaṭṭhau| mukhanti niyyānaṭṭhau| satīti upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā’’ti|

165. satauva assasati, satau passasatīti bāttiṃsāya ākāraihi  satau kārī hauti| dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā  hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti| rassaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti| rassaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ  avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti|

paṭhamacatuk‍kaniddaisau

166. kathaṃ dīghaṃ assasantau ‘‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ passasantau ‘‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’’ti pajānāti? dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati, dīghaṃ passāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai passasati, dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi chandau uppaj‍jati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati, chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ passāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai passasati, chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati, pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ passāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai  passasati, pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi dīghaṃ assāsapassāsāpi cittaṃ vivattati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| imaihi navahākāraihi dīghaṃ assāsapassāsā kāyau| upaṭṭhānaṃ sati| anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

167. anupassatīti  kathaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati? anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau| dukkhatau anupassati, nau sukhatau| anattatau anupassati, nau attatau| nibbindati, nau nandati| viraj‍jati, nau raj‍jati| niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti| paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau  anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati| dukkhatau anupassantau sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati| anattatau anupassantau attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati| nibbindantau nandiṃ pajahati| viraj‍jantau rāgaṃ pajahati| niraudhaintau samudayaṃ pajahati| paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| aivaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dīghaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina  cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

kathaṃ viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti? kathaṃ vaidanāya uppādau viditau hauti? avij‍jāsamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti – pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vaidanāya uppādau viditau hauti| taṇhāsamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti… kammasamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti… phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vaidanāya uppādau viditau hauti| nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ passataupi vaidanāya uppādau viditau hauti| aivaṃ vaidanāya uppādau viditau hauti|

kathaṃ vaidanāya upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| aivaṃ vaidanāya upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti|

kathaṃ vaidanāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti? avij‍jāniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti – pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina vaidanāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| taṇhāniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti …pai॰… kammaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti…pai॰… phassaniraudhā  vaidanāniraudhauti pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina vaidanāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ passataupi vaidanāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ vaidanāya  atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

kathaṃ  viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti? kathaṃ sañ‍ñāya uppādau viditau hauti? avij‍jāsamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayauti – pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina sañ‍ñāya uppādau viditau hauti| taṇhāsamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayauti…pai॰… kammasamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayauti…pai॰… phassasamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayauti – pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina sañ‍ñāya uppādau viditau hauti| nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ passataupi sañ‍ñāya uppādau viditau hauti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāya uppādau viditau hauti|

kathaṃ sañ‍ñāya upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāya upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti|

kathaṃ sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti? avij‍jāniraudhā sañ‍ñāniraudhauti – pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| taṇhāniraudhā sañ‍ñāniraudhauti…pai॰… kammaniraudhā sañ‍ñāniraudhauti…pai॰… phassaniraudhā  sañ‍ñāniraudhauti – pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ passataupi sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

kathaṃ viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti? kathaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ uppādau viditau hauti? avij‍jāsamudayā vitak‍kasamudayauti – pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vitak‍kānaṃ uppādau viditau hauti| taṇhāsamudayā vitak‍kasamudayauti…pai॰… kammasamudayā vitak‍kasamudayauti…pai॰… sañ‍ñāsamudayā vitak‍kasamudayauti – pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vitak‍kānaṃ uppādau viditau hauti| nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ passataupi vitak‍kānaṃ uppādau viditau hauti| aivaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ uppādau viditau hauti|

kathaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ  hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatupaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti| aivaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ viditaṃ hauti|

kathaṃ  vitak‍kānaṃ atthaṅgamau viditau hauti? avij‍jāniraudhā vitak‍kaniraudhauti – pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina vitak‍kānaṃ atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| taṇhāniraudhā vitak‍kaniraudhauti…pai॰… kammaniraudhā vitak‍kaniraudhauti…pai॰… sañ‍ñāniraudhā  vitak‍kaniraudhauti – pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina vitak‍kānaṃ atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ passataupi vitak‍kānaṃ atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ atthaṅgamau viditau hauti| aivaṃ viditā  vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

168. dīghaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti, gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti, samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati…pai॰… maggaṃ samaudhānaiti, dhammai samaudhānaiti, gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti, samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati|

indriyāni samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ indriyāni samaudhānaiti? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti| ayaṃ puggalau imāni indriyāni imasmiṃ ārammaṇai samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘indriyāni samaudhānaitī’’ti|

gaucarañ‍ca pajānātīti yaṃ tassa ārammaṇaṃ, taṃ tassa gaucaraṃ| yaṃ tassa gaucaraṃ, taṃ tassa ārammaṇaṃ| pajānātīti puggalau| pajānanā pañ‍ñā|

samanti ārammaṇassa upaṭṭhānaṃ samaṃ, cittassa avikkhaipau samaṃ, cittassa adhiṭṭhānaṃ samaṃ, cittassa vaudānaṃ samaṃ| atthauti  anavaj‍jaṭṭhau nik‍kilaisaṭṭhau vaudānaṭṭhau paramaṭṭhau| paṭivijjhatīti ārammaṇassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa vaudānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

balāni samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ balāni samaudhānaiti? assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhaina saddhābalaṃ samaudhānaiti, kausaj‍jai akampiyaṭṭhaina vīriyabalaṃ samaudhānaiti, pamādai akampiyaṭṭhaina satibalaṃ samaudhānaiti, uddhac‍cai akampiyaṭṭhaina samādhibalaṃ samaudhānaiti, avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñābalaṃ samaudhānaiti| ayaṃ puggalau imāni balāni imasmiṃ ārammaṇai samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – balāni samaudhānaitīti| gaucarañ‍ca pajānātīti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

baujjhaṅgai  samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ baujjhaṅgai samaudhānaiti? upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, pavicayaṭṭhaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, pharaṇaṭṭhaina pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, upasamaṭṭhaina passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ samaudhānaiti, paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhaina upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  samaudhānaiti| ayaṃ puggalau imai baujjhaṅgai imasmiṃ ārammaṇai samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘baujjhaṅgai samaudhānaitī’’ti| gaucarañ‍ca pajānātīti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

maggaṃ samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ maggaṃ samaudhānaiti? dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhiṃ samaudhānaiti, abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappaṃ samaudhānaiti, pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācaṃ samaudhānaiti, samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantaṃ samaudhānaiti, vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvaṃ samaudhānaiti, paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmaṃ samaudhānaiti, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsatiṃ samaudhānaiti, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhiṃ samaudhānaiti| ayaṃ puggalau imaṃ maggaṃ imasmiṃ ārammaṇai samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘maggaṃ samaudhānaitī’’ti| gaucarañ‍ca pajānātīti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

dhammai samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ dhammai samaudhānaiti? ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyāni samaudhānaiti, akampiyaṭṭhaina balāni samaudhānaiti, niyyānaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgai samaudhānaiti, haituṭṭhaina maggaṃ samaudhānaiti, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satipaṭṭhānaṃ samaudhānaiti, padahanaṭṭhaina sammappadhānaṃ samaudhānaiti, ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhipādaṃ samaudhānaiti tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ samaudhānaiti, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samathaṃ samaudhānaiti, anupassanaṭṭhaina  vipassanaṃ samaudhānaiti, aikarasaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ samaudhānaiti, anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhaṃ samaudhānaiti, saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhiṃ samaudhānaiti, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhiṃ samaudhānaiti, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhiṃ samaudhānaiti, vimuttaṭṭhaina vimaukkhaṃ samaudhānaiti, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina vij‍jaṃ samaudhānaiti, paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vimuttiṃ samaudhānaiti, samucchaidaṭṭhaina khayai ñāṇaṃ samaudhānaiti, paṭippassaddhaṭṭhaina anuppādai ñāṇaṃ samaudhānaiti, chandaṃ  mūlaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, manasikāraṃ samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, phassaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, vaidanaṃ samausaraṇaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, samādhiṃ pamukhaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, satiṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, pañ‍ñaṃ tatuttaraṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, vimuttiṃ sāraṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti, amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina samaudhānaiti| ayaṃ puggalau imai dhammai imasmiṃ ārammaṇai samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammai samaudhānaitī’’ti|

gaucarañ‍ca pajānātīti yaṃ tassa ārammaṇaṃ, taṃ tassa gaucaraṃ| yaṃ tassa gaucaraṃ, taṃ tassa ārammaṇaṃ pajānātīti puggalau| pajānanā pañ‍ñā| samanti ārammaṇassa upaṭṭhānaṃ samaṃ, cittassa avikkhaipau samaṃ, cittassa adhiṭṭhānaṃ samaṃ, cittassa vaudānaṃ samaṃ| atthauti anavaj‍jaṭṭhau nik‍kilaisaṭṭhau vaudānaṭṭhau paramaṭṭhau| paṭivijjhatīti  ārammaṇassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati, cittassa vaudānaṭṭhaṃ paṭivijjhati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

169. kathaṃ rassaṃ assasantau ‘‘rassaṃ assasāmī’’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ passasantau ‘‘rassaṃ passasāmī’’ti pajānāti? rassaṃ assāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasati, rassaṃ passāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai passasati, rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi chandau uppaj‍jati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ passāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai passasati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasati, pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ passāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai passasati, pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ rassaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi rassā assāsapassāsā cittaṃ vivattati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| imaihi  navahākāraihi rassā assāsapassāsā  kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau  sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti  kathaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| rassaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… rassaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

170. kathaṃ ‘‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? kāyauti dvai kāyā – nāmakāyau ca rūpakāyau ca| katamau nāmakāyau? vaidanā, sañ‍ñā, caitanā, phassau, manasikārau, nāmañ‍ca nāmakāyau ca, yai ca vuc‍canti cittasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ nāmakāyau| katamau rūpakāyau? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ, assāsau ca passāsau ca, nimittañ‍ca upanibandhanā, yai ca vuc‍canti kāyasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ rūpakāyau|

kathaṃ tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa  aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti| rassaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti| rassaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti|

āvaj‍jatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, pajānatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, passatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, pac‍cavaikkhatau  tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, saddhāya adhimuc‍catau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, satiṃ upaṭṭhāpayatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, cittaṃ samādahatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, pañ‍ñāya pajānatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānatau tai kāyā paṭividitā  haunti, pahātabbaṃ pajahatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvayatau tai kāyā paṭividitā haunti, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarautau tai kāyā paṭividitā  haunti| aivaṃ tai kāyā paṭividitā  haunti| sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsā kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsānaṃ assāsapassāsā (syā॰) saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| yau tattha saṃvaraṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā| yau tattha avikkhaipaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā| yau tattha dassanaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhati, jānantau sikkhati, passantau sikkhati, pac‍cavaikkhantau sikkhati, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahantau sikkhati, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau sikkhati, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau sikkhati, satiṃ upaṭṭhapaintau sikkhati, cittaṃ samādahantau sikkhati, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau sikkhati, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau sikkhati, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau sikkhati, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau sikkhati, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvaintau sikkhati, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau sikkhati|

sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī  assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

171. kathaṃ  ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamau kāyasaṅkhārau? dīghaṃ assāsā kāyikā| aitai dhammā kāyapaṭibaddhā kāyasaṅkhārā| tai kāyasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati| dīghaṃ passāsā kāyikā| aitai dhammā kāyapaṭibaddhā kāyasaṅkhārā| tai kāyasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati| rassaṃ assāsā rassaṃ passāsā| sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsā sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsā kāyikā| aitai dhammā kāyapaṭibaddhā kāyasaṅkhārā| tai kāyasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati|

yathārūpaihi  kāyasaṅkhāraihi yā kāyassa ānamanā vinamanā san‍namanā paṇamanā iñ‍janā phandanā calanā pakampanā  – passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmīti sikkhati, passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmīti sikkhati| yathārūpaihi kāyasaṅkhāraihi yā kāyassa na ānamanā na vinamanā na san‍namanā na paṇamanā aniñ‍janā aphandanā acalanā akampanā santaṃ sukhumaṃ passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmīti sikkhati, passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmīti sikkhati|

iti kira ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ santai vātūpaladdhiyā ca pabhāvanā na hauti, assāsapassāsānañ‍ca pabhāvanā na hauti, ānāpānassatiyā ca pabhāvanā na hauti, ānāpānassatisamādhissa ca  pabhāvanā na hauti; na ca naṃ taṃ samāpattiṃ paṇḍitā samāpaj‍jantipi vuṭṭhahantipi|

iti kira ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ santai vātūpaladdhiyā ca pabhāvanā hauti, assāsapassāsānañ‍ca pabhāvanā hauti, ānāpānassatiyā ca pabhāvanā hauti| ānāpānassatisamādhissa  ca pabhāvanā hauti; tañ‍ca naṃ samāpattiṃ paṇḍitā samāpaj‍jantipi vuṭṭhahantipi| yathā kathaṃ viya? saiyyathāpi kaṃsai ākauṭitai paṭhamaṃ auḷārikā saddā pavattanti| auḷārikānaṃ saddānaṃ nimittaṃ suggahitattā sumanasikatattā sūpadhāritattā niruddhaipi auḷārikai saddai, atha pacchā sukhumakā saddā pavattanti| sukhumakānaṃ saddānaṃ nimittaṃ suggahitattā sumanasikatattā sūpadhāritattā niruddhaipi sukhumakai saddai, atha pacchā sukhumasaddanimittārammaṇatāpi cittaṃ pavattati| aivamaivaṃ paṭhamaṃ auḷārikā assāsapassāsā pavattanti; auḷārikānaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ nimittaṃ suggahitattā sumanasikatattā sūpadhāritattā niruddhaipi auḷārikai assāsapassāsai, atha pacchā sukhumakā assāsapassāsā pavattanti| sukhumakānaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ nimittaṃ suggahitattā sumanasikatattā  sūpadhāritattā niruddhaipi sukhumakai assāsapassāsai, atha pacchā sukhumakaassāsapassāsānaṃ nimittārammaṇatāpi cittaṃ na vikkhaipaṃ gacchati|

aivaṃ santai vātūpaladdhiyā ca pabhāvanā hauti, assāsapassāsānañ‍ca pabhāvanā  hauti, ānāpānassatiyā ca pabhāvanā hauti, ānāpānassatisamādhissa ca pabhāvanā hauti; tañ‍ca naṃ  samāpattiṃ paṇḍitā samāpaj‍jantipi vuṭṭhahantipi| passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsapassāsā kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ, assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| yau tattha saṃvaraṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā; yau tattha avikkhaipaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā; yau tattha dassanaṭṭhau ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhati…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau sikkhati, passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānantau  indriyāni samaudhānaiti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

aṭṭha anupassanāñāṇāni, aṭṭha ca upaṭṭhānānussatiyau, cattāri suttantikavatthūni kāyai kāyānupassanāya|

bhāṇavārau|

dutiyacatuk‍kaniddaisau

172. kathaṃ ‘‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati ‘‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamā pīti? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ| yā pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā| cittassa dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ…pai॰… rassaṃ assāsavasaina, rassaṃ passāsavasaina, sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina, sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī  passāsavasaina, passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsavasaina, passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ| yā pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ pīti|

kathaṃ  sā pīti paṭividitā hauti? dīghaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina sā pīti paṭividitā hauti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina sā pīti paṭividitā hauti| rassaṃ assāsavasaina…pai॰… rassaṃ passāsavasaina… sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ  passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina sā pīti paṭividitā hauti| āvaj‍jatau sā pīti paṭividitā hauti, jānatau…pai॰… passatau… pac‍cavaikkhatau… cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahatau… saddhāya  adhimuc‍catau… vīriyaṃ paggaṇhatau… satiṃ upaṭṭhāpayatau… cittaṃ samādahatau… pañ‍ñāya pajānatau… abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānatau… pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānatau… pahātabbaṃ pajahatau… bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvayatau… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarautau sā pīti paṭividitā hauti| aivaṃ sā pīti paṭividitā hauti|

pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati? anic‍catau anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

173. kathaṃ ‘‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? sukhanti  dvai sukhāni – kāyikañ‍ca sukhaṃ, caitasikañ‍ca sukhaṃ| katamaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ, kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ| katamaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ, caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ, caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ|

kathaṃ  tai sukhā paṭividitā haunti? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai sukhā paṭividitā haunti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai sukhā paṭividitā haunti…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarautau tai sukhā paṭividitā haunti| aivaṃ tai sukhā paṭividitā haunti| sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati  ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| anic‍catau anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī  assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

174. kathaṃ ‘‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamau cittasaṅkhārau? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā…pai॰… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ cittasaṅkhārau|

kathaṃ tai cittasaṅkhārā paṭividitā haunti? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai cittasaṅkhārā paṭividitā haunti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai cittasaṅkhārā paṭividitā haunti…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarautau tai cittasaṅkhārā paṭividitā haunti| aivaṃ tai cittasaṅkhārā paṭividitā  haunti| cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva  sati ca| tāya satiyā tai ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti  kathaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati? anic‍catau anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa  aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

175. kathaṃ ‘‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamau cittasaṅkhārau? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā| tai cittasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā| tai cittasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati| cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā – aitai dhammā cittapaṭibaddhā cittasaṅkhārā| tai cittasaṅkhārai passambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau sikkhati| passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ  assāsapassāsavasaina vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

aṭṭha anupassanāñāṇāni aṭṭha ca upaṭṭhānānussatiyau cattāri suttantikavatthūni vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāya|

tatiyacatuk‍kaniddaisau

176. kathaṃ ‘‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamaṃ taṃ cittaṃ? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ  manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau  manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina  …pai॰… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assāsavasaina… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passāsavasaina  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau mānayatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ cittaṃ|

kathaṃ taṃ cittaṃ paṭividitaṃ hauti? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ paṭividitaṃ hauti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ paṭividitaṃ hauti…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarautau taṃ cittaṃ paṭividitaṃ hauti| aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ paṭividitaṃ hauti| cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

177. kathaṃ  ‘‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti, ‘‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamau cittassa abhippamaudau? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati cittassa abhippamaudau| yā cittassa āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā| cittassa dīghaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati cittassa abhippamaudau| yā cittassa āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa…pai॰… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assāsavasaina… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau uppaj‍jati cittassa abhippamaudau| yā cittassa āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ cittassa abhippamaudau| abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ sati  anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau  sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassatīti| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| abhippamaudayaṃ  cittaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

178. kathaṃ ‘‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? katamau  samādhi katamañ‍ca samādhindiyaṃ (syā॰)? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi, yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti adhiṭṭiti (syā॰) avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā avisāhatamānasatā (syā॰ ka॰) samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi…pai॰… samādahaṃ cittaṃ assāsavasaina…pai॰… samādahaṃ cittaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ samādhi| samādahaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| samādahaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… samādahaṃ  cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

179. kathaṃ  ‘‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? ‘‘rāgatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘rāgatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘dausatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti  sikkhati, ‘‘dausatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘mauhatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati…pai॰… mānatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… diṭṭhiyā vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… vicikicchāya vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… thinatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… uddhac‍catau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… ahirikatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ… ‘‘anauttappatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘anauttappatau vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ sati…pai॰…|

anupassatīti kathaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau  …pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

aṭṭha anupassanāñāṇāni aṭṭha ca upaṭṭhānānussatiyau cattāri suttantikavatthūni cittai cittānupassanāya|

catutthacatuk‍kaniddaisau

180. kathaṃ ‘‘anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? anic‍canti kiṃ anic‍caṃ? pañ‍cakkhandhā anic‍cā| kainaṭṭhaina anic‍cā? uppādavayaṭṭhaina anic‍cā| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ udayaṃ passantau kati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṃ passantau kati lakkhaṇāni passati, udayabbayaṃ passantau kati lakkhaṇāni passati? pañ‍can‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ udayaṃ passantau pañ‍cavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṃ passantau pañ‍cavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ passantau imāni pañ‍ñāsa lakkhaṇāni passati|

‘‘rūpai anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘rūpai anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| ‘‘vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘jarāmaraṇai  anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| anic‍cānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai dhammai anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti  kathaṃ tai dhammai anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ tai dhammai anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| anic‍cānupassī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… anic‍cānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

kathaṃ ‘‘virāgānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘virāgānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? rūpai ādīnavaṃ disvā rūpavirāgai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘rūpai virāgānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘rūpai virāgānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai ādīnavaṃ disvā jarāmaraṇavirāgai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘jarāmaraṇai virāgānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘jarāmaraṇai virāgānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| virāgānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai dhammai anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti  kathaṃ tai dhammai anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ tai dhammai anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| virāgānupassī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… virāgānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

kathaṃ  ‘‘niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? rūpai ādīnavaṃ disvā rūpaniraudhai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘rūpai niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘rūpai niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai ādīnavaṃ disvā jarāmaraṇaniraudhai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘jarāmaraṇai niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘jarāmaraṇai niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati|

181. katihākāraihi  avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti? katihākāraihi avij‍jā nirujjhati? pañ‍cahākāraihi avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti| aṭṭhahākāraihi avij‍jā nirujjhati|

katamaihi  pañ‍cahākāraihi avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti? anic‍caṭṭhaina  avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti, anattaṭṭhaina avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti, santāpaṭṭhaina avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti, vipariṇāmaṭṭhaina avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avij‍jāya ādīnavau hauti|

katamaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi avij‍jā nirujjhati? nidānaniraudhaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, samudayaniraudhaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, jātiniraudhaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, pabhavaniraudhaina āhāraniraudhaina (syā॰) avij‍jā nirujjhati, haituniraudhaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, pac‍cayaniraudhaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, ñāṇuppādaina avij‍jā nirujjhati, niraudhupaṭṭhānaina avij‍jā nirujjhati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi avij‍jā nirujjhati| imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avij‍jāya ādīnavaṃ disvā – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi avij‍jāniraudhai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘avij‍jāya niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘avij‍jāya niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati|

katihākāraihi saṅkhāraisu ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi saṅkhārā nirujjhanti…pai॰… katihākāraihi viñ‍ñāṇai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nirujjhati… katihākāraihi nāmarūpai ādīnavau  hauti, katihākāraihi nāmarūpaṃ nirujjhati… katihākāraihi saḷāyatanai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi saḷāyatanaṃ nirujjhati… katihākāraihi phassai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi phassau nirujjhati… katihākāraihi  vaidanāya ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi vaidanā nirujjhati… katihākāraihi taṇhāya ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi taṇhā nirujjhati… katihākāraihi upādānai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi upādānaṃ nirujjhati… katihākāraihi bhavai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi bhavau nirujjhati… katihākāraihi jātiyā ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi jāti nirujjhati… katihākāraihi jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti, katihākāraihi jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati? pañ‍cahākāraihi jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti, aṭṭhahākāraihi jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati|

katamaihi  pañ‍cahākāraihi jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti? anic‍caṭṭhaina  jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina…pai॰… anattaṭṭhaina…pai॰… santāpaṭṭhaina…pai॰… vipariṇāmaṭṭhaina jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi jarāmaraṇai ādīnavau hauti|

katamaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati? nidānaniraudhaina jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati, samudayaniraudhaina…pai॰… jātiniraudhaina…pai॰… pabhavaniraudhaina bhavaniraudhaina (syā॰) … haituniraudhaina… pac‍cayaniraudhaina… ñāṇuppādaina…pai॰… niraudhupaṭṭhānaina jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi jarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati| imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi jarāmaraṇai ādīnavaṃ disvā imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi jarāmaraṇaniraudhai chandajātau hauti saddhādhimuttau, cittañ‍cassa svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| ‘‘jarāmaraṇai niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘jarāmaraṇai niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| niraudhānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina  dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai dhammai anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti  kathaṃ tai dhammai anupassati…pai॰… aivaṃ tai dhammai anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| niraudhānupassī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… niraudhānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau…pai॰… pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

182. kathaṃ ‘‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati? paṭinissaggāti dvai paṭinissaggā – paric‍cāgapaṭinissaggau ca pakkhandanapaṭinissaggau ca| rūpaṃ paric‍cajatīti – paric‍cāgapaṭinissaggau| rūpaniraudhai nibbānai cittaṃ pakkhandatīti – pakkhandanapaṭinissaggau| ‘‘rūpai paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’’ti sikkhati, ‘‘rūpai paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ paric‍cajatīti – paric‍cāgapaṭinissaggau| jarāmaraṇaniraudhai nibbānai cittaṃ pakkhandatīti – pakkhandanapaṭinissaggau| ‘‘jarāmaraṇai paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’’ti  sikkhati, ‘‘jarāmaraṇai paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’’ti sikkhati| paṭinissaggānupassī  assāsapassāsavasaina dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ sati anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati; sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai dhammai anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

anupassatīti kathaṃ tai dhammai anupassati? anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| aivaṃ tai dhammai anupassati| bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| paṭinissaggānupassī assāsapassāsānaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| yau tattha saṃvaraṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā; yau tattha avikkhaipaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā; yau tattha dassanaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā – imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhati jānantau sikkhati…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau sikkhati|

paṭinissaggānupassī assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti  …pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī  assāsapassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānantau indriyāni samaudhānaiti gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati; balāni samaudhānaiti… baujjhaṅgai samaudhānaiti… maggaṃ samaudhānaiti… dhammai samaudhānaiti gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati|

indriyāni samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ indriyāni samaudhānaiti? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

aṭṭha anupassanai ñāṇāni aṭṭha ca upaṭṭhānānussatiyau cattāri suttantikavatthūni dhammaisu dhammānupassanāya| imāni bāttiṃsa sataukārissa ñāṇāni|

sataukāriñāṇaniddaisau pañ‍camau|

6. ñāṇarāsichak‍kaniddaisau

183. katamāni  catuvīsati samādhivasaina ñāṇāni? dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi, dīghaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi…pai॰… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi, vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| imāni catuvīsati samādhivasaina ñāṇāni|

katamāni dvaisattati vipassanāvasaina ñāṇāni? dīghaṃ assāsaṃ  anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dīghaṃ passāsaṃ anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā…pai॰… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ  assāsaṃ, vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passāsaṃ anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| imāni dvaisattati vipassanāvasaina ñāṇāni|

katamāni  aṭṭha nibbidāñāṇāni? anic‍cānupassī assāsaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passatīti – nibbidāñāṇaṃ, anic‍cānupassī passāsaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passatīti – nibbidāñāṇaṃ…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passatīti – nibbidāñāṇaṃ, paṭinissaggānupassī passāsaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passatīti – nibbidāñāṇaṃ| imāni aṭṭha nibbidāñāṇāni|

katamāni aṭṭha nibbidānulaumai ñāṇāni? anic‍cānupassī assāsaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā nibbidānulaumai ñāṇaṃ, anic‍cānupassī passāsaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā nibbidānulaumai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā nibbidānulaumai ñāṇaṃ, paṭinissaggānupassī passāsaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā nibbidānulaumai ñāṇaṃ – imāni aṭṭha nibbidānulaumai ñāṇāni|

katamāni aṭṭha nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇāni? anic‍cānupassī assāsaṃ paṭisaṅkhā santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇaṃ, anic‍cānupassī passāsaṃ paṭisaṅkhā santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā  nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsaṃ paṭisaṅkhā santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇaṃ, paṭinissaggānupassī passāsaṃ paṭisaṅkhā santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇaṃ – imāni aṭṭha nibbidāpaṭippassaddhiñāṇāni|

katamāni aikavīsati vimuttisukhai ñāṇāni? sautāpattimaggaina sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā  pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati vimuttisukhai ñāṇaṃ, vicikicchāya pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati vimuttisukhai ñāṇaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsassa…pai॰… diṭṭhānusayassa, vicikicchānusayassa pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati vimuttisukhai ñāṇaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikassa, kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanassa…pai॰… paṭighasañ‍ñaujanassa, auḷārikassa kāmarāgānusayassa, paṭighānusayassa pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati  vimuttisukhai ñāṇaṃ, anāgāmimaggaina anusahagatassa kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanassa…pai॰… paṭighasañ‍ñaujanassa, anusahagatassa kāmarāgānusayassa, paṭighānusayassa pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati vimuttisukhai ñāṇaṃ, arahattamaggaina, rūparāgassa…pai॰… arūparāgassa, mānassa, uddhac‍cassa, avij‍jāya, mānānusayassa, bhavarāgānusayassa, avij‍jānusayassa pahīnattā samucchin‍nattā uppaj‍jati vimuttisukhai  ñāṇaṃ| imāni aikavīsati vimuttisukhai ñāṇāni| sauḷasavatthukaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ bhāvayatau samadhikāni imāni dvai ñāṇasatāni uppaj‍janti|

ñāṇarāsichak‍kaniddaisau chaṭṭhau|

ānāpānassatikathā ānāpānakathā (syā॰ ka॰) niṭṭhitā|

4. indriyakathā

1. paṭhamasuttantaniddaisau

184. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai  anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti ‘‘bhaddantai’’ti (ka॰) tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni’’ saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.471|

185. imāni pañ‍cindriyāni katihākāraihi visujjhanti? imāni pañ‍cindriyāni pan‍narasahi ākāraihi visujjhanti| assaddhai puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, saddhai puggalai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau, pasādanīyai suttantai pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi tīhākāraihi saddhindriyaṃ visujjhati| kusītai puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, āraddhavīriyai puggalai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau, sammappadhānai pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi tīhākāraihi vīriyindriyaṃ visujjhati| muṭṭhassatī puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, upaṭṭhitassatī puggalai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau, satipaṭṭhānai pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi tīhākāraihi satindriyaṃ visujjhati| asamāhitai puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, samāhitai puggalai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau, jhānavimaukkhai pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi tīhākāraihi samādhindriyaṃ visujjhati| duppañ‍ñai puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, pañ‍ñavantai puggalai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau, gambhīrañāṇacariyaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi  tīhākāraihi pañ‍ñindriyaṃ visujjhati| iti imai pañ‍ca puggalai parivaj‍jayatau, pañ‍ca puggalai  saivatau bhajatau  payirupāsatau, pañ‍ca suttantakkhandhai pac‍cavaikkhatau – imaihi pan‍narasahi ākāraihi imāni pañ‍cindriyāni visujjhanti|

katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti, katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti? dasahākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti, dasahākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti| assaddhiyaṃ pajahantau saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti, saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaintau assaddhiyaṃ pajahati; kausaj‍jaṃ pajahantau vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaintau kausaj‍jaṃ pajahati; pamādaṃ pajahantau satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti, satindriyaṃ bhāvaintau pamādaṃ pajahati; uddhac‍caṃ pajahantau samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti, samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaintau uddhac‍caṃ pajahati; avij‍jaṃ pajahantau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaintau avij‍jaṃ pajahati| imaihi dasahākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti, imaihi dasahākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti|

katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni? dasahākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| assaddhiyassa pahīnattā suppahīnattā saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti subhāvitaṃ; saddhindriyassa bhāvitattā subhāvitattā assaddhiyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti suppahīnaṃ| kausaj‍jassa pahīnattā suppahīnattā vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti subhāvitaṃ; vīriyindriyassa bhāvitattā subhāvitattā kausaj‍jaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti suppahīnaṃ| pamādassa pahīnattā suppahīnattā satindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti subhāvitaṃ; satindriyassa bhāvitattā subhāvitattā pamādau pahīnau hauti suppahīnau| uddhac‍cassa pahīnattā suppahīnattā samādhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti subhāvitaṃ; samādhindriyassa bhāvitattā subhāvitattā uddhac‍caṃ  pahīnaṃ hauti suppahīnaṃ| avij‍jāya pahīnattā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti subhāvitaṃ; pañ‍ñindriyassa bhāvitattā subhāvitattā avij‍jā pahīnā hauti suppahīnā| imaihi dasahākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni|

186. katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti, katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca? catūhākāraihi  pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti catūhākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca| sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti; sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai  pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca| sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni  bhāviyanti; sakadāgāmiphalakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca| anāgāmimaggakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti; anāgāmiphalakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni  bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca| arahattamaggakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti; arahattaphalakkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca| iti catassau maggavisuddhiyau, catassau phalavisuddhiyau, catassau samucchaidavisuddhiyau, catassau paṭippassaddhivisuddhiyau| imaihi catūhākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāviyanti; imaihi catūhākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni caiva haunti subhāvitāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca|

katinaṃ puggalānaṃ indriyabhāvanā; kati puggalā bhāvitindriyā? aṭṭhan‍naṃ puggalānaṃ indriyabhāvanā; tayau puggalā bhāvitindriyā| katamaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ puggalānaṃ indriyabhāvanā? sattan‍nañ‍ca saikkhānaṃ, puthuj‍janakalyāṇakassa ca – imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ puggalānaṃ indriyabhāvanā| katamai tayau puggalā bhāvitindriyā? savanaina buddhau tathāgatassa sāvakau khīṇāsavau bhāvitindriyau, sayaṃ bhūtaṭṭhaina pac‍caikasambuddhau bhāvitindriyau, appamaiyyaṭṭhaina tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhāvitindriyau – imai tayau puggalā bhāvitindriyā| iti imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ puggalānaṃ indriyabhāvanā; imai tayau puggalā  bhāvitindriyā|

suttantaniddaisau paṭhamau|

2. dutiyasuttantaniddaisau

187. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.476 vā samaṇasammatā, brāhmaṇaisu  vā brāhmaṇasammatā; na ca panaitai āyasmantā āyasmantau saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.476 sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| yai ca khau kaici yai kaici khau (syā॰), yai hi kaici khau (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.476 passitabbā bhikkhavai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca  assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā, brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā; tai ca panāyasmantā sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti|

188. katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayau hauti; katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānāti? katihākāraihi  pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamau hauti; katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamaṃ pajānāti? katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādau hauti, katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādaṃ pajānāti? katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavau hauti; katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavaṃ pajānāti? katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ hauti; katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti?

cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayau hauti; cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānāti| cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamau hauti; cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamaṃ pajānāti| pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādau hauti; pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādaṃ pajānāti| pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavau hauti; pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavaṃ pajānāti| asītisataṃ  ākāraihi asītisatākāraihi (syā॰) pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ hauti; asītisataṃ ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti|

katamaihi  cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayau hauti; katamaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānāti? adhimaukkhatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau saddhindriyassa  samudayau hauti, adhimaukkhavasaina chandassa samudayau saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti, adhimaukkhavasaina manasikārassa samudayau saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti, saddhindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti; paggahatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, paggahavasaina chandassa samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, paggahavasaina manasikārassa samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, vīriyindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti; upaṭṭhānatthāya āvaj‍janāya  samudayau satindriyassa samudayau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina chandassa samudayau satindriyassa samudayau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina manasikārassa samudayau satindriyassa samudayau hauti, satindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa samudayau hauti; avikkhaipatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina chandassa samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina manasikārassa samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, samādhindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti;
