dassanatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti, dassanavasaina chandassa samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti, dassanavasaina manasikārassa samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau  hauti| adhimaukkhatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti, paggahatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, upaṭṭhānatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau  satindriyassa samudayau hauti, avikkhaipatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, dassanatthāya āvaj‍janāya samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti| adhimaukkhavasaina chandassa samudayau saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti, paggahavasaina chandassa samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau  hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina chandassa samudayau satindriyassa samudayau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina chandassa samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, dassanavasaina chandassa samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti| adhimaukkhavasaina manasikārassa samudayau saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti, paggahavasaina manasikārassa samudayau vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina manasikārassa samudayau satindriyassa samudayau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina manasikārassa samudayau samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, dassanavasaina manasikārassa samudayau pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti| saddhindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa samudayau hauti vīriyindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa samudayau hauti, satindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa samudayau hauti, samādhindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa samudayau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina aikattupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa samudayau hauti|

imaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayau hauti, imaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānāti|

katamaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamau hauti; katamaihi cattārīsāya  ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamaṃ pajānāti? adhimaukkhatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, adhimaukkhavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, adhimaukkhavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, saddhindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ aikattaṃ anupaṭṭhānaṃ (syā॰) saddhindriyassa vasaina atthaṅgamau hauti; paggahatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, paggahavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau  hauti, paggahavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, vīriyindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti; upaṭṭhānatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, satindriyassa vasaina  aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti; avikkhaipatthāya  āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, samādhindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti; dassanatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, dassanavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, dassanavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti|

adhimaukkhatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, paggahatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, upaṭṭhānatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, avikkhaipatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, dassanatthāya āvaj‍janāya atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti| adhimaukkhavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, paggahavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, dassanavasaina chandassa atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau  hauti| adhimaukkhavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau saddhindriyassa  atthaṅgamau hauti, paggahavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, upaṭṭhānavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, avikkhaipavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, dassanavasaina manasikārassa atthaṅgamau pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti| saddhindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa  atthaṅgamau hauti, vīriyindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, satindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, samādhindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina aikattaanupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa atthaṅgamau hauti|

imaihi  cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamau hauti; imaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ atthaṅgamaṃ pajānāti|

ka. assādaniddaisau

189. katamaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādau hauti; katamaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādaṃ pajānāti? assaddhiyassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa assādau hauti, assaddhiyapariḷāhassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa assādau hauti, adhimaukkhacariyāya vaisāraj‍jaṃ saddhindriyassa assādau hauti, santau ca vihārādhigamau saddhindriyassa  assādau hauti, yaṃ saddhindriyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ ayaṃ saddhindriyassa assādau|

kausaj‍jassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa assādau hauti, kausaj‍japariḷāhassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa assādau hauti, paggahacariyāya vaisāraj‍jaṃ vīriyindriyassa assādau hauti, santau ca vihārādhigamau vīriyindriyassa assādau hauti, yaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ ayaṃ vīriyindriyassa assādau|

pamādassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa assādau hauti, pamādapariḷāhassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa assādau hauti, upaṭṭhānacariyāya vaisāraj‍jaṃ satindriyassa assādau hauti, santau ca vihārādhigamau satindriyassa assādau hauti, yaṃ  satindriyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ ayaṃ satindriyassa assādau|

uddhac‍cassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa assādau hauti, uddhac‍capariḷāhassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa assādau hauti, avikkhaipacariyāya vaisāraj‍jaṃ samādhindriyassa assādau hauti, santau ca vihārādhigamau samādhindriyassa assādau hauti, yaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ ayaṃ samādhindriyassa assādau|

avij‍jāya  anupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa assādau hauti, avij‍jāpariḷāhassa anupaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa assādau hauti, dassanacariyāya vaisāraj‍jaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa assādau hauti, santau ca vihārādhigamau pañ‍ñindriyassa  assādau hauti, yaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ ayaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa assādau|

imaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādau hauti; imaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādaṃ pajānāti|

kha. ādīnavaniddaisau

190. katamaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavau hauti; katamaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavaṃ pajānāti? assaddhiyassa upaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, assaddhiyapariḷāhassa upaṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anic‍caṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anattaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti|

kausaj‍jassa upaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, kausaj‍japariḷāhassa upaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anic‍caṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina…pai॰… anattaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa ādīnavau hauti|

pamādassa upaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, pamādapariḷāhassa upaṭṭhānaṃ satindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anic‍caṭṭhaina satindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina…pai॰… anattaṭṭhaina satindriyassa ādīnavau hauti|

uddhac‍cassa upaṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, uddhac‍capariḷāhassa upaṭṭhānaṃ  samādhindriyassa  ādīnavau hauti, anic‍caṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina…pai॰… anattaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa ādīnavau hauti|

avij‍jāya upaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, avij‍jāpariḷāhassa upaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anic‍caṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, dukkhaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa ādīnavau hauti, anattaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa ādīnavau hauti|

imaihi  pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavau hauti; imaihi pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādīnavaṃ pajānāti|

ga. nissaraṇaniddaisau

191. katamaihi asītisataṃ ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ hauti; katamaihi asītisataṃ ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ assaddhiyā nissaṭaṃ hauti, assaddhiyapariḷāhā nissaṭaṃ hauti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca nissaṭaṃ hauti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi nissaṭaṃ hauti, tatau paṇītatarasaddhindriyassa paṭilābhā purimatarasaddhindriyā nissaṭaṃ hauti; paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ kausaj‍jā nissaṭaṃ hauti, kausaj‍japariḷāhā nissaṭaṃ hauti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca nissaṭaṃ hauti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi nissaṭaṃ hauti, tatau paṇītataravīriyindriyassa paṭilābhā purimataravīriyindriyā nissaṭaṃ hauti; upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ pamādā nissaṭaṃ hauti, pamādapariḷāhā nissaṭaṃ hauti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca nissaṭaṃ hauti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi nissaṭaṃ hauti, tatau paṇītatarasatindriyassa paṭilābhā purimatarasatindriyā nissaṭaṃ hauti; avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ uddhac‍cā nissaṭaṃ hauti, uddhac‍capariḷāhā nissaṭaṃ hauti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca nissaṭaṃ hauti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi nissaṭaṃ hauti, tatau paṇītatarasamādhindriyassa paṭilābhā purimatarasamādhindriyā nissaṭaṃ hauti; dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ avij‍jāya nissaṭaṃ hauti, avij‍jāpariḷāhā nissaṭaṃ hauti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca nissaṭaṃ hauti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi nissaṭaṃ hauti, tatau paṇītatarapañ‍ñindriyassa paṭilābhā purimatarapañ‍ñindriyā nissaṭaṃ hauti|

192. pubbabhāgai pañ‍cahi indriyaihi paṭhamajjhānavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, paṭhamai jhānai pañ‍cahindriyaihi dutiyajjhānavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, dutiyai jhānai pañ‍cahindriyaihi tatiyajjhānavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, tatiyai jhānai pañ‍cahindriyaihi catutthajjhānavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, catutthai jhānai pañ‍cahindriyaihi ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni  nissaṭāni haunti, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina  pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni  nissaṭāni haunti, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi anic‍cānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti|

anic‍cānupassanā pañ‍cahindriyaihi dukkhānupassanāvasai pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, dukkhānupassanā pañ‍cahindriyaihi anattānupassanāvasai pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, anattānupassanā pañ‍cahindriyaihi nibbidānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, nibbidānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi virāgānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, virāgānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi niraudhānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, niraudhānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi paṭinissaggānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, paṭinissaggānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi khayānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, khayānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi vayānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, vayānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi vipariṇāmānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, vipariṇāmānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi animittānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, animittānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi appaṇihitānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni  nissaṭāni haunti, appaṇihitānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi suñ‍ñatānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti suñ‍ñatānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti| adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi yathābhūtañāṇadassanavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, yathābhūtañāṇadassanai pañ‍cahindriyaihi ādīnavānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, ādīnavānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi paṭisaṅkhānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi vivaṭṭanānupassanāvasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti| vivaṭṭanānupassanāya pañ‍cahindriyaihi sautāpattimaggavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti|

sautāpattimaggai  pañ‍cahindriyaihi sautāpattiphalasamāpattivasaina  pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, sautāpattiphalasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi sakadāgāmimaggavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, sakadāgāmimaggai pañ‍cahindriyaihi sakadāgāmiphalasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, sakadāgāmiphalasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi anāgāmimaggavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, anāgāmimaggai pañ‍cahindriyaihi anāgāmiphalasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, anāgāmiphalasamāpattiyā pañ‍cahindriyaihi  arahattamaggavasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti, arahattamaggai  pañ‍cahindriyaihi arahattaphalasamāpattivasaina pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni haunti|

naikkhammai pañ‍cindriyāni kāmacchandatau nissaṭāni haunti, abyāpādai pañ‍cindriyāni byāpādatau nissaṭāni haunti, ālaukasañ‍ñāya pañ‍cindriyāni thinamiddhatau nissaṭāni haunti, avikkhaipai pañ‍cindriyāni uddhac‍catau nissaṭāni haunti, dhammavavatthānai pañ‍cindriyāni vicikicchāya nissaṭāni haunti, ñāṇai pañ‍cindriyāni avij‍jāya nissaṭāni haunti, pāmauj‍jai pañ‍cindriyāni aratiyā nissaṭāni haunti|

193. paṭhamai jhānai pañ‍cindriyāni nīvaraṇaihi nissaṭāni haunti, dutiyai jhānai pañ‍cindriyāni vitak‍kavicāraihi nissaṭāni haunti, tatiyai jhānai pañ‍cindriyāni pītiyā nissaṭāni haunti, catutthai jhānai pañ‍cindriyāni sukhadukkhaihi nissaṭāni haunti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cindriyāni rūpasañ‍ñāya paṭighasañ‍ñāya nānattasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cindriyāni ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cindriyāni viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā pañ‍cindriyāni ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti|

anic‍cānupassanā pañ‍cindriyāni nic‍casañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, dukkhānupassanā pañ‍cindriyāni sukhasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, anattānupassanā pañ‍cindriyāni  attasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, nibbidānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni nandiyā nissaṭāni haunti, virāgānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni rāgatau nissaṭāni haunti, niraudhānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni samudayatau nissaṭāni  haunti, paṭinissaggānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni ādānatau nissaṭāni haunti, khayānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni ghanasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, vayānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni āyūhanatau nissaṭāni haunti, vipariṇāmānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni dhuvasañ‍ñāya nissaṭāni haunti, animittānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni nimittatau nissaṭāni haunti, appaṇihitānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni paṇidhiyā nissaṭāni haunti, suñ‍ñatānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni abhinivaisatau nissaṭāni haunti| adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni sārādānābhinivaisatau nissaṭāni haunti, yathābhūtañāṇadassanai pañ‍cindriyāni sammauhābhinivaisatau nissaṭāni haunti, ādīnavānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni ālayābhinivaisatau  nissaṭāni haunti, paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni appaṭisaṅkhāya nissaṭāni haunti, vivaṭṭanānupassanāya pañ‍cindriyāni sañ‍ñaugābhinivaisatau nissaṭāni haunti|

sautāpattimaggai pañ‍cindriyāni diṭṭhaikaṭṭhaihi kilaisaihi nissaṭāni haunti, sakadāgāmimaggai pañ‍cindriyāni auḷārikaihi kilaisaihi nissaṭāni haunti, anāgāmimaggai pañ‍cindriyāni anusahagataihi kilaisaihi nissaṭāni  haunti, arahattamaggai pañ‍cindriyāni sabbakilaisaihi nissaṭāni haunti, sabbaisañ‍ñaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍cindriyāni nissaṭāni caiva haunti sunissaṭāni ca paṭippassaddhāni ca suppaṭippassaddhāni ca|

imaihi asītisataṃ ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ hauti; imaihi asītisataṃ ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti|

suttantaniddaisau dutiyau|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

3. tatiyasuttantaniddaisau

194. sāvatthinidānaṃ saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.478-479 passitabbā| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu – aittha saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu  sammappadhānaisu – aittha vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu – aittha satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu jhānaisu – aittha samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu – aittha  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? catūsu sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa  vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni?

catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni| catūsu sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni| catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni| catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni| catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

ka. pabhaidagaṇananiddaisau

195. catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? sappurisasaṃsaivai sautāpattiyaṅgai adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ; saddhammasavanai sautāpattiyaṅgai… yaunisaumanasikārai  sautāpattiyaṅgai… dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā sautāpattiyaṅgai adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

catūsu  sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya sammappadhānai paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, vīriyindriyassa vasaina upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya sammappadhānai…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya sammappadhānai…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā sammappadhānai paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, vīriyindriyassa vasaina upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| catūsu sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, satindriyassa vasaina avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai…pai॰… cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai… dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, satindriyassa  vasaina avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? paṭhamai jhānai avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, samādhindriyassa vasaina dassanaṭṭhaina  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| dutiyai jhānai…pai॰… tatiyai jhānai… catutthai jhānai avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, samādhindriyassa vasaina dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? dukkhai ariyasac‍cai dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| dukkhasamudayai ariyasac‍cai…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhai ariyasac‍cai… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cai dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ| catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍cindriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

kha. cariyavārau

196. catūsu  sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā?

catūsu sammappadhānaisu…pai॰… catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu… catūsu jhānaisu… catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina katihākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā| catūsu sammappadhānaisu…pai॰… catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu… catūsu  jhānaisu… catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? sappurisasaṃsaivai sautāpattiyaṅgai adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| saddhammasavanai sautāpattiyaṅgai…pai॰… yaunisau manasikārai sautāpattiyaṅgai… dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā sautāpattiyaṅgai adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā  daṭṭhabbā, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā  daṭṭhabbā, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu saddhindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

catūsu sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya sammappadhānai paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, vīriyindriyassa vasaina upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya sammappadhānai…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya sammappadhānai…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā sammappadhānai paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā…pai॰… catūsu  sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai  upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, satindriyassa vasaina avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai…pai॰… cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānai upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā…pai॰… catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu satindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? paṭhamai jhānai avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, samādhindriyassa vasaina dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa  cariyā daṭṭhabbā| dutiyai jhānai…pai॰… tatiyai jhānai…pai॰… catutthai jhānai avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā…pai॰…  catūsu jhānaisu samādhindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā? dukkhai ariyasac‍cai dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| dukkhasamudayai ariyasac‍cai…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhai ariyasac‍cai… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cai dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, paggahaṭṭhaina  vīriyindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina  samādhindriyassa cariyā daṭṭhabbā| catūsu ariyasac‍caisu pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ cariyā daṭṭhabbā|

ga. cāravihāraniddaisau

197. cārau ca vihārau ca anubuddhau hauti paṭividdhau, yathācarantaṃ yathāviharantaṃ viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī gambhīraisu ṭhānaisu aukappaiyyuṃ  – ‘‘addhā, ayamāyasmā pattau vā pāpuṇissati vā’’|

cariyāti aṭṭha cariyāyau – iriyāpathacariyā, āyatanacariyā, saticariyā, samādhicariyā, ñāṇacariyā, maggacariyā, patticariyā, laukatthacariyāti| iriyāpathacariyāti catūsu iriyāpathaisu| āyatanacariyāti chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu| saticariyāti catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu| samādhicariyāti catūsu jhānaisu| ñāṇacariyāti catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| maggacariyāti catūsu ariyamaggaisu| patticariyāti catūsu sāmañ‍ñaphalaisu| laukatthacariyāti tathāgataisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu, padaisai pac‍caikabuddhaisu, padaisai sāvakaisu| iriyāpathacariyā ca paṇidhisampan‍nānaṃ, āyatanacariyā ca indriyaisu  guttadvārānaṃ, saticariyā ca appamādavihārīnaṃ, samādhicariyā ca adhicittamanuyuttānaṃ, ñāṇacariyā ca buddhisampan‍nānaṃ, maggacariyā ca sammāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ, patticariyā ca adhigataphalānaṃ, laukatthacariyā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ, padaisai pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ, padaisai sāvakānaṃ| imā aṭṭha cariyāyau|

aparāpi  aṭṭha cariyāyau| adhimuc‍cantau saddhāya carati, paggaṇhantau vīriyaina carati, upaṭṭhāpaintau satiyā carati, avikkhaipaṃ karauntau  samādhinā carati, pajānantau pañ‍ñāya carati, vijānantau viñ‍ñāṇacariyāya carati, aivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa kusalā dhammā āyāpaintīti āyatanacariyāya carati, aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau visaisamadhigacchatīti visaisacariyāya carati – imā aṭṭha cariyāyau|

aparāpi aṭṭha cariyāyau| dassanacariyā ca sammādiṭṭhiyā, abhiniraupanacariyā ca sammāsaṅkappassa, pariggahacariyā ca sammāvācāya, samuṭṭhānacariyā ca sammākammantassa, vaudānacariyā ca sammāājīvassa, paggahacariyā ca sammāvāyāmassa, upaṭṭhānacariyā ca sammāsatiyā, avikkhaipacariyā ca sammāsamādhissa – imā aṭṭha cariyāyau|

vihārauti  adhimuc‍cantau saddhāya viharati, paggaṇhantau vīriyaina viharati, upaṭṭhāpaintau satiyā viharati, avikkhaipaṃ karauntau samādhinā viharati, pajānantau pañ‍ñāya viharati|

anubuddhauti saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhau anubuddhau hauti, vīriyindriyassa paggahaṭṭhau anubuddhau hauti, satindriyassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau anubuddhau hauti, samādhindriyassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau anubuddhau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa dassanaṭṭhau anubuddhau hauti|

paṭividdhauti saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhau paṭividdhau hauti, vīriyindriyassa paggahaṭṭhau paṭividdhau hauti, satindriyassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau paṭividdhau hauti, samādhindriyassa avikkhaipaṭṭhau paṭividdhau hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa  dassanaṭṭhau paṭividdhau hauti| yathācarantanti aivaṃ saddhāya carantaṃ, aivaṃ vīriyaina carantaṃ, aivaṃ satiyā carantaṃ, aivaṃ samādhinā carantaṃ, aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya carantaṃ| yathāviharantanti aivaṃ saddhāya viharantaṃ, aivaṃ vīriyaina viharantaṃ, aivaṃ satiyā viharantaṃ, aivaṃ samādhinā viharantaṃ, aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya viharantaṃ| viñ‍ñūti  viñ‍ñū  vibhāvī maidhāvī paṇḍitā buddhisampan‍nā| sabrahmacārīti aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā| gambhīraisu ṭhānaisūti gambhīrāni ṭhānāni vuc‍canti jhānā ca vimaukkhā ca samādhī ca samāpattiyau ca maggā ca phalāni ca abhiñ‍ñāyau ca paṭisambhidā ca| aukappaiyyunti saddahaiyyuṃ adhimuc‍caiyyuṃ| addhāti aikaṃsavacanamaitaṃ nissaṃsayavacanamaitaṃ nik‍kaṅkhavacanamaitaṃ advaijjhavacanamaitaṃ advaiḷhakavacanamaitaṃ niyaugavacanamaitaṃ apaṇṇakavacanamaitaṃ avatthāpanavacanamaitaṃ – addhāti| āyasmāti piyavacanamaitaṃ garuvacanamaitaṃ sagāravasappatissādhivacanamaitaṃ – āyasmāti| pattau  vāti adhigatau vā| pāpuṇissati vāti adhigamissati vā|

suttantaniddaisau tatiyau|

4. catutthasuttantaniddaisau

198. purimanidānaṃ paripuṇṇanidānaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni katihākāraihi kainaṭṭhaina daṭṭhabbāni? imāni pañ‍cindriyāni  chahākāraihi tainaṭṭhaina daṭṭhabbāni – ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina ādivisaudhanaṭṭhaina adhimattaṭṭhaina, adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina, pariyādānaṭṭhaina, patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhaina’’|

ka. ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaniddaisau

199. kathaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni?  assaddhiyaṃ pajahatau adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kausaj‍jaṃ pajahatau paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, vīriyindriyassa vasaina upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| pamādaṃ pajahatau upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, satindriyassa vasaina avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ| uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, samādhindriyassa vasaina dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| avij‍jaṃ pajahatau dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina paggahādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, vīriyindriyassa vasaina upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina upaṭṭhānādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, satindriyassa vasaina avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina avikkhaipādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, samādhindriyassa vasaina dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ pajahatau naikkhammavasaina dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

byāpādaṃ pajahatau abyāpādavasaina…pai॰… thinamiddhaṃ  pajahatau ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahatau arahattamaggavasaina adhimaukkhādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, saddhindriyassa vasaina paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahatau arahattamaggavasaina dassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina  satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

kha. ādivisaudhanaṭṭhaniddaisau

200. kathaṃ ādivisaudhanaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina  saddhindriyaṃ, assaddhiyasaṃvaraṭṭhaina assaddhiyaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina (syā॰ ka॰) aivamīdisaisu padaisupi sīlavisuddhi – saddhindriyassa ādivisaudhanā| paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ, kausaj‍jasaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – vīriyindriyassa ādivisaudhanā| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ, pamādasaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – satindriyassa ādivisaudhanā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ, uddhac‍casaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – samādhindriyassa ādivisaudhanā| dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, avij‍jāsaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – pañ‍ñindriyassa ādivisaudhanā| naikkhammai pañ‍cindriyāni, kāmacchandasaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādivisaudhanā| abyāpādai pañ‍cindriyāni, byāpādasaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādivisaudhanā…pai॰… arahattamaggai pañ‍cindriyāni, sabbakilaisasaṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi – pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ādivisaudhanā| aivaṃ ādivisaudhanaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

ga. adhimattaṭṭhaniddaisau

201. kathaṃ adhimattaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni?  saddhindriyassa bhāvanāya chandau uppaj‍jati – chandavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| chandavasaina pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati – pāmauj‍javasaina saddhāvasaina  saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pāmauj‍javasaina pīti uppaj‍jati – pītivasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pītivasaina passaddhi uppaj‍jati  – passaddhivasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| passaddhivasaina sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati – sukhavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| sukhavasaina aubhāsau uppaj‍jati – aubhāsavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| aubhāsavasaina saṃvaigau uppaj‍jati – saṃvaigavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saṃvaijaitvā cittaṃ samādahati – samādhivasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ paggaṇhāti – paggahavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| tathāpaggahitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhati – upaikkhāvasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| upaikkhāvasaina nānattakilaisaihi  cittaṃ vimuc‍cati – vimaukkhavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vimuttattā tai dhammā aikarasā haunti – aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanāvasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| bhāvitattā tatau paṇītatarai vivaṭṭanti – vivaṭṭanāvasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vivaṭṭitattā tatau vausaj‍jati vaussaj‍jati (syā॰ ka॰) – vausaggavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vausaj‍jitattā tatau nirujjhanti – niraudhavasaina  saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| niraudhavasaina dvai vausaggā – paric‍cāgavausaggau ca, pakkhandanavausaggau ca| kilaisai ca khandhai ca paric‍cajatīti – paric‍cāgavausaggau| niraudhanibbānadhātuyā cittaṃ pakkhandatīti – pakkhandanavausaggau| niraudhavasaina imai dvai vausaggā|

assaddhiyassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… assaddhiyapariḷāhassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhaikaṭṭhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… auḷārikānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… anusahagatānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati – chandavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti…pai॰… vīriyindriyassa bhāvanāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… kausaj‍jassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… kausaj‍japariḷāhassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhaikaṭṭhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… satindriyassa bhāvanāya  chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pamādassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… pamādapariḷāhassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… samādhindriyassa bhāvanāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… uddhac‍cassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati uddhac‍capariḷāhassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… pañ‍ñindriyassa bhāvanāya chandau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avij‍jāya pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… avij‍jāpariḷāhassa pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhaikaṭṭhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… auḷārikānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… anusahagatānaṃ  kilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya chandau uppaj‍jati  – chandavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| chandavasaina pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati – pāmauj‍javasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pāmauj‍javasaina pīti uppaj‍jati – pītivasaina pañ‍ñā vasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pītivasaina passaddhi uppaj‍jati – passaddhivasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| passaddhivasaina sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati – sukhavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| sukhavasaina aubhāsau uppaj‍jati – aubhāsavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| aubhāsavasaina saṃvaigau uppaj‍jati – saṃvaigavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saṃvaijaitvā cittaṃ samādahati – samādhivasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ paggaṇhāti – paggahavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| tathāpaggahitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhati – upaikkhāvasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| upaikkhāvasaina nānattakilaisaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati – vimaukkhavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vimuttattā tai dhammā aikarasā haunti – bhāvanāvasaina aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanāvasaina (syā॰ ka॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbau pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| bhāvitattā tatau paṇītatarai vivaṭṭanti – vivaṭṭanāvasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vivaṭṭitattā tatau vausaj‍jati – vausaggavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| vausaj‍jitattā  tatau nirujjhanti – niraudhavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| niraudhavasaina dvai vausaggā – paric‍cāgavausaggau ca, pakkhandanavausaggau ca| kilaisai ca khandhai ca paric‍cajatīti  – paric‍cāgavausaggau| niraudhanibbānadhātuyā cittaṃ pakkhandatīti – pakkhandanavausaggau| niraudhavasaina imai dvai vausaggā| aivaṃ adhimattaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

gha. adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaniddaisau

202. kathaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? saddhindriyassa bhāvanāya chandau uppaj‍jati – chandavasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| chandavasaina pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati – pāmauj‍javasaina saddhāvasaina saddhindriyaṃ adhiṭṭhāti…pai॰… aivaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

ṅa. pariyādānaṭṭhaniddaisau

kathaṃ  pariyādānaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ assaddhiyaṃ pariyādiyati, assaddhiyapariḷāhaṃ pariyādiyati| paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ kausaj‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, kausaj‍japariḷāhaṃ pariyādiyati| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ pamādaṃ pariyādiyati, pamādapariḷāhaṃ pariyādiyati| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ uddhac‍caṃ pariyādiyati, uddhac‍capariḷāhaṃ pariyādiyati| dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ avij‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, avij‍jāpariḷāhaṃ pariyādiyati| naikkhammai pañ‍cindriyāni kāmacchandaṃ pariyādiyanti| abyāpādai pañ‍cindriyāni  byāpādaṃ pariyādiyanti| ālaukasañ‍ñāya pañ‍cindriyāni thinamiddhaṃ pariyādiyanti| avikkhaipai pañ‍cindriyāni uddhac‍caṃ pariyādiyanti…pai॰… arahattamaggai pañ‍cindriyāni sabbakilaisai pariyādiyanti| aivaṃ pariyādānaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

ca. patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhaniddaisau

203. kathaṃ patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni? saddhau saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhai patiṭṭhāpaiti, saddhassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhai patiṭṭhāpaiti| vīriyavā vīriyindriyaṃ paggahai patiṭṭhāpaiti, vīriyavatau vīriyindriyaṃ paggahai patiṭṭhāpaiti| satimā satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānai patiṭṭhāpaiti, satimatau satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānai patiṭṭhāpaiti| samāhitau samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipai patiṭṭhāpaiti, samāhitassa samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipai patiṭṭhāpaiti| pañ‍ñavā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dassanai patiṭṭhāpaiti, pañ‍ñavatau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dassanai patiṭṭhāpaiti| yaugāvacarau pañ‍cindriyāni naikkhammai patiṭṭhāpaiti, yaugāvacarassa pañ‍cindriyāni naikkhammai patiṭṭhāpainti| yaugāvacarau  pañ‍cindriyāni abyāpādai patiṭṭhāpaiti, yaugāvacarassa pañ‍cindriyāni abyāpādai patiṭṭhāpainti| yaugāvacarau pañ‍cindriyāni ālaukasañ‍ñāya patiṭṭhāpaiti, yaugāvacarassa pañ‍cindriyāni ālaukasañ‍ñāya patiṭṭhāpainti| yaugāvacarau pañ‍cindriyāni avikkhaipai patiṭṭhāpaiti, yaugāvacarassa pañ‍cindriyāni avikkhaipai patiṭṭhāpainti…pai॰… yaugāvacarau pañ‍cindriyāni arahattamaggai patiṭṭhāpaiti, yaugāvacarassa pañ‍cindriyāni arahattamaggai patiṭṭhāpainti| aivaṃ patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhaina indriyāni daṭṭhabbāni|

suttantaniddaisau catutthau|

5. indriyasamaudhānaṃ

204. puthuj‍janau  samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? saikkhau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? vītarāgau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi  upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? puthuj‍janau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau sattahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| saikkhau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau aṭṭhahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| vītarāgau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

puthuj‍janau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi sattahākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? āvaj‍jitattā ārammaṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, samathanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, paggahanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, avikkhaipūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, aubhāsūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, sampahaṃsanūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, upaikkhūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – puthuj‍janau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau imaihi sattahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

saikkhau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi aṭṭhahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? āvaj‍jitattā ārammaṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, samathanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, paggahanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, avikkhaipūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, aubhāsūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, sampahaṃsanūpaṭṭhānakusalau  hauti, upaikkhūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, aikattūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – saikkhau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau imaihi aṭṭhahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

vītarāgau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? āvaj‍jitattā  ārammaṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti…pai॰… aikattūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, ñāṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, vimuttūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – vītarāgau samādhiṃ bhāvaintau imaihi dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

205. puthuj‍janau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, katihākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? saikkhau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, katihākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? vītarāgau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katihākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, katihākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti?

puthuj‍janau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau navahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, navahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| saikkhau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, dasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| vītarāgau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau dvādasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, dvādasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

puthuj‍janau  vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi navahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau  hauti, katamaihi navahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? anic‍catau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, nic‍catau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| dukkhatau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, sukhatau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| anattatau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, attatau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| khayatau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, ghanatau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| vayatau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, āyūhanānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| vipariṇāmūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, dhuvatau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| animittūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, nimittānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| appaṇihitūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, paṇidhianupaṭṭhānakusalau paṇidhānupaṭṭhānakusalau (syā॰) hauti| suñ‍ñatūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, abhinivaisānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – puthuj‍janau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau imaihi navahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, imaihi navahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

206. saikkhau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau  hauti, katamaihi dasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? anic‍catau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, nic‍catau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti…pai॰… suñ‍ñatūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, abhinivaisānūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| ñāṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, añāṇaanupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – saikkhau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau imaihi  dasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, imaihi dasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau  hauti|

vītarāgau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau katamaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, katamaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? anic‍catau upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, nic‍catau anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti…pai॰… ñāṇūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, añāṇānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| visañ‍ñaugūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, sañ‍ñaugānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti| niraudhūpaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, saṅkhārānupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti – vītarāgau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi upaṭṭhānakusalau hauti, imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi anupaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

āvaj‍jitattā ārammaṇūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina indriyāni samaudhānaiti, gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti, samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati…pai॰… dhammai samaudhānaiti, gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti, samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati| indriyāni samaudhānaitīti kathaṃ indriyāni samaudhānaiti? adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ samaudhānaiti…pai॰… samathanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, paggahanimittūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, avikkhaipūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, aubhāsūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, sampahaṃsanūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, upaikkhūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, aikattūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, ñāṇūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, vimuttūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina|

anic‍catau upaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, nic‍catau anupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, dukkhatau upaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, sukhatau anupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, anattatau  upaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, attatau anupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, khayatau upaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, ghanatau anupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, vayatau upaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, āyūhanānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, vipariṇāmūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, dhuvatau anupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, animittūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, nimittānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, appaṇihitūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, paṇidhianupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, suñ‍ñatūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, abhinivaisānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, ñāṇūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, añāṇānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, visañ‍ñaugūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, sañ‍ñaugānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, niraudhūpaṭṭhānakusalavasaina, saṅkhārānupaṭṭhānakusalavasaina indriyāni samaudhānaiti, gaucarañ‍ca pajānāti, samatthañ‍ca paṭivijjhati|

207. catusaṭṭhiyā  ākāraihi tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ vasībhāvatāpañ‍ñā āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ? anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa, añ‍ñindriyassa, añ‍ñātāvindriyassa|

anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? añ‍ñindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ aikaṃ ṭhānaṃ gacchati – sautāpattimaggaṃ| añ‍ñindriyaṃ cha ṭhānāni gacchati – sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmimaggaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattamaggaṃ| añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ aikaṃ ṭhānaṃ gacchati – arahattaphalaṃ|

sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai  anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ paggahaparivāraṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ upaṭṭhānaparivāraṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ avikkhaipaparivāraṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dassanaparivāraṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ vijānanaparivāraṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ abhisandanaparivāraṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai jātā dhammā, ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, sabbaiva kusalā haunti, sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva niyyānikā haunti, sabbaiva apacayagāminau haunti, sabbaiva  laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaparivārā haunti, nissayaparivārā haunti, sampayuttaparivārā haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca|

sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai…pai॰… arahattaphalakkhaṇai añ‍ñātāvindriyassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimaukkhaparivāraṃ hauti…pai॰… jīvitindriyaṃ pavattasantatādhipataiyyaparivāraṃ hauti| arahattaphalakkhaṇai jātā dhammā sabbaiva abyākatā haunti, ṭhapaitvā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ sabbaiva anāsavā haunti, sabbaiva laukuttarā haunti, sabbaiva nibbānārammaṇā haunti| arahattaphalakkhaṇai  añ‍ñātāvindriyassa  imāni aṭṭhindriyāni sahajātaparivārā haunti| taiva tassa ākārā caiva haunti parivārā ca| iti imāni aṭṭha aṭṭhakāni catusaṭṭhi haunti|

āsavāti katamai tai āsavā? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, diṭṭhāsavau, avij‍jāsavau| katthaitai āsavā khīyanti khiyyanti (ka॰)? sautāpattimaggaina anavasaisau diṭṭhāsavau khīyati| apāyagamanīyau kāmāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau bhavāsavau khīyati, apāyagamanīyau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| anāgāmimaggaina anavasaisau kāmāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau bhavāsavau khīyati, tadaikaṭṭhau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti| arahattamaggaina anavasaisau bhavāsavau khīyati, anavasaisau avij‍jāsavau khīyati| aitthaitai āsavā khīyanti|

208.

 na tassa addiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci, athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ, tathāgatau taina samantacakkhūti||

samantacakkhūti kainaṭṭhaina samantacakkhu? cuddasa buddhañāṇāni – dukkhai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ| imāni cuddasa  buddhañāṇāni| imaisaṃ cuddasan‍naṃ buddhañāṇānaṃ aṭṭha ñāṇāni sāvakasādhāraṇāni, cha ñāṇāni asādhāraṇāni sāvakaihi|

yāvatā  dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau ñātau, añ‍ñātau dukkhaṭṭhau natthīti – samantacakkhu| yaṃ samantacakkhu taṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ| yāvatā dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau diṭṭhau viditau sacchikatau phassitau  pañ‍ñāya; aphassitau pañ‍ñāya dukkhaṭṭhau natthīti – samantacakkhu| yaṃ samantacakkhu taṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ| yāvatā  samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau…pai॰… yāvatā niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau… yāvatā maggassa maggaṭṭhau… yāvatā atthapaṭisambhidāya atthapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā dhammapaṭisambhidāya dhammapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā niruttipaṭisambhidāya niruttipaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya paṭibhānapaṭisambhidaṭṭhau… yāvatā indriyaparaupariyattai ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ… yāvatā sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā  pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, taṃ ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya natthīti – samantacakkhu| yaṃ samantacakkhu taṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ, paggahaṭṭhaina vīriyindriyaṃ, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satindriyaṃ, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhindriyaṃ|

saddahantau paggaṇhāti, paggaṇhantau saddahati| saddahantau upaṭṭhāpaiti, upaṭṭhāpaintau saddahati| saddahantau samādahati, samādahantau saddahati| saddahantau pajānāti, pajānantau saddahati| paggaṇhantau upaṭṭhāpaiti, upaṭṭhāpaintau paggaṇhāti| paggaṇhantau samādahati, samādahantau paggaṇhāti| paggaṇhantau pajānāti, pajānantau paggaṇhāti| paggaṇhantau saddahati, saddahantau paggaṇhāti| upaṭṭhāpaintau samādahati, samādahantau upaṭṭhāpaiti| upaṭṭhāpaintau pajānāti, pajānantau upaṭṭhāpaiti| upaṭṭhāpaintau saddahati, saddahantau upaṭṭhāpaiti| upaṭṭhāpaintau paggaṇhāti, paggaṇhantau upaṭṭhāpaiti| samādahantau pajānāti, pajānantau samādahati| samādahantau saddahati, saddahantau samādahati| samādahantau paggaṇhāti, paggaṇhantau samādahati| samādahantau upaṭṭhāpaiti, upaṭṭhāpaintau samādahati| pajānantau saddahati, saddahantau pajānāti| pajānantau paggaṇhāti, paggaṇhantau  pajānāti| pajānantau upaṭṭhāpaiti, upaṭṭhāpaintau pajānāti| pajānantau samādahati, samādahantau pajānāti|

saddahitattā  paggahitaṃ, paggahitattā saddahitaṃ| saddahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ, upaṭṭhāpitattā saddahitaṃ| saddahitattā samādahitaṃ, samādahitattā saddahitaṃ| saddahitattā pajānitaṃ, pajānitattā saddahitaṃ| paggahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ, upaṭṭhāpitattā paggahitaṃ| paggahitattā samādahitaṃ, samādahitattā paggahitaṃ| paggahitattā pajānitaṃ pajānitattā paggahitaṃ| paggahitattā saddahitaṃ, saddahitattā  paggahitaṃ| upaṭṭhāpitattā samādahitaṃ, samādahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ| upaṭṭhāpitattā pajānitaṃ, pajānitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ| upaṭṭhāpitattā saddahitaṃ, saddahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ| upaṭṭhāpitattā paggahitaṃ, paggahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ| samādahitattā pajānitaṃ, pajānitattā samādahitaṃ| samādahitattā saddahitaṃ, saddahitattā samādahitaṃ| samādahitattā paggahitaṃ, paggahitattā samādahitaṃ| samādahitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ, upaṭṭhāpitattā samādahitaṃ| pajānitattā saddahitaṃ, saddahitattā pajānitaṃ| pajānitattā paggahitaṃ, paggahitattā pajānitaṃ| pajānitattā upaṭṭhāpitaṃ, upaṭṭhāpitattā pajānitaṃ| pajānitattā samādahitaṃ, samādahitattā pajānitaṃ|

yaṃ buddhacakkhu taṃ buddhañāṇaṃ, yaṃ buddhañāṇaṃ taṃ buddhacakkhu, yaina cakkhunā tathāgatau sattai passati apparajakkhai  mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai (syā॰ ka॰), appaikac‍cai naparalaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau|

apparajakkhai mahārajakkhaiti saddhau puggalau apparajakkhau, assaddhau puggalau mahārajakkhau| āraddhavīriyau puggalau apparajakkhau, kusītau puggalau mahārajakkhau| upaṭṭhitassati puggalau apparajakkhau, muṭṭhassati puggalau mahārajakkhau| samāhitau puggalau apparajakkhau, asamāhitau puggalau mahārajakkhau| pañ‍ñavā puggalau apparajakkhau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau mahārajakkhau|

tikkhindriyai mudindriyaiti saddhau puggalau tikkhindriyau, assaddhau puggalau mudindriyau…pai॰… pañ‍ñavā puggalau tikkhindriyau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau mudindriyau| svākārai dvākāraiti saddhau puggalau svākārau, assaddhau puggalau dvākārau…pai॰… pañ‍ñavā puggalau svākārau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau dvākārau| suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayaiti saddhau puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, assaddhau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau…pai॰… pañ‍ñavā puggalau suviñ‍ñāpayau, duppañ‍ñau puggalau duviñ‍ñāpayau|

appaikac‍cai  paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau, appaikac‍cai naparalaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinauti saddhau puggalau  paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, assaddhau puggalau naparalaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī| āraddhavīriyau puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, kusītau puggalau naparalaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī…pai॰… pañ‍ñavā puggalau paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī, duppañ‍ñau puggalau naparalaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvī|

laukauti khandhalaukau dhātulaukau āyatanalaukau vipattibhavalaukau  vipattisambhavalaukau sampattibhavalaukau sampattisambhavalaukau|

aikau laukau – sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā| dvai laukā – nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca| tayau laukā – tissau vaidanā| cattārau laukā – cattārau āhārā| pañ‍ca laukā – pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| cha laukā – cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni| satta laukā – satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| aṭṭha laukā – aṭṭha laukadhammā| nava laukā – nava sattāvāsā| dasa laukā – dasāyatanāni| dvādasa laukā – dvādasāyatanāni| aṭṭhārasa laukā – aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau|

vaj‍janti sabbai kilaisā vaj‍jā, sabbai duc‍caritā vaj‍jā, sabbai abhisaṅkhārā vaj‍jā, sabbai bhavagāmikammā vaj‍jā| iti imasmiñ‍ca laukai imasmiñ‍ca vaj‍jai tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā haunti, saiyyathāpi ukkhittāsikai vadhakai| imaihi pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi imāni pañ‍cindriyāni jānāti passati añ‍ñāti paṭivijjhatīti|

indriyasamaudhānau pañ‍camau|

tatiyabhāṇavārau|

indriyakathā niṭṭhitā|

5. vimaukkhakathā

1. uddaisau

209. purimanidānaṃ| ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, vimaukkhā| katamai tayau? suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau, animittau vimaukkhau, appaṇihitau vimaukkhau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau vimaukkhā|

‘‘api  ca, aṭṭhasaṭṭhi vimaukkhā – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau, animittau vimaukkhau, appaṇihitau vimaukkhau; ajjhattavuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau, bahiddhāvuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau, dubhatau vuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau; ajjhattavuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā, bahiddhāvuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā, dubhatau vuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā; ajjhattavuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā, bahiddhāvuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā, dubhatau vuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā; ajjhattavuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī ajjhattavuṭṭhānā paṭippassaddhī (syā॰) cattārau vimaukkhā, bahiddhāvuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā, dubhatau vuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā; rūpī rūpāni passatīti vimaukkhau, ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passatīti vimaukkhau, subhaṃ taiva adhimuttau hautīti vimaukkhau; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti  vimaukkhau; samayavimaukkhau, asamayavimaukkhau; sāmayikau vimaukkhau, asāmayikau vimaukkhau; kuppau vimaukkhau, akuppau vimaukkhau; laukiyau vimaukkhau, laukuttarau vimaukkhau; sāsavau vimaukkhau, anāsavau vimaukkhau; sāmisau vimaukkhau, nirāmisau vimaukkhau; nirāmisānirāmisatarau vimaukkhau, paṇihitau vimaukkhau, appaṇihitau vimaukkhau, paṇihitappaṭippassaddhi vimaukkhau; sañ‍ñuttau  vimaukkhau, visañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau; aikattavimaukkhau, nānattavimaukkhau, sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau, ñāṇavimaukkhau; sītisiyāvimaukkhau sītīsiyāvimaukkhau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), jhānavimaukkhau, anupādācittassa vimaukkhau’’|

2. niddaisau

210. katamau suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’’ti| sau tattha abhinivaisaṃ na karautīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau|

katamau  animittau vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’’ti| sau tattha nimittaṃ na karautīti – animittau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ animittau vimaukkhau|

katamau appaṇihitau vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’’ti| sau tattha paṇidhiṃ na karautīti – appaṇihitau  vimaukkhau| ayaṃ appaṇihitau vimaukkhau|

katamau ajjhattavuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau? cattāri jhānāni – ayaṃ ajjhattavuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau| katamau bahiddhāvuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau? catassau arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ bahiddhāvuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau| katamau dubhatau vuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau? cattārau ariyamaggā – ayaṃ dubhatau vuṭṭhānau vimaukkhau|

katamai ajjhattavuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ nīvaraṇaihi vuṭṭhāti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ vitak‍kavicāraihi vuṭṭhāti| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ pītiyā vuṭṭhāti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sukhadukkhaihi vuṭṭhāti| imai ajjhattavuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai bahiddhāvuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā? ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti rūpasañ‍ñāya paṭighasañ‍ñāya nānattasañ‍ñāya vuṭṭhāti| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya vuṭṭhāti| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya vuṭṭhāti| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti  ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya vuṭṭhāti| imai bahiddhāvuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai dubhatau vuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā? sautāpattimaggau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhivicikicchāsīlabbataparāmāsā diṭṭhānusayā vicikicchānusayā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā  ca sabbanimittaihi  vuṭṭhāti| sakadāgāmimaggau auḷārikā kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanā paṭighasañ‍ñaujanā auḷārikā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| anāgāmimaggau anusahagatā kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanā paṭighasañ‍ñaujanā anusahagatā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| arahattamaggau rūparāgā arūparāgā mānā uddhac‍cā avij‍jāya mānānusayā bhavarāgānusayā avij‍jānusayā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| imai dubhatau vuṭṭhānā cattārau vimaukkhā|

211. katamai ajjhattavuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca – imai ajjhattavuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai bahiddhāvuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā? ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ  …pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca – imai bahiddhāvuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai dubhatau vuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā? sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā| sakadāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaṃ  paṭilābhatthāya…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā – imai dubhatau vuṭṭhānānaṃ anulaumā cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai ajjhattavuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā? paṭhamassa jhānassa paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā, dutiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… tatiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… catutthassa  jhānassa paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā – imai ajjhattavuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai  bahiddhāvuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā? ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā – imai bahiddhāvuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā|

katamai dubhatau vuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā? sautāpattimaggassa sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggassa sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmimaggassa anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattamaggassa arahattaphalaṃ – imai dubhatau vuṭṭhānapaṭippassaddhī cattārau vimaukkhā|

212. kathaṃ rūpī rūpāni passatīti – vimaukkhau? idhaikac‍cau ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ nīlanimittaṃ manasikarauti, nīlasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ supadhāritaṃ (ka॰) upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā bahiddhā nīlanimittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati, nīlasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca ubhayamidaṃ rūpa’’nti, rūpasañ‍ñī hauti| idhaikac‍cau ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ pītanimittaṃ…pai॰… lauhitanimittaṃ…pai॰… audātanimittaṃ manasikarauti, audātasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā bahiddhā audātanimittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati, audātasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā āsaivati  bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca ubhayamidaṃ rūpa’’nti, rūpasañ‍ñī hauti| aivaṃ rūpī rūpāni passatīti – vimaukkhau|

kathaṃ  ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passatīti – vimaukkhau? idhaikac‍cau ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ nīlanimittaṃ  na manasikarauti, nīlasañ‍ñaṃ na paṭilabhati; bahiddhā nīlanimittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati, nīlasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpaṃ, bahiddhā rūpamida’’nti, rūpasañ‍ñī hauti| idhaikac‍cau ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ pītanimittaṃ…pai॰… lauhitanimittaṃ…pai॰… audātanimittaṃ na manasikarauti, audātasañ‍ñaṃ na paṭilabhati; bahiddhā audātanimittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati, audātasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ karauti, sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraiti, svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraitvā svāvatthitaṃ avatthāpaitvā āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpaṃ bahiddhā rūpamida’’nti, rūpasañ‍ñī hauti| aivaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passatīti – vimaukkhau|

kathaṃ ‘‘subha’’ntaiva adhimuttau hautīti – vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā  tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (syā॰) pharitvā viharati| maittāya bhāvitattā sattā appaṭikūlā haunti| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… karuṇāya bhāvitattā sattā appaṭikūlā haunti| muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāya bhāvitattā sattā appaṭikūlā haunti| upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati…pai॰… upaikkhāya bhāvitattā sattā appaṭikūlā haunti| aivaṃ ‘‘subhaṃ’’ taiva adhimuttau hautīti – vimaukkhau|

213. katamau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ  amanasikārā ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau|

katamau  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau|

katamau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau|

katamau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti vimaukkhau|

katamau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti vimaukkhau? idha bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti vimaukkhau|

katamau samayavimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ samayavimaukkhau|

katamau asamayavimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ asamayavimaukkhau|

katamau sāmayikau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ sāmayikau vimaukkhau|

katamau asāmayikau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ asāmayikau vimaukkhau|

katamau kuppau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ kuppau vimaukkhau|

katamau  akuppau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ akuppau vimaukkhau|

katamau  laukiyau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ laukiyau vimaukkhau|

katamau laukuttarau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ laukuttarau vimaukkhau|

katamau sāsavau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ sāsavau vimaukkhau|

katamau anāsavau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri  ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ anāsavau vimaukkhau|

katamau sāmisau vimaukkhau? rūpappaṭisañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau – ayaṃ sāmisau vimaukkhau|

katamau nirāmisau vimaukkhau? arūpappaṭisañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau – ayaṃ nirāmisau vimaukkhau|

katamau nirāmisā nirāmisatarau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ nirāmisānirāmisatarau vimaukkhau|

katamau paṇihitau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ paṇihitau vimaukkhau| katamau appaṇihitau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ appaṇihitau vimaukkhau|

katamau paṇihitappaṭippassaddhi vimaukkhau? paṭhamassa jhānassa paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā  …pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā paṭilābhau  vā vipākau vā – ayaṃ paṇihitappaṭippassaddhi vimaukkhau|

katamau sañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ sañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau|

katamau visañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ visañ‍ñuttau vimaukkhau|

katamau  aikattavimaukkhau? cattārau ca ariyamaggā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, nibbānañ‍ca – ayaṃ aikattavimaukkhau|

katamau nānattavimaukkhau? cattāri ca jhānāni, catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau – ayaṃ nānattavimaukkhau|

214. katamau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau? siyā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau hauti, vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍cānupassanāñāṃ nic‍catau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṃ sukhatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ nandiyā sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ rāgatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ samudayatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ ādānatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ  nimittatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

rūpai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai  suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sañ‍ñāya muc‍catīti – sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā, aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā aikau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina – ayaṃ sañ‍ñāvimaukkhau|

215. katamau ñāṇavimaukkhau? siyā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā haunti, dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍cānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ sukhatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| anattānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ attatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ nandiyā sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| virāgānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ  ñāṇaṃ rāgatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ samudayatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ ādānatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| animittānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ nimittatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā haunti, dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

rūpai anic‍cānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| aivaṃ  siyā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā  haunti, dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

vaidanāya  …pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanā yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau sammauhā añ‍ñāṇā muc‍catīti – ñāṇavimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā haunti, dasa ñāṇavimaukkhā aikau ñāṇavimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina – ayaṃ ñāṇavimaukkhau|

216. katamau sītisiyāvimaukkhau? siyā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍cānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ sukhatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| anattānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ attatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanā  anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ nandiyā santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| virāgānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ rāgatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ samudayatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ ādānatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| animittānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ nimittatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

rūpai  anic‍cānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau  santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ nic‍catau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanā anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ ñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau santāpapariḷāhadarathā muc‍catīti – sītisiyāvimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā haunti, dasa sītisiyāvimaukkhā aikau sītisiyāvimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina – ayaṃ sītisiyāvimaukkhau|

217. katamau jhānavimaukkhau? naikkhammaṃ jhāyatīti jāyatīti (syā॰) jhānaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ  jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| jhāyantau jāyantau (syā॰) muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāpaintau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāyantīti – dhammā| jhāpaitīti – kilaisai| jhātai ca jhāpai ca jānātīti – jhānavimaukkhau| abyāpādau  jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| byāpādaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| jhāyantau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāpaintau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāyantīti – dhammā| jhāpaitīti – kilaisai| jhātai ca jhāpai ca jānātīti – jhānavimaukkhau| ālaukasañ‍ñā jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ…pai॰… avikkhaipau jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| uddhac‍caṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| dhammavavatthānaṃ jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| vicikicchaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| ñāṇaṃ jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| avij‍jaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| pāmauj‍jaṃ jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| aratiṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| nīvaraṇai jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ…pai॰… arahattamaggau jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| sabbakilaisai jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| jhāyantau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāpaintau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāyantīti – dhammā| jhāpaitīti – kilaisai| jhātai ca jhāpai ca jānātīti – jhānavimaukkhau – ayaṃ jhānavimaukkhau|
