218. katamau  anupādā cittassa vimaukkhau? siyā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā haunti, dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ  sukhatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ nandiyā upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ rāgatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ samudayatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ ādānatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ nimittatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ  paṇidhiyā upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā haunti, dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

rūpai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau dasa anupādācittassa  vimaukkhā haunti, dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau upādānā muc‍catīti – anupādācittassa vimaukkhau| aivaṃ siyā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā haunti, dasa anupādācittassa vimaukkhā aikau anupādācittassa vimaukkhau hauti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ katihupādānaihi muc‍cati? dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ katihupādānaihi muc‍cati? anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ katihupādānaihi muc‍cati? nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ…pai॰… virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ… niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ… paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ… animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ… appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ… suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ katihupādānaihi muc‍catīti?

anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ  tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ  tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati|

anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati? diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ imaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamā aikupādānā aikūpādānā (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) muc‍cati? kāmupādānā  – dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ idaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| imā aikupādānā (syā॰) anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati? diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ imaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamā aikupādānā muc‍cati? kāmupādānā – nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ idaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamā aikupādānā muc‍cati? kāmupādānā – virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ idaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati? kāmupādānā, diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ imaihi catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati? kāmupādānā, diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ  imaihi catūhupādānaihi muc‍cati| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati? diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā  – animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ imaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamā aikupādānā muc‍cati? kāmupādānā – appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ idaṃ aikupādānā muc‍cati| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ katamaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati? diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ imaihi tīhupādānaihi muc‍cati|

yañ‍ca anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ – imāni cattāri ñāṇāni tīhupādānaihi muc‍canti – diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā| yañ‍ca dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yaṃ ca nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ  – imāni cattāri ñāṇāni aikupādānā muc‍canti – kāmupādānā| yañ‍ca niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ, yañ‍ca paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ – imāni dvai ñāṇāni catūhupādānaihi muc‍canti – kāmupādānā, diṭṭhupādānā, sīlabbatupādānā, attavādupādānā – ayaṃ anupādācittassa vimaukkhau|

vimaukkhakathāya paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

219. tīṇi  khau panimāni vimaukkhamukhāni laukaniyyānāya saṃvattanti| sabbasaṅkhārai paricchaidaparivaṭumatau paricchaidaparivaṭṭumatau (syā॰) samanupassanatāya animittāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya, sabbasaṅkhāraisu manausamuttaijanatāya appaṇihitāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya, sabbadhammai paratau samanupassanatāya suñ‍ñatāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya – imāni tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni laukaniyyānāya saṃvattanti|

anic‍catau manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? dukkhatau manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? anattatau manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti, dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti, anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti|

anic‍catau  manasikarautau kiṃ bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? dukkhatau manasikarautau kiṃ bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti|

anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau katamindriyaṃ paṭilabhati? dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau katamindriyaṃ paṭilabhati? anattatau manasikarauntau  vaidabahulau katamindriyaṃ paṭilabhati? anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau saddhindriyaṃ paṭilabhati| dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau samādhindriyaṃ paṭilabhati| anattatau manasikarauntau vaidabahulau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ paṭilabhati|

anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti, bhāvanāya katindriyāni  tadanvayā tadanvayāni (syā॰) haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti? dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti, bhāvanāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti? anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti, bhāvanāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti? anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa saddhindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa indriyabhāvanā| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa samādhindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa indriyabhāvanā| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa indriyabhāvanā|

220. anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? bhāvanāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti? paṭivaidhakālai katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? paṭivaidhāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti? kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kainaṭṭhaina  paṭivaidhau? dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? bhāvanāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti? paṭivaidhakālai katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? paṭivaidhāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti? kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kainaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau? anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? bhāvanāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti? paṭivaidhakālai katamindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? paṭivaidhāya katindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti? kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā? kainaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau?

anic‍catau  manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa saddhindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭivaidhakālai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| paṭivaidhāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau| aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa samādhindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭivaidhakālai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| paṭivaidhāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau| aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| bhāvanāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭivaidhakālaipi pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| paṭivaidhāya cattārindriyāni tadanvayā  haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau| aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati|

221. anic‍catau manasikarautau katamindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti? katamindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau saddhādhimuttau (syā॰) hauti? dukkhatau manasikarautau katamindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti? katamindriyassa  adhimattattā kāyasakkhī kāyasakkhi (syā॰ ka॰) abhi॰ pu॰ pa॰ 95 passitabbā hauti? anattatau manasikarautau katamindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti? katamindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti?

anic‍catau  manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| samādhindriyassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| anattatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti|

saddahantau vimuttauti – saddhāvimuttau| phuṭṭhattā sacchikatauti sacchikarautīti (syā॰ pī॰) – kāyasakkhī| diṭṭhattā pattauti – diṭṭhippattau| saddahantau vimuc‍catīti – saddhāvimuttau| jhānaphassaṃ paṭhamaṃ phusati, pacchā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarautīti – kāyasakkhī| ‘‘dukkhā saṅkhārā, sukhau niraudhau’’ti ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāyāti – diṭṭhippattau| yau cāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau, yau ca kāyasakkhī, yau  ca diṭṭhippattau, siyā imai tayau puggalā saddhāvimuttāpi kāyasakkhīpi diṭṭhippattāpi vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| anattatau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| aivaṃ imai tayau puggalā saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā|

dukkhatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| samādhindriyassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| anattatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| samādhindriyassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| anic‍catau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| samādhindriyassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| aivaṃ imai tayau puggalā samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī|

anattatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti| anic‍catau manasikarautau  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau  hauti| aivaṃ imai tayau puggalā  pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yau  cāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau, yau ca kāyasakkhī, yau ca diṭṭhippattau, aivaṃ siyā imai tayau puggalā saddhāvimuttāpi kāyasakkhīpi diṭṭhippattāpi vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina| yau cāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau, yau ca kāyasakkhī, yau ca diṭṭhippattau, siyā imai tayau puggalā…pai॰… añ‍ñauyaiva saddhāvimuttau, añ‍ñau kāyasakkhī, añ‍ñau diṭṭhippattau siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| samādhindriyassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| anattatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti| yau cāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau, yau ca kāyasakkhī, yau ca diṭṭhippattau, aivaṃ siyā imai tayau puggalā saddhāvimuttāpi kāyasakkhīpi diṭṭhippattāpi vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina| añ‍ñauyaiva saddhāvimuttau, añ‍ñau kāyasakkhī, añ‍ñau diṭṭhippattau|

anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, saddhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhati; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘saddhānusārī’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| saddhindriyassa vasaina catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti| yai hi kaici saddhindriyassa vasaina sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhanti, sabbai tai saddhānusārinau|

anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, saddhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘saddhāvimuttau’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| saddhindriyassa vasaina cattārindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| yaihi kaici saddhindriyassa vasaina sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikatā, sabbai tai saddhāvimuttā|

anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, saddhindriyassa adhimattattā sakadāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati… anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ paṭilabhati… arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā sacchikataṃ hauti; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘saddhāvimuttau’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti…pai॰… sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| saddhindriyassa vasaina cattārindriyāni bhāvitāni  haunti subhāvitāni| yaihi kaici saddhindriyassa vasaina arahattaṃ sacchikatā, sabbai tai saddhāvimuttā|

dukkhatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, samādhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhati; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyasakkhī’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| samādhindriyassa vasaina catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti| yai hi kaici samādhindriyassa vasaina sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhanti, sabbai tai kāyasakkhī|

dukkhatau manasikarautau samādhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, samādhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… sakadāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyasakkhī’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| samādhindriyassa vasaina cattārindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| yaihi kaici samādhindriyassa vasaina arahattaṃ sacchikatā, sabbai tai kāyasakkhī|

anattatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhati; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammānusārī’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti…pai॰… sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| pañ‍ñindriyassa  vasaina  catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanā hauti| yai hi kaici pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhanti, sabbai tai dhammānusārinau|

anattatau manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘diṭṭhippattau’’| cattārindriyāni  tadanvayā haunti…pai॰… sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina cattārindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| yaihi kaici pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikatā, sabbai tai diṭṭhippattā|

anattatau  manasikarautau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyassa adhimattattā sakadāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ paṭilabhati…pai॰… arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti; taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘diṭṭhippattau’’| cattārindriyāni tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina cattārindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| yaihi kaici pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina arahattaṃ sacchikatā, sabbai tai diṭṭhippattā|

222. yai hi kaici naikkhammaṃ bhāvitā vā bhāvainti vā bhāvissanti vā, adhigatā vā adhigacchanti vā adhigamissanti vā, pattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, paṭiladdhā vā paṭilabhanti vā paṭilabhissanti vā, paṭividdhā vā paṭivijjhanti vā paṭivijjhissanti vā, sacchikatā vā sacchikaraunti vā sacchikarissanti vā, phassitā vā phassanti vā phassissanti vā, vasippattā  vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, pāramippattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, vaisāraj‍jappattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, sabbai tai saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai hi kaici abyāpādaṃ…pai॰… ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ… avikkhaipaṃ… dhammavavatthānaṃ… ñāṇaṃ… pāmauj‍jaṃ… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ … ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ… anic‍cānupassanaṃ… dukkhānupassanaṃ… anattānupassanaṃ… nibbidānupassanaṃ… virāgānupassanaṃ … niraudhānupassanaṃ… paṭinissaggānupassanaṃ… khayānupassanaṃ… vayānupassanaṃ… vipariṇāmānupassanaṃ… animittānupassanaṃ… appaṇihitānupassanaṃ… suñ‍ñatānupassanaṃ… adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanaṃ… yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ… ādīnavānupassanaṃ… paṭisaṅkhānupassanaṃ… vivaṭṭanānupassanaṃ… sautāpattimaggaṃ… sakadāgāmimaggaṃ… anāgāmimaggaṃ… arahattamaggaṃ…|

yai hi kaici cattārau satipaṭṭhānai… cattārau sammappadhānai… cattārau iddhipādai… pañ‍cindriyāni… pañ‍ca balāni… satta baujjhaṅgai… ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ… yai hi kaici aṭṭha  vimaukkhai bhāvitā vā bhāvainti  vā bhāvissanti vā, adhigatā vā adhigacchanti vā adhigamissanti vā, pattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, paṭiladdhā vā paṭilabhanti vā paṭilabhissanti vā, paṭividdhā vā paṭivijjhanti vā paṭivijjhissanti vā, sacchikatā vā sacchikaraunti vā sacchikarissanti vā, phassitā vā phassanti vā phassissanti vā, vasippattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā pāramippattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, vaisāraj‍jappattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, sabbai tai saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai hi kaici catassau paṭisambhidā pattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā…pai॰… sabbai tai saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai hi kaici tissau vij‍jā paṭividdhā vā paṭivijjhanti vā paṭivijjhissanti vā…pai॰… sabbai tai saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai hi kaici tissau sikkhā sikkhitā vā sikkhanti vā sikkhissanti vā, sacchikatā vā sacchikaraunti vā sacchikarissanti vā, phassitā vā  phassanti vā phassissanti vā, vasippattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, pāramippattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, vaisāraj‍jappattā vā pāpuṇanti vā pāpuṇissanti vā, sabbai tai saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai  hi kaici dukkhaṃ parijānanti samudayaṃ pajahanti niraudhaṃ sacchikaraunti maggaṃ bhāvainti, sabbai tai saddhindriyassa  vasaina saddhāvimuttā, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

katihākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti? katihākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati? catūhākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| catūhākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, samudayasac‍caṃ pahānappaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ  paṭivijjhati, maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| imaihi catūhākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| imaihi catūhākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhantau saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttau, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattau|

katihākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti? katihākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati? navahākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti, navahākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, samudayasac‍caṃ pahānappaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| abhiñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau ca sabbadhammānaṃ, pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau ca sabbasaṅkhārānaṃ, pahānappaṭivaidhau ca sabbākusalānaṃ, bhāvanāpaṭivaidhau ca catun‍naṃ maggānaṃ, sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhau ca niraudhassa| imaihi navahākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| imaihi navahākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhantau saddhindriyassa vasaina saddhāvimuttau, samādhindriyassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, pañ‍ñindriyassa vasaina diṭṭhippattau|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

223. anic‍catau  manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? dukkhatau manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? anattatau manasikarautau kathaṃ saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti? anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti| dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhanti|

anic‍catau manasikarautau kiṃ bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? dukkhatau manasikarautau kiṃ bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ  bahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulaṃ cittaṃ hauti| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulaṃ  cittaṃ hauti|

anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau katamaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati? dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau katamaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati? anattatau manasikarauntau vaidabahulau katamaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati? anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau animittaṃ  vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati| dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati| anattatau manasikarauntau vaidabahulau suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati|

224. anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti? dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti? anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā  haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kau bhāvaiti?

anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa animittau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti| bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa vimaukkhabhāvanā| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa appaṇihitau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti| bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā  haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa vimaukkhabhāvanā| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti| bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti| aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| yau sammāpaṭipan‍nau sau bhāvaiti; natthi micchāpaṭipan‍nassa vimaukkhabhāvanā|

anic‍catau  manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālai katamau vimaukkhau  ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kainaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau? dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālai katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kainaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau? anattatau  manasikarautau vaidabahulassa katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya kati vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā  haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālai katamau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya kativimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, kainaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, kainaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau?

225. anic‍catau manasikarautau adhimaukkhabahulassa animittau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālaipi animittau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau, aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati| dukkhatau manasikarautau passaddhibahulassa appaṇihitau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālaipi  appaṇihitau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau, aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati| anattatau manasikarautau vaidabahulassa suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, bhāvanāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā  haunti, aikarasā haunti, paṭivaidhakālaipi suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, paṭivaidhāya dvai vimaukkhā tadanvayā haunti, sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti, aikarasā haunti, aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, dassanaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhau, aivaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi bhāvaiti, bhāvaintaupi paṭivijjhati|

226. anic‍catau manasikarautau katamau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti? katamavimaukkhassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti? dukkhatau  manasikarautau katamau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti? katamavimaukkhassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti? anattatau manasikarautau katamau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti? katamavimaukkhassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau  hauti?

anic‍catau manasikarautau animittau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti| animittavimaukkhassa adhimattattā saddhāvimuttau hauti| dukkhatau manasikarautau appaṇihitau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti| appaṇihitavimaukkhassa adhimattattā kāyasakkhī hauti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau adhimattau hauti| suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa adhimattattā diṭṭhippattau hauti|

saddahantau vimuttauti – saddhāvimuttau| phuṭṭhattā sacchikatauti – kāyasakkhī| diṭṭhattā pattauti – diṭṭhippattau| saddahantau vimuc‍catīti – saddhāvimuttau| jhānaphassaṃ paṭhamaṃ phusati, pacchā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarautīti – kāyasakkhī| ‘‘dukkhā saṅkhārā, sukhau niraudhau’’ti viñ‍ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāyāti – diṭṭhippattau…pai॰… yai hi kaici naikkhammaṃ bhāvitā vā bhāvainti vā bhāvissanti vā…pai॰… sabbai tai animittavimaukkhassa vasaina saddhāvimuttā, appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

yai hi kaici abyāpādaṃ… ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ… avikkhaipaṃ… yai hi kaici dukkhaṃ parijānanti, samudayaṃ pajahanti, niraudhaṃ sacchikaraunti, maggaṃ bhāvainti, sabbai tai animittavimaukkhassa vasaina  saddhāvimuttā, appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina diṭṭhippattā|

katihākāraihi  sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti? katihākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati? catūhākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| catūhākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati, samudayasac‍caṃ pahānapaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaṃ  paṭivijjhati| maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati| imaihi catūhākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| imaihi catūhākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhantau animittavimaukkhassa vasaina saddhāvimuttau, appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina diṭṭhippattau|

katihākāraihi  sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti? katihākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati? navahākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| navahākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| dukkhasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaṃ paṭivijjhati…pai॰… sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhau ca niraudhassa| imaihi navahākāraihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hauti| imaihi navahākāraihi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhantau animittavimaukkhassa vasaina saddhāvimuttau, appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina kāyasakkhī, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina diṭṭhippattau|

227. anic‍catau manasikarauntau katamai dhammai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati? kathaṃ sammādassanaṃ hauti? kathaṃ tadanvayaina sabbai saṅkhārā  anic‍catau sudiṭṭhā haunti? kattha kaṅkhā pahīyati? dukkhatau manasikarauntau katamai dhammai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati? kathaṃ sammādassanaṃ hauti? kathaṃ tadanvayaina sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhatau sudiṭṭhā haunti? kattha kaṅkhā pahīyati? anattatau manasikarauntau katamai dhammai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati? kathaṃ sammādassanaṃ hauti? kathaṃ tadanvayaina sabbai dhammā anattatau sudiṭṭhā haunti? kattha kaṅkhā pahīyati?

anic‍catau manasikarauntau nimittaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; taina vuc‍cati – sammādassanaṃ| aivaṃ tadanvayaina sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍catau sudiṭṭhā haunti| aittha kaṅkhā pahīyati| dukkhatau manasikarauntau pavattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; taina vuc‍cati – sammādassanaṃ| aivaṃ tadanvayaina  sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhatau sudiṭṭhā haunti| aittha kaṅkhā pahīyati| anattatau manasikarauntau nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; taina vuc‍cati – sammādassanaṃ| aivaṃ tadanvayaina sabbai dhammā anattatau sudiṭṭhā haunti| aittha kaṅkhā pahīyati|

yañ‍ca  yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ yañ‍ca sammādassanaṃ yā ca kaṅkhāvitaraṇā, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ yañ‍ca sammādassanaṃ  yā ca kaṅkhāvitaraṇā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

anic‍catau manasikarautau kiṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti? dukkhatau manasikarautau kiṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti? anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti? anic‍catau manasikarautau nimittaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| dukkhatau manasikarautau pavattaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| anattatau manasikarautau nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca bhayatau upaṭṭhāti|

yā ca bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ yā ca nibbidā, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yā ca bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ yā ca nibbidā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

yā ca anattānupassanā yā ca suñ‍ñatānupassanā, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yā ca anattānupassanā yā ca suñ‍ñatānupassanā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

anic‍catau manasikarautau kiṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati? dukkhatau manasikarautau kiṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati? anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati? anic‍catau manasikarautau nimittaṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| dukkhatau manasikarautau pavattaṃ  paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ  uppaj‍jati| anattatau manasikarautau nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati|

yā ca muc‍citukamyatā yā ca paṭisaṅkhānupassanā yā ca saṅkhārupaikkhā, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yā ca muc‍citukamyatā yā ca paṭisaṅkhānupassanā yā ca saṅkhārupaikkhā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

anic‍catau manasikarautau kutau cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti, kattha cittaṃ pakkhandati? dukkhatau manasikarautau kutau cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti, kattha cittaṃ pakkhandati? anattatau manasikarautau kutau cittaṃ  vuṭṭhāti, kattha cittaṃ pakkhandati? anic‍catau manasikarautau nimittā cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti, animittai cittaṃ pakkhandati| dukkhatau manasikarautau pavattā cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti, appavattai cittaṃ pakkhandati| anattatau manasikarautau nimittā ca pavattā ca cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti, animittai appavattai niraudhai nibbānadhātuyā niraudhanibbānadhātuyā (sī॰) cittaṃ pakkhandati|

yā ca bahiddhāvuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā yai ca gautrabhū dhammā, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yā ca bahiddhāvuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai  pañ‍ñā yai ca gautrabhū dhammā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

anic‍catau  manasikarauntau katamaina vimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati? dukkhatau manasikarauntau katamaina vimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati? anattatau manasikarauntau katamaina vimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati? anic‍catau manasikarauntau animittavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| dukkhatau manasikarauntau appaṇihitavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| anattatau manasikarauntau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| yā ca dubhatauvuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca maggai ñāṇaṃ, imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca, udāhu aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānanti? yā ca dubhatauvuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca maggai ñāṇaṃ, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ|

228. katihākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai nānākhaṇai (syā॰ ka॰) haunti? katihākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti? catūhākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti| sattahākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti|

katamaihi catūhākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti? ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina, adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina, abhinīhāraṭṭhaina, niyyānaṭṭhaina| kathaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti? anic‍catau manasikarautau animittau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, dukkhatau manasikarautau appaṇihitau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti, anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau ādhipataiyyau hauti| aivaṃ  ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti|

kathaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti? anic‍catau manasikarauntau animittavimaukkhassa vasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, dukkhatau manasikarauntau appaṇihitavimaukkhassa  vasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, anattatau manasikarauntau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| aivaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti|

kathaṃ  abhinīhāraṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti? anic‍catau manasikarauntau animittavimaukkhassa vasaina cittaṃ abhinīharati, dukkhatau manasikarauntau appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina cittaṃ abhinīharati, anattatau manasikarauntau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina cittaṃ abhinīharati| aivaṃ abhinīhāraṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti|

kathaṃ niyyānaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti? anic‍catau manasikarauntau animittavimaukkhassa vasaina niraudhaṃ  nibbānaṃ niyyāti, dukkhatau manasikarauntau appaṇihitavimaukkhassa vasaina niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ niyyāti, anattatau manasikarauntau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhassa vasaina niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ niyyāti| aivaṃ niyyānaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti| imaihi catūhākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā nānākkhaṇai haunti|

katamaihi sattahākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti? samaudhānaṭṭhaina, adhigamanaṭṭhaina, paṭilābhaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina, sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina, phassanaṭṭhaina, abhisamayaṭṭhaina| kathaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina adhigamanaṭṭhaina paṭilābhaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina phassanaṭṭhaina abhisamayaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti? anic‍catau manasikarauntau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| yatau muc‍cati, tattha na paṇidahatīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| yattha na paṇidahati, taina suñ‍ñauti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| yaina suñ‍ñau, taina nimittaina animittauti – animittau vimaukkhau| aivaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina adhigamanaṭṭhaina paṭilābhaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina phassanaṭṭhaina abhisamayaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti|

dukkhatau manasikarauntau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| yattha na paṇidahati, taina suñ‍ñauti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| yaina suñ‍ñau, taina nimittaina animittauti – animittau vimaukkhau| yaina nimittaina animittau, tattha na paṇidahatīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| aivaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina adhigamanaṭṭhaina paṭilābhaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina phassanaṭṭhaina abhisamayaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti|

anattatau  manasikarauntau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| yaina suñ‍ñau, taina nimittaina animittauti – animittau vimaukkhau| yaina nimittaina animittau, tattha na paṇidahatīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| yattha na paṇidahati, taina suñ‍ñauti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| aivaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina  adhigamanaṭṭhaina paṭilābhaṭṭhaina paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina phassanaṭṭhaina abhisamayaṭṭhaina tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti| imaihi sattahākāraihi tayau vimaukkhā aikakkhaṇai haunti|

229. atthi vimaukkhau, atthi mukhaṃ, atthi vimaukkhamukhaṃ, atthi vimaukkhapac‍canīkaṃ, atthi vimaukkhānulaumaṃ, atthi vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, atthi vimaukkhabhāvanā, atthi vimaukkhappaṭippassaddhi|

katamau vimaukkhau? suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau, animittau vimaukkhau, appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| katamau suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau? anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ nandiyā abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ rāgatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ samudayatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ ādānatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ nimittatau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbābhinivaisaihi muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau|

rūpai  anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbābhinivaisaihi muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau abhinivaisā muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbābhinivaisaihi  muc‍catīti – suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ  suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau|

katamau animittau vimaukkhau? anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau  vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ nandiyā nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ rāgatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ samudayatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ ādānatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbanimittaihi muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau|

rūpai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau  …pai॰… rūpai animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbanimittaihi muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| rūpai appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbanimittaihi muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| jarāmaraṇai appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ paṇidhiyā nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau nimittā muc‍catīti – animittau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ animittau vimaukkhau|

katamau appaṇihitau vimaukkhau? anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ nandiyā paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti  – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| virāgānupassanāñāṇaṃ  rāgatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| niraudhānupassanāñāṇaṃ samudayatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| paṭinissaggānupassanāñāṇaṃ ādānatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| animittānupassanāñāṇaṃ  nimittatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ  sabbapaṇidhīhi muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau|

rūpai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau…pai॰… rūpai appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbapaṇidhīhi muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| rūpai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai appaṇihitānupassanāñāṇaṃ sabbapaṇidhīhi muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| jarāmaraṇai suñ‍ñatānupassanāñāṇaṃ abhinivaisatau paṇidhiyā muc‍catīti – appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| ayaṃ vimaukkhau|

230. katamaṃ mukhaṃ? yai tattha jātā anavaj‍jā kusalā baudhipakkhiyādhammā, idaṃ mukhaṃ|

katamaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ? yaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ niraudhau nibbānaṃ, idaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ| vimaukkhañ‍ca mukhañ‍ca vimaukkhamukhaṃ, idaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

katamaṃ  vimaukkhapac‍canīkaṃ? tīṇi akusalamūlāni vimaukkhapac‍canīkāni, tīṇi duc‍caritāni vimaukkhapac‍canīkāni, sabbaipi akusalā dhammā vimaukkhapac‍canīkā, idaṃ vimaukkhapac‍canīkaṃ|

katamaṃ  vimaukkhānulaumaṃ? tīṇi kusalamūlāni vimaukkhānulaumāni, tīṇi sucaritāni vimaukkhānulaumāni, sabbaipi kusalā dhammā vimaukkhānulaumā, idaṃ vimaukkhānulaumaṃ|

katamau  vimaukkhavivaṭṭau? sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau, caitauvivaṭṭau, cittavivaṭṭau, ñāṇavivaṭṭau, vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, sac‍cavivaṭṭau| sañ‍jānantau vivaṭṭatīti – sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau| caitayantau vivaṭṭatīti – caitauvivaṭṭau| vijānantau vivaṭṭatīti – cittavivaṭṭau| ñāṇaṃ karauntau vivaṭṭatīti – ñāṇavivaṭṭau| vausaj‍jantau vivaṭṭatīti – vimaukkhavivaṭṭau| tathaṭṭhaina vivaṭṭatīti – sac‍cavivaṭṭau|

yattha  sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau tattha caitauvivaṭṭau, yattha caitauvivaṭṭau tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau, yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau tattha cittavivaṭṭau, yattha cittavivaṭṭau tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau tattha ñāṇavivaṭṭau, yattha ñāṇavivaṭṭau tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau tattha vimaukkhavivaṭṭau, yattha vimaukkhavivaṭṭau tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau| yattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau vimaukkhavivaṭṭau tattha sac‍cavivaṭṭau, yattha sac‍cavivaṭṭau tattha sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭau caitauvivaṭṭau cittavivaṭṭau ñāṇavivaṭṭau vimaukkhavivaṭṭau| ayaṃ vimaukkhavivaṭṭau|

katamā  vimaukkhabhāvanā? paṭhamassa jhānassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, dutiyassa jhānassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, tatiyassa jhānassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, catutthassa jhānassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, sautāpattimaggassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, anāgāmimaggassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, arahattamaggassa āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, ayaṃ  vimaukkhabhāvanā|

katamā vimaukkhappaṭippassaddhi? paṭhamassa jhānassa paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā, dutiyassa jhānassa paṭilābhau vā vipākau vā, tatiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā paṭilābhau vā vipākau  vā, sautāpattimaggassa sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmimaggassa sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmimaggassa anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattamaggassa arahattaphalaṃ, ayaṃ vimaukkhappaṭippassaddhīti|

tatiyabhāṇavārau|

vimaukkhakathā niṭṭhitā|

6. gatikathā

231. gatisampattiyā  ñāṇasampayuttai katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttai katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? rūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? arūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti?

gatisampattiyā ñāṇasampayuttai aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti| khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttai aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti| rūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti| arūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

232. gatisampattiyā ñāṇasampayuttai katamaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai tayau haitū kusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – kusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā| nikantikkhaṇai dvai haitū akusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – akusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū abyākatā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’|

paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍cakkhandhā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā  haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau  mahābhūtā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau jīvitasaṅkhārā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau khandhā arūpinau sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai  cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai aṭṭhavīsati dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| gatisampattiyā ñāṇasampayuttai imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttai katamaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai tayau haitū kusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā’’| nikantikkhaṇai dvai haitū akusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘akusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā’’| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū abyākatā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā  haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’|

paṭisandhikkhaṇai  pañ‍cakkhandhā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau mahābhūtā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau jīvitasaṅkhārā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā  haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā  haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau khandhā arūpinau sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai aṭṭhavīsati dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttai imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ  haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

rūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ katamaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai tayau haitū kusalā…pai॰… rūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

arūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ katamaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ  pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai tayau haitū kusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā’’| nikantikkhaṇai dvai haitū akusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya  sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘akusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā’’| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū abyākatā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ’’|

paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau khandhā arūpinau sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau haitū sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā  haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā  haunti| arūpāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

233. gatisampattiyā ñāṇavippayuttai katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttai katinaṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti?

gatisampattiyā ñāṇavippayuttai chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti| khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttai chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

gatisampattiyā ñāṇavippayuttai katamaisaṃ chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai dvai haitū kusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – kusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā| nikantikkhaṇai dvai haitū akusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – akusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai dvai haitū abyākatā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya  sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’|

paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍cakkhandhā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā  haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau mahābhūtā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau jīvitasaṅkhārā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai cuddasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau khandhā arūpinau sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattāri indriyāni sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai dvai haitū sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai nāmañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayā  haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai imai dvādasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, sampayuttapac‍cayā haunti| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  imai chabbīsati dhammā sahajātapac‍cayā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haunti, nissayapac‍cayā haunti, vippayuttapac‍cayā haunti| gatisampattiyā ñāṇavippayuttai imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti|

khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttai katamaisaṃ chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hauti? kusalakammassa javanakkhaṇai dvai haitū kusalā; tasmiṃ khaṇai jātacaitanāya sahajātapac‍cayā haunti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārā…pai॰… khattiyamahāsālānaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ  kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttai imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ haitūnaṃ pac‍cayā upapatti hautī’’ti|

gatikathā niṭṭhitā|

7. kammakathā

234. ahausi  kammaṃ, ahausi kammavipākau| ahausi kammaṃ, nāhausi kammavipākau| ahausi kammaṃ, atthi kammavipākau| ahausi kammaṃ, natthi kammavipākau| ahausi kammaṃ, bhavissati kammavipākau| ahausi kammaṃ, na bhavissati kammavipākau| [atītakammaṃ]

atthi kammaṃ, atthi kammavipākau| atthi kammaṃ, natthi kammavipākau| atthi kammaṃ, bhavissati kammavipākau| atthi kammaṃ, na bhavissati kammavipākau| [pac‍cuppan‍nakammaṃ]

bhavissati kammaṃ, bhavissati kammavipākau| bhavissati kammaṃ, na bhavissati kammavipākau| [anāgatakammaṃ]

235. ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, ahausi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, nāhausi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, atthi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, natthi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau|

atthi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, atthi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, natthi kusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi kusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau|

bhavissati  kusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau| bhavissati kusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati kusalassa kammassa vipākau|

ahausi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, ahausi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, nāhausi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi  akusalaṃ kammaṃ, atthi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, natthi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati akusalassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati akusalassa kammassa vipākau|

atthi akusalaṃ  kammaṃ, atthi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, natthi akusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati akusalassa kammassa vipākau| atthi akusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati akusalassa kammassa vipākau|

bhavissati akusalaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati  akusalassa kammassa vipākau| bhavissati akusalaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati akusalassa kammassa vipākau|

ahausi sāvaj‍jaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi anavaj‍jaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi kaṇhaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi suk‍kaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi sukhudrayaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi dukkhudrayaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi sukhavipākaṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, ahausi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, nāhausi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, atthi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, natthi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| ahausi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau|

atthi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, atthi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| atthi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, natthi dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| atthi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| atthi dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau|

bhavissati  dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākau| bhavissati dukkhavipākaṃ kammaṃ, na bhavissati dukkhavipākassa kammassa vipākauti|

kammakathā niṭṭhitā|

8. vipal‍lāsakathā

236. purimanidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai a॰ ni॰ 4.49 passitabbā, sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā cittavipal‍lāsā diṭṭhivipal‍lāsā| katamai cattārau? anic‍cai, bhikkhavai, nic‍canti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| dukkhai, bhikkhavai, sukhanti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| anattani, bhikkhavai, attāti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| asubhai, bhikkhavai, subhanti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā cittavipal‍lāsā diṭṭhivipal‍lāsā|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā nacittavipal‍lāsā nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsā| katamai cattārau? anic‍cai, bhikkhavai, anic‍canti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| dukkhai, bhikkhavai, dukkhanti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| anattani, bhikkhavai, anattāti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| asubhai, bhikkhavai, asubhanti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā nacittavipal‍lāsā nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsā’’ti|

‘‘anic‍cai  nic‍casañ‍ñinau, dukkhai ca sukhasañ‍ñinau|

anattani ca attāti attasañ‍ñinau (syā॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.49 passitabbā, asubhai subhasañ‍ñinau|

micchādiṭṭhihatā sattā, khittacittā visañ‍ñinau||

‘‘tai  yaugayuttā mārassa, ayaugakkhaiminau janā|

sattā gacchanti saṃsāraṃ, jātimaraṇagāminau||

‘‘yadā ca buddhā laukasmiṃ, uppaj‍janti pabhaṅkarā|

tai imaṃ dhammaṃ pakāsainti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘taisaṃ sutvāna sappañ‍ñā, sacittaṃ pac‍caladdhu pac‍caladdhuṃ (syā॰), pac‍caladdhā a॰ ni॰ 4.49 tai|

anic‍caṃ anic‍catau dakkhuṃ, dukkhamaddakkhu dukkhatau||

‘‘anattani  anattāti, asubhaṃ asubhataddasuṃ|

sammādiṭṭhisamādānā, sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti||

imai cattārau vipal‍lāsā diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa pahīnā, appahīnāti| kaici pahīnā, kaici appahīnā? anic‍cai nic‍canti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau pahīnau| dukkhai sukhanti sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, cittaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau pahīnau| anattani attāti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau pahīnau| asubhai subhanti sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, cittaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau pahīnau| dvīsu vatthūsu cha vipal‍lāsā pahīnā| dvīsu vatthūsu dvai vipal‍lāsā pahīnā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā appahīnā| catūsu vatthūsu aṭṭha vipal‍lāsā pahīnā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā appahīnāti|

vipal‍lāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

9. maggakathā

237. maggauti  kainaṭṭhaina maggau? sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya uttariṃ paṭivaidhāya (syā॰) maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau micchāsaṅkappassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva  haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari  paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācā micchāvācāya pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya  maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina  sammākammantau micchākammantassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvau micchāājīvassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca…pai॰… paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmau micchāvāyāmassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu  ca…pai॰… upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsati micchāsatiyā pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi micchāsamādhissa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi auḷārikassa kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanassa paṭighasañ‍ñaujanassa auḷārikassa kāmarāgānusayassa paṭighānusayassa pahānāya maggau caiva  haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

anāgāmimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina  sammāsamādhi anusahagatassa kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanassa  paṭighasañ‍ñaujanassa, anusahagatassa kāmarāgānusayassa paṭighānusayassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

arahattamaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi rūparāgassa arūparāgassa mānassa uddhac‍cassa avij‍jāya, mānānusayassa bhavarāgānusayassa avij‍jānusayassa pahānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sahajātānaṃ dhammānaṃ upatthambhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, kilaisānaṃ pariyādānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, paṭivaidhādivisaudhanāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa adhiṭṭhānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, cittassa, vaudānāya maggau caiva haitu ca, visaisādhigamāya maggau caiva haitu ca, uttari paṭivaidhāya maggau caiva haitu ca, sac‍cābhisamayāya maggau caiva, haitu ca, niraudhai patiṭṭhāpanāya maggau caiva haitu ca|

dassanamaggau sammādiṭṭhi, abhiniraupanamaggau sammāsaṅkappau, pariggahamaggau sammāvācā, samuṭṭhānamaggau sammākammantau, vaudānamaggau sammāājīvau, paggahamaggau sammāvāyāmau, upaṭṭhānamaggau sammāsati, avikkhaipamaggau sammāsamādhi| upaṭṭhānamaggau satisambaujjhaṅgau, pavicayamaggau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, paggahamaggau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pharaṇamaggau pītisambaujjhaṅgau, upasamamaggau  passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, avikkhaipamaggau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, paṭisaṅkhānamaggau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

assaddhiyai akampiyamaggau saddhābalaṃ, kausaj‍jai akampiyamaggau vīriyabalaṃ, pamādai akampiyamaggau satibalaṃ, uddhac‍cai akampiyamaggau samādhibalaṃ, avij‍jāya akampiyamaggau pañ‍ñābalaṃ| adhimaukkhamaggau saddhindriyaṃ, paggahamaggau vīriyindriyaṃ, upaṭṭhānamaggau  satindriyaṃ, avikkhaipamaggau samādhindriyaṃ, dassanamaggau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ|

ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyā maggau, akampiyaṭṭhaina balā maggau, niyyānaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā maggau, haituṭṭhaina maggaṅgā maggau, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satipaṭṭhānā maggau, padahanaṭṭhaina sammappadhānā maggau, ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhipādā maggau, tathaṭṭhaina sac‍cāni maggau, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samathau maggau, anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā maggau, aikarasaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā maggau, anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhā maggau, saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi maggau, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi maggau, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi maggau, muttaṭṭhaina vimaukkhau maggau, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina vij‍jā  maggau, paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vimutti maggau, samucchaidaṭṭhaina khayai ñāṇaṃ maggau, chandau mūlaṭṭhaina maggau, manasikārau samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina  maggau, phassau samaudhānaṭṭhaina maggau, vaidanā samausaraṇaṭṭhaina maggau, samādhi pamukhaṭṭhaina maggau, sati ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina maggau, pañ‍ñā tatuttaraṭṭhaina maggau, vimutti sāraṭṭhaina maggau, amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina maggauti|

maggakathā niṭṭhitā|

10. maṇḍapaiyyakathā

238. ‘‘maṇḍapaiyyamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ| satthā sammukhībhūtau tidhattamaṇḍau tividhau maṇḍau (bahūsu) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā| satthari sammukhībhūtai daisanāmaṇḍau, paṭiggahamaṇḍau, brahmacariyamaṇḍau’’|

katamau daisanāmaṇḍau? catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ uttānikammaṃ (ka॰), catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ…pai॰… catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ… catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ  balānaṃ… sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ… ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā  paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ – ayaṃ daisanāmaṇḍau|

katamau paṭiggahamaṇḍau? bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau daivā manussā yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici viñ‍ñātārau – ayaṃ paṭiggahamaṇḍau|

katamau brahmacariyamaṇḍau? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ brahmacariyamaṇḍau|

239. adhimaukkhamaṇḍau saddhindriyaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭau; assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhamaṇḍaṃ  pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau vīriyindriyaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau; kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā vīriyindriyassa paggahamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau satindriyaṃ, pamādau kasaṭau; pamādaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā satindriyassa upaṭṭhānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau samādhindriyaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā  samādhindriyassa avikkhaipamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| dassanamaṇḍau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, avij‍jaṃ kasaṭau; avij‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā pañ‍ñindriyassa dassanamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ|

assaddhiyai akampiyamaṇḍau saddhābalaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭau; assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭaṃ  chaḍḍaitvā saddhābalassa assaddhiyai akampiyamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| kausaj‍jai akampiyamaṇḍau vīriyabalaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau; kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā vīriyabalassa kausaj‍jai akampiyamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| pamādai akampiyamaṇḍau satibalaṃ, pamādau kasaṭau; pamādaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā satibalassa pamādai akampiyamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| uddhac‍cai akampiyamaṇḍau samādhibalaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā samādhibalassa uddhac‍cai akampiyamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| avij‍jāya akampiyamaṇḍau pañ‍ñābalaṃ, avij‍jā kasaṭau; avij‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā pañ‍ñābalassa avij‍jāya akampiyamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ|

upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau  satisambaujjhaṅgā, pamādau kasaṭau; pamādaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā satisambaujjhaṅgassa upaṭṭhānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| pavicayamaṇḍau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, avij‍jā kasaṭau; avij‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa pavicayamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau; kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa paggahamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| pharaṇamaṇḍau pītisambaujjhaṅgau, pariḷāhau kasaṭau; pariḷāhaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa pharaṇamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| upasamamaṇḍau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, duṭṭhul‍laṃ kasaṭau; duṭṭhul‍laṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa  upasamamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa avikkhaipamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| paṭisaṅkhānamaṇḍau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, appaṭisaṅkhā kasaṭau; appaṭisaṅkhaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa paṭisaṅkhānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ|

dassanamaṇḍau sammādiṭṭhi, micchādiṭṭhi kasaṭau; micchādiṭṭhiṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammādiṭṭhiyā dassanamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| abhiniraupanamaṇḍau sammāsaṅkappau, micchāsaṅkappau kasaṭau; micchāsaṅkappaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāsaṅkappassa abhiniraupanamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| pariggahamaṇḍau  sammāvācā, micchāvācā kasaṭau; micchāvācaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāvācāya pariggahamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| samuṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammākammantau, micchākammantau kasaṭau; micchākammantaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammākammantassa samuṭṭhānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| vaudānamaṇḍau sammāājīvau, micchāājīvau kasaṭau; micchāājīvaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāājīvassa vaudānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau sammāvāyāmau, micchāvāyāmau kasaṭau; micchāvāyāmaṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāvāyāmassa paggahamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammāsati, micchāsati kasaṭau; micchāsatiṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāsatiyā upaṭṭhānamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau  sammāsamādhi, micchāsamādhi kasaṭau; micchāsamādhiṃ kasaṭaṃ chaḍḍaitvā sammāsamādhissa avikkhaipamaṇḍaṃ pivatīti – maṇḍapaiyyaṃ|

240. atthi maṇḍau, atthi paiyyaṃ, atthi kasaṭau| adhimaukkhamaṇḍau saddhindriyaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau  – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau vīriyindriyaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau  dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau satindriyaṃ, pamādau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau samādhindriyaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| dassanamaṇḍau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, avij‍jā kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ|

assaddhiyai akampiyamaṇḍau saddhābalaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| kausaj‍jai akampiyamaṇḍau vīriyabalaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| pamādai akampiyamaṇḍau satibalaṃ, pamādau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| uddhac‍cai akampiyamaṇḍau samādhibalaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| avij‍jāya akampiyamaṇḍau pañ‍ñābalaṃ, avij‍jā kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau  vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ|

upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau satisambaujjhaṅgau, pamādau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| pavicayamaṇḍau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, avij‍jā kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, kausaj‍jaṃ kasaṭau;  yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| pharaṇamaṇḍau pītisambaujjhaṅgau, pariḷāhau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| upasamamaṇḍau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, duṭṭhul‍laṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, uddhac‍caṃ kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| paṭisaṅkhānamaṇḍau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, apaṭisaṅkhā kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ|

dassanamaṇḍau sammādiṭṭhi, micchādiṭṭhi kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| abhiniraupanamaṇḍau sammāsaṅkappau, micchāsaṅkappau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ  paiyyaṃ| pariggahamaṇḍau sammāvācā, micchāvācā kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau  dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| samuṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammākammantau, micchākammantau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| vaudānamaṇḍau  sammāājīvau, micchāājīvau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| paggahamaṇḍau sammāvāyāmau, micchāvāyāmau kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammāsati, micchāsati kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ| avikkhaipamaṇḍau sammāsamādhi, micchāsamādhi kasaṭau; yau tattha attharasau dhammarasau vimuttirasau – idaṃ paiyyaṃ|

dassanamaṇḍau sammādiṭṭhi… abhiniraupanamaṇḍau sammāsaṅkappau… pariggahamaṇḍau sammāvācā… samuṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammākammantau… vaudānamaṇḍau sammāājīvau… paggahamaṇḍau sammāvāyāmau… upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau sammāsati… avikkhaipamaṇḍau sammāsamādhi|

upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau satisambaujjhaṅgau… pavicayamaṇḍau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau… paggahamaṇḍau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau… pharaṇamaṇḍau pītisambaujjhaṅgau… upasamamaṇḍau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau… avikkhaipamaṇḍau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau… paṭisaṅkhānamaṇḍau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

assaddhiyai akampiyamaṇḍau saddhābalaṃ… kausaj‍jai akampiyamaṇḍau vīriyabalaṃ… pamādai akampiyamaṇḍau satibalaṃ… uddhac‍cai akampiyamaṇḍau samādhibalaṃ… avij‍jāya akampiyamaṇḍau pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

adhimaukkhamaṇḍau  saddhindriyaṃ… paggahamaṇḍau vīriyindriyaṃ… upaṭṭhānamaṇḍau satindriyaṃ… avikkhaipamaṇḍau  samādhindriyaṃ… dassanamaṇḍau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ|

ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyā maṇḍau, akampiyaṭṭhaina balā maṇḍau, niyyānaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā maṇḍau, haituṭṭhaina maggau maṇḍau, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satipaṭṭhānā maṇḍau, padahanaṭṭhaina sammappadhānā maṇḍau, ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhipādā maṇḍau, tathaṭṭhaina sac‍cā maṇḍau, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samathau maṇḍau, anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā maṇḍau, aikarasaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā maṇḍau, anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhā maṇḍau, saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi maṇḍau, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi maṇḍau, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi maṇḍau, muttaṭṭhaina  vimaukkhau  maṇḍau, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina vij‍jā maṇḍau, paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vimutti maṇḍau, samucchaidaṭṭhaina khayai ñāṇaṃ maṇḍau, paṭippassaddhaṭṭhaina anuppādai ñāṇaṃ maṇḍau, chandau mūlaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, manasikārau samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, phassau samaudhānaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, vaidanā samausaraṇaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, samādhi pamukhaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, sati ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina maṇḍau, pañ‍ñā tatuttaraṭṭhaina maṇḍau, vimutti sāraṭṭhaina maṇḍau, amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina maṇḍauti|

catutthabhāṇavārau|

maṇḍapaiyyakathā niṭṭhitā|

mahāvaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ñāṇadiṭṭhī  ca assāsā, indriyaṃ vimaukkhapañ‍camā|

gatikammavipal‍lāsā, maggau maṇḍaina tai dasāti||

aisa nikāyadharaihi ṭhapitau, asamau assamau (syā॰) paṭhamau pavarau

varavaggauti varamaggauti (syā॰)|


2. yuganaddhavaggau

1. yuganaddhakathā
