1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā ānandau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā mama santikai arahattapattaṃ arahattaṃ (syā॰), arahattapattiṃ a॰ ni॰ 4.170 byākarauti, sabbasau catūhi maggaihi aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina| katamaihi catūhi?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti bahuliṃ karauti (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.170 passitabbā| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ  maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ yuganandhaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) bhāvaiti| tassa samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ  bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau  bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau dhammuddhac‍caviggahitaṃ mānasaṃ hauti| sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattamaiva ajjhattañ‍ñaiva (syā॰ ka॰) santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi  hauti samādhiyati| tassa maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā mama santikai arahattapattaṃ byākarauti, sabbasau imaihi catūhi maggaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñatarainā’’ti|

1. suttantaniddaisau

2. kathaṃ samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaiti? naikkhammavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| tattha jātai dhammai anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau  anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| iti paṭhamaṃ samathau, pacchā vipassanā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati? dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi maggau sañ‍jāyati, abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau maggau sañ‍jāyati, pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācā maggau sañ‍jāyati, samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantau maggau sañ‍jāyati, vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvau maggau sañ‍jāyati, paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmau maggau sañ‍jāyati, upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsati maggau sañ‍jāyati, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi maggau sañ‍jāyati – aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati|

sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti āsaivatīti kathaṃ āsaivati? āvaj‍jantau āsaivati, jānantau āsaivati, passantau āsaivati, pac‍cavaikkhantau āsaivati, cittaṃ  adhiṭṭhahantau āsaivati, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau āsaivati, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau āsaivati, satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaintau āsaivati, cittaṃ samādahantau āsaivati, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau āsaivati, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  abhijānantau āsaivati, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau āsaivati, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau āsaivati, bhāvaitabbaṃ  bhāvaintau āsaivati, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau āsaivati – aivaṃ āsaivati|

bhāvaitīti kathaṃ bhāvaiti? āvaj‍jantau bhāvaiti, jānantau bhāvaiti, passantau bhāvaiti, pac‍cavaikkhantau bhāvaiti, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahantau bhāvaiti, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau bhāvaiti, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau bhāvaiti, satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaintau bhāvaiti, cittaṃ samādahantau bhāvaiti, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau bhāvaiti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau bhāvaiti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau bhāvaiti, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau bhāvaiti, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvaintau bhāvaiti, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau bhāvaiti – aivaṃ bhāvaiti|

bahulīkarautīti kathaṃ bahulīkarauti? āvaj‍jantau bahulīkarauti, jānantau bahulīkarauti, passantau bahulīkarauti, pac‍cavaikkhantau bahulīkarauti, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahantau bahulīkarauti, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau bahulīkarauti, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau bahulīkarauti, satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaintau bahulīkarauti, cittaṃ samādahantau bahulīkarauti, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau bahulīkarauti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau bahulīkarauti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau bahulīkarauti, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau bahulīkarauti, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvaintau bahulīkarauti, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau bahulīkarauti – aivaṃ bahulīkarauti|

tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti  anusayā byantīhauntīti kathaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti? sautāpattimaggaina, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau – imāni tīṇi sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikaṃ kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ – imāni dvai sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; auḷārikau kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| anāgāmimaggaina anusahagataṃ kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ  – imāni dvai sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; anusahagatau kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| arahattamaggaina rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni pañ‍ca sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau – imai tayau anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

3. abyāpādavasaina  cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi…pai॰… ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| tattha jātai dhammai anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau  anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| iti paṭhamaṃ samathau, pacchā vipassanā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati? dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi maggau sañ‍jāyati, abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati|

sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti āsaivatīti kathaṃ āsaivati? āvaj‍jantau āsaivati…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau āsaivati, aivaṃ āsaivati| bhāvaitīti kathaṃ bhāvaiti? āvaj‍jantau bhāvaiti, jānantau bhāvaiti…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau bhāvaiti, aivaṃ bhāvaiti| bahulīkarautīti kathaṃ bahulīkarauti? āvaj‍jantau bahulīkarauti, jānantau bahulīkarauti…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau bahulīkarauti, aivaṃ bahulīkarauti|

tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhauntīti kathaṃ sañ‍ñaujanā pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti? sautāpattimaggaina  sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau – imāni tīṇi sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikaṃ kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ – imāni dvai sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; auḷārikau kāmarāgānusayau paṭighānusayau  – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| anāgāmimaggaina anusahagataṃ kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ – imāni dvai sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; anusahagatau kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau – imai dvai anusayā byantīhaunti| arahattamaggaina rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni pañ‍ca sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti; mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau – imai tayau anusayā  byantīhaunti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaiti|

4. kathaṃ vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti? anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānañ‍ca vausaggārammaṇatā vaussaggārammaṇatā (syā॰ ka॰) cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau| samādhi iti paṭhamaṃ vipassanā, pacchā samathau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti  catassau bhāvanā – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

rūpaṃ anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, rūpaṃ dukkhatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, rūpaṃ anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānañ‍ca vausaggārammaṇatā cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| iti paṭhamaṃ vipassanā, pacchā samathau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ … saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā, jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau…pai॰… anattatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā| tattha jātānaṃ dhammānañ‍ca vausaggārammaṇatā cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi| iti paṭhamaṃ vipassanā, pacchā samathau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ  maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ  bhāvaiti|

5. kathaṃ samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? sauḷasahi ākāraihi samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti| ārammaṇaṭṭhaina gaucaraṭṭhaina pahānaṭṭhaina paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina vivaṭṭanaṭṭhaina santaṭṭhaina paṇītaṭṭhaina vimuttaṭṭhaina anāsavaṭṭhaina taraṇaṭṭhaina animittaṭṭhaina appaṇihitaṭṭhaina suñ‍ñataṭṭhaina aikarasaṭṭhaina anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhaṭṭhaina|

kathaṃ  ārammaṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhārammaṇau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhārammaṇā| iti ārammaṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘ārammaṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ ārammaṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti|

kathaṃ gaucaraṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti gaucaraṭṭhaina  samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘gaucaraṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ pahānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍casahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti pahānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘pahānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍casahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca paric‍cajatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca paric‍cajatau anupassanaṭṭhaina  vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍casahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca  vuṭṭhahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ  vivaṭṭanaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍casahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vivaṭṭatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vivaṭṭatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti vivaṭṭanaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vivaṭṭanaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ santaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi santau haunti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau  anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā santā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti santaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘santaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ paṇītaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi paṇītau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā paṇītā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti paṇītaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘paṇītaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ vimuttaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi kāmāsavā vimuttau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā avij‍jāsavā vimuttā  hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñā vimuttaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vimuttaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ anāsavaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi kāmāsavaina anāsavau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā avij‍jāsavaina anāsavā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti anāsavaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘anāsavaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ  taraṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍casahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca taratau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca taratau anupassanaṭṭhaina  vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti taraṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘taraṇaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ animittaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ  bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi sabbanimittaihi animittau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā sabbanimittaihi animittā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti animittaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘animittaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ appaṇihitaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi sabbapaṇidhīhi appaṇihitau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā sabbapaṇidhīhi appaṇihitā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti appaṇihitaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘appaṇihitaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

kathaṃ  suñ‍ñataṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti? uddhac‍caṃ pajahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi sabbābhinivaisaihi suñ‍ñau hauti niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jaṃ pajahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā  sabbābhinivaisaihi suñ‍ñā hauti niraudhagaucarā| iti suñ‍ñataṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘suñ‍ñataṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| bhāvaitīti catassau bhāvanā – āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… maggau sañ‍jāyatīti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ suñ‍ñataṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti| imaihi sauḷasahi ākāraihi samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti, aivaṃ samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti|

suttantaniddaisau|

2. dhammuddhac‍cavāraniddaisau

6. kathaṃ  dhammuddhac‍caviggahitaṃ mānasaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau aubhāsau uppaj‍jati, aubhāsau  dhammauti aubhāsaṃ āvaj‍jati, tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, dukkhatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammuddhac‍caviggahitamānasau hauti sau samayau, yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattamaiva santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| tassa maggau sañ‍jāyatī’’ti kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati, aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

anic‍catau manasikarautau ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, pīti uppaj‍jati, passaddhi uppaj‍jati, sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati, adhimaukkhau uppaj‍jati, paggahau uppaj‍jati, upaṭṭhānaṃ uppaj‍jati, upaikkhā uppaj‍jati, nikanti uppaj‍jati, ‘nikanti dhammau’ti nikantiṃ āvaj‍jati| tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, dukkhatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammuddhac‍caviggahitamānasau  hauti sau samayau, yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattamaiva santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| tassa maggau sañ‍jāyatī’’ti| kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati, aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

dukkhatau manasikarautau…pai॰… anattatau manasikarautau aubhāsau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, pīti uppaj‍jati, passaddhi uppaj‍jati, sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati, adhimaukkhau uppaj‍jati, paggahau uppaj‍jati, upaṭṭhānaṃ uppaj‍jati, upaikkhā uppaj‍jati, nikanti uppaj‍jati, ‘nikanti dhammau’ti nikantiṃ āvaj‍jati| tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau  anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ, anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ, dukkhatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammuddhac‍caviggahitamānasau…pai॰… aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti’’|

rūpaṃ anic‍catau manasikarautau…pai॰… rūpaṃ dukkhatau manasikarautau… rūpaṃ anattatau manasikarautau… vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau  manasikarautau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau manasikarautau, jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau manasikarautau aubhāsau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, pīti uppaj‍jati, passaddhi uppaj‍jati, sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati, adhimaukkhau uppaj‍jati, paggahau uppaj‍jati, upaṭṭhānaṃ uppaj‍jati, upaikkhā uppaj‍jati, nikanti uppaj‍jati, ‘nikanti dhammau’ti nikantiṃ āvaj‍jati| tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau| jarāmaraṇaṃ anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| jarāmaraṇaṃ anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammuddhac‍caviggahitamānasau hauti| sau samayau, yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattamaiva santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| tassa maggau sañ‍jāyatī’’ti| kathaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati…pai॰… aivaṃ maggau sañ‍jāyati| aivaṃ sañ‍ñaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti| aivaṃ dhammuddhac‍caviggahitaṃ  mānasaṃ hauti|

7.

 aubhāsai caiva ñāṇai ca, pītiyā ca vikampati|

passaddhiyā sukhai caiva, yaihi cittaṃ pavaidhati||

adhimaukkhai  ca paggāhai, upaṭṭhānai ca kampati|

upaikkhāvaj‍janāya caiva, upaikkhāya ca nikantiyā||

imāni dasa ṭhānāni, pañ‍ñā yassa paric‍citā|

dhammuddhac‍cakusalau hauti, na ca sammauha gacchati||

vikkhipati caiva kilissati ca, cavati cittabhāvanā|

vikkhipati  na kilissati, bhāvanā parihāyati||

vikkhipati na kilissati, bhāvanā na parihāyati|

na ca vikkhipatai cittaṃ na kilissati, na cavati cittabhāvanā||

imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi cittassa saṅkhaipavikkhaipaviggahitaṃ saṅkhaipaṃ vikkhaipaṃ viggahitaṃ (syā॰) dasa ṭhānai sampajānātīti|

yuganaddhakathā niṭṭhitā|

2. sac‍cakathā

8. purimanidānaṃ| ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni’’|

1. paṭhamasuttantaniddaisau

kathaṃ dukkhaṃ tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ? cattārau dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau, saṅkhataṭṭhau, santāpaṭṭhau, vipariṇāmaṭṭhau – imai cattārau dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ dukkhaṃ tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

kathaṃ samudayau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ? cattārau samudayassa samudayaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau, nidānaṭṭhau, saṃyaugaṭṭhau  palibaudhaṭṭhau – imai cattārau samudayassa samudayaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ samudayau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

kathaṃ niraudhau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ? cattārau niraudhassa  niraudhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau, vivaikaṭṭhau, asaṅkhataṭṭhau, amataṭṭhau – imai cattārau niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ niraudhau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

kathaṃ maggau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ? cattārau maggassa maggaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| maggassa  niyyānaṭṭhau, haituṭṭhau, dassanaṭṭhau, ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau – imai cattārau maggassa maggaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ maggau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

9. katihākāraihi  cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? catūhākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| tathaṭṭhaina, anattaṭṭhaina, sac‍caṭṭhaina, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina – imaihi catūhākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? catūhākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, maggassa maggaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau – imaihi catūhākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ anattaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? catūhākāraihi anattaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau anattaṭṭhau, samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau anattaṭṭhau, niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau anattaṭṭhau, maggassa maggaṭṭhau anattaṭṭhau – imaihi catūhākāraihi anattaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ sac‍caṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? catūhākāraihi sac‍caṭṭhaina  cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau sac‍caṭṭhau, samudayassa  samudayaṭṭhau sac‍caṭṭhau, niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau sac‍caṭṭhau, maggassa  maggaṭṭhau sac‍caṭṭhau – imaihi catūhākāraihi sac‍caṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? catūhākāraihi paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, maggassa maggaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau – imaihi catūhākāraihi paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri  sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

10. katihākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? yaṃ anic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ taṃ anic‍caṃ, yaṃ anic‍cañ‍ca dukkhañ‍ca taṃ anattā| yaṃ anic‍cañ‍ca dukkhañ‍ca anattā ca taṃ tathaṃ| yaṃ anic‍caṃ ca dukkhañ‍ca anattā ca tathañ‍ca taṃ sac‍caṃ| yaṃ anic‍cañ‍ca dukkhañ‍ca anattā ca tathañ‍ca sac‍cañ‍ca taṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

katihākāraihi  cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? navahākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| tathaṭṭhaina, anattaṭṭhaina, sac‍caṭṭhaina, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina, abhiñ‍ñaṭṭhaina, pariñ‍ñaṭṭhaina, pahānaṭṭhaina, bhāvanaṭṭhaina, sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina – imaihi navahākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? navahākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, maggassa maggaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, abhiñ‍ñāya abhiñ‍ñaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, pariñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, pahānassa pahānaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, bhāvanāya bhāvanaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau, sacchikiriyāya sacchikiriyaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau – imaihi navahākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ  anattaṭṭhaina… sac‍caṭṭhaina… paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? navahākāraihi paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, samudayassa samudayaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, maggassa maggaṭṭhau  paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, abhiñ‍ñāya abhiñ‍ñaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, pariñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, pahānassa pahānaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, bhāvanāya bhāvanaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau, sacchikiriyāya sacchikiriyaṭṭhau paṭivaidhaṭṭhau – imaihi navahākāraihi paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

11. katihākāraihi  cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? dvādasahi ākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| tathaṭṭhaina, anattaṭṭhaina, sac‍caṭṭhaina, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina, abhijānanaṭṭhaina, parijānanaṭṭhaina, dhammaṭṭhaina, tathaṭṭhaina, ñātaṭṭhaina, sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina, phassanaṭṭhaina, abhisamayaṭṭhaina – imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? sauḷasahi ākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau, saṅkhataṭṭhau, santāpaṭṭhau, vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, tathaṭṭhau; samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau, nidānaṭṭhau, saṃyaugaṭṭhau, palibaudhaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau; niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau, vivaikaṭṭhau, asaṅkhataṭṭhau, amataṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau; maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau, haituṭṭhau, dassanaṭṭhau, ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau tathaṭṭhau – imaihi  sauḷasahi ākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

kathaṃ  anattaṭṭhaina…pai॰… sac‍caṭṭhaina… paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina… abhijānanaṭṭhaina… parijānanaṭṭhaina… dhammaṭṭhaina… tathaṭṭhaina… ñātaṭṭhaina… sacchikiriyaṭṭhaina… phassanaṭṭhaina… abhisamayaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni? sauḷasahi ākāraihi abhisamayaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau, saṅkhataṭṭhau, santāpaṭṭhau, vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, abhisamayaṭṭhau; samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau, nidānaṭṭhau, saṃyaugaṭṭhau, palibaudhaṭṭhau, abhisamayaṭṭhau; niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau, vivaikaṭṭhau, asaṅkhataṭṭhau, amataṭṭhau, abhisamayaṭṭhau; maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau, haituṭṭhau, dassanaṭṭhau, ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau, abhisamayaṭṭhau – imaihi sauḷasahi ākāraihi abhisamayaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ taṃ aikattaṃ| aikattaṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti – cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhāni|

12. sac‍cānaṃ kati lakkhaṇāni? sac‍cānaṃ dvai lakkhaṇāni| saṅkhatalakkhaṇañ‍ca, asaṅkhatalakkhaṇañ‍ca – sac‍cānaṃ imāni dvai lakkhaṇāni|

sac‍cānaṃ kati lakkhaṇāni? sac‍cānaṃ cha lakkhaṇāni| saṅkhatānaṃ sac‍cānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ  pañ‍ñāyati asaṅkhatassa sac‍cassa na uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, na vayau pañ‍ñāyati, na ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati – sac‍cānaṃ imāni cha lakkhaṇāni|

sac‍cānaṃ kati lakkhaṇāni? sac‍cānaṃ dvādasa lakkhaṇāni| dukkhasac‍cassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati; samudayasac‍cassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati; maggasac‍cassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati; niraudhasac‍cassa na uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, na vayau pañ‍ñāyati, na ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati – sac‍cānaṃ imāni dvādasa lakkhaṇāni|

catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā? samudayasac‍caṃ akusalaṃ, maggasac‍caṃ kusalaṃ, niraudhasac‍caṃ abyākataṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

siyā tīṇi sac‍cāni aikasac‍caina saṅgahitāni, aikasac‍caṃ tīhi sac‍caihi saṅgahitaṃ? vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti| kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ akusalaṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ akusalaṃ – aivaṃ akusalaṭṭhaina dvai sac‍cāni aikasac‍caina saṅgahitāni, aikasac‍caṃ dvīhi sac‍caihi saṅgahitaṃ| yaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ kusalaṃ, maggasac‍caṃ kusalaṃ – aivaṃ kusalaṭṭhaina dvai sac‍cāni aikasac‍caina saṅgahitāni, aikasac‍caṃ dvīhi sac‍caihi saṅgahitaṃ| yaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ abyākataṃ, niraudhasac‍caṃ abyākataṃ – aivaṃ abyākataṭṭhaina dvai sac‍cāni aikasac‍caina saṅgahitāni, aikasac‍caṃ dvīhi sac‍caihi saṅgahitaṃ| aivaṃ siyā tīṇi sac‍cāni aikasac‍caina saṅgahitāni, aikasac‍caṃ tīhi sac‍caihi saṅgahitaṃ vatthuvasaina pariyāyaināti|

2. dutiyasuttantapāḷi

13. ‘‘pubbai  mai, bhikkhavai saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.26, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau rūpassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau sañ‍ñāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau saṅkhārānaṃ assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau  rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa assādau| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa ādīnavau| yau rūpasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ’’|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca  ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ abhisambuddhau pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ (syā॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.26 passitabbā yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (syā॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.27 passitabbā| ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti|

3. dutiyasuttantaniddaisau

14. yaṃ  rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ rūpassa assādauti – pahānappaṭivaidhau samudayasac‍caṃ| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ rūpassa ādīnavauti – pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau dukkhasac‍caṃ| yau rūpasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇanti – sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhau niraudhasac‍caṃ| yā imaisu tīsu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhi saṅkappau vācā kammantau ājīvau vāyāmau sati samādhi – bhāvanāpaṭivaidhau maggasac‍caṃ|

yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa  assādauti – pahānappaṭivaidhau samudayasac‍caṃ| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavauti – pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau dukkhasac‍caṃ| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇanti – sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhau niraudhasac‍caṃ| yā imaisu  tīsu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhi saṅkappau vācā kammantau ājīvau vāyāmau sati samādhi – bhāvanāpaṭivaidhau maggasac‍caṃ|

15. sac‍canti katihākāraihi sac‍caṃ? aisanaṭṭhaina, pariggahaṭṭhaina, paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina| kathaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ? jarāmaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavanti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| jarāmaraṇaṃ jātinidānaṃ, jātisamudayaṃ, jātijātikaṃ, jātippabhavanti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| jarāmaraṇañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

jāti kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavāti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| jāti bhavanidānā, bhavasamudayā, bhavajātikā, bhavappabhavāti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| jātiñ‍ca pajānāti, jātisamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jātiniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, jātiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

bhavau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavauti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| bhavau upādānanidānau, upādānasamudayau, upādānajātikau, upādānappabhavauti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| bhavaṃ ca pajānāti, bhavasamudayañ‍ca  pajānāti, bhavaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, bhavaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

upādānaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavanti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| upādānaṃ taṇhānidānaṃ, taṇhāsamudayaṃ, taṇhājātikaṃ, taṇhāpabhavanti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| upādānañ‍ca pajānāti, upādānasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, upādānaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti upādānaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

taṇhā kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavāti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| taṇhā vaidanānidānā, vaidanāsamudayā, vaidanājātikā, vaidanāpabhavāti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| taṇhañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāsamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

vaidanā  kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavāti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| vaidanā phassanidānā, phassasamudayā, phassajātikā, phassappabhavāti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| vaidanañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāsamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

phassau  kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavauti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| phassau saḷāyatananidānau, saḷāyatanasamudayau, saḷāyatanajātikau, saḷāyatanappabhavauti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| phassañ‍ca pajānāti, phassasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, phassaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, phassaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

saḷāyatanaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavanti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpanidānaṃ, nāmarūpasamudayaṃ, nāmarūpajātikaṃ, nāmarūpappabhavanti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| saḷāyatanañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatanasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatananiraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatananiraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

nāmarūpaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavanti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanidānaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇajātikaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇappabhavanti  – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| nāmarūpañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

viñ‍ñāṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavanti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhāranidānaṃ, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ, saṅkhārajātikaṃ, saṅkhārappabhavanti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

saṅkhārā  kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavāti – aivaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| saṅkhārā avij‍jānidānā, avij‍jāsamudayā, avij‍jājātikā, avij‍jāpabhavāti – aivaṃ pariggahaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| saṅkhārai ca pajānāti, saṅkhārasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aivaṃ paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ|

16. jarāmaraṇaṃ  dukkhasac‍caṃ, jāti samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| jāti dukkhasac‍caṃ, bhavau samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| bhavau dukkhasac‍caṃ, upādānaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā  maggasac‍caṃ| upādānaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, taṇhā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| taṇhā dukkhasac‍caṃ, vaidanā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| vaidanā dukkhasac‍caṃ, phassau samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| phassau dukkhasac‍caṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| saḷāyatanaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, nāmarūpaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| nāmarūpaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, saṅkhārā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| saṅkhārā dukkhasac‍caṃ, avij‍jā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ|

jarāmaraṇaṃ  siyā dukkhasac‍caṃ, siyā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍caṃ| jāti siyā dukkhasac‍caṃ, siyā samudayasac‍caṃ…pai॰… bhavau siyā dukkhasac‍caṃ, siyā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhappajānanā maggasac‍canti|

bhāṇavārau|

sac‍cakathā niṭṭhitā|

3. baujjhaṅgakathā

17.  sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.200, baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā’’|

baujjhaṅgāti kainaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā? baudhāya saṃvattantīti – baujjhaṅgā| bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anubujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭibujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sambujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

bujjhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, anubujjhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, paṭibujjhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, sambujjhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā|

baudhaintīti – baujjhaṅgā| anubaudhaintīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭibaudhaintīti – baujjhaṅgā| sambaudhaintīti – baujjhaṅgā|

baudhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, anubaudhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, paṭibaudhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, sambaudhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā|

baudhipakkhiyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, anubaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, paṭibaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, sambaudhipakkhiyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā|

buddhilabhanaṭṭhaina  baujjhaṅgā, buddhipaṭilabhanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, buddhiraupanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, buddhiabhiraupanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, buddhipāpanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, buddhisampāpanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā|

mūlamūlakādidasakaṃ

18. mūlaṭṭhaina  baujjhaṅgā, mūlacariyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlapariggahaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlaparivāraṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlaparipūraṇaṭṭhaina mūlaparipūraṭṭhaina (syā॰ ka॰) aivamuparipi baujjhaṅgā, mūlaparipākaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaina  baujjhaṅgā, mūlapaṭisambhidāpāpanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, mūlapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvappattānampi baujjhaṅgā|

haituṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitucariyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitupariggahaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haituparivāraṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haituparipūraṇaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haituparipākaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitupaṭisambhidaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitupaṭisambhidāpāpanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitupaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, haitupaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvappattānampi baujjhaṅgā|

pac‍cayaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayacariyaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayapariggahaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayaparivāraṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayaparipūraṇaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayaparipākaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayapaṭisambhidāpāpanaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā, pac‍cayapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvappattānampi baujjhaṅgā|

19. mūlaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| haituṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| pac‍cayaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| visuddhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anavaj‍jaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| naikkhammaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimuttaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anāsavaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vivaikaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vausaggaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

mūlacariyaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| haitucariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| pac‍cayacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| visuddhicariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anavaj‍jacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| naikkhammacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimutticariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anāsavacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vivaikacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vausaggacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

mūlapariggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggapariggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlaparivāraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggaparivāraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlaparipūraṇaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggaparipūraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlaparipākaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggaparipākaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggapaṭisambhidaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlapaṭisambhidāpāpanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggapaṭisambhidāpāpanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| mūlapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vausaggapaṭisambhidāya vasībhāvaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰…|

pariggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| parivāraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paripūraṇaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| aikaggaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| avisāraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anāvilaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| aniñ‍janaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| aikattupaṭṭhānavasaina cittassa ṭhitaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ārammaṇaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| gaucaraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| pahānaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paric‍cāgaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vivaṭṭanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| santaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṇītaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimuttaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anāsavaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| taraṇaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| animittaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| appaṇihitaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| suñ‍ñataṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| aikarasaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anativattanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| yuganaddhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niyyānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| haituṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

samathassa avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vipassanāya anupassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti  – baujjhaṅgā| samathavipassanānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| yuganaddhassa anativattanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sikkhāya samādānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti  – baujjhaṅgā| ārammaṇassa gaucaraṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| līnassa cittassa paggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| uddhatassa cittassa niggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ubhauvisuddhānaṃ ajjhupaikkhanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| visaisādhigamaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| uttari paṭivaidhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sac‍cābhisamayaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niraudhai patiṭṭhāpakaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyassa dassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| saddhābalassa assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… pañ‍ñābalassa avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| satisambaujjhaṅgassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sammādiṭṭhiyā dassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… sammāsamādhissa avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

indriyānaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| balānaṃ akampiyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niyyānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| maggassa haituṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| satipaṭṭhānānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sammappadhānānaṃ padahanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| iddhipādānaṃ ijjhanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sac‍cānaṃ tathaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| payaugānaṃ paṭippassaddhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| phalānaṃ sacchikiriyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

patāpanaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| viraucanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| kilaisānaṃ santāpanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| amalaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimalaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| nimmalaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samayaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vivaikaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vivaikacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| virāgaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| virāgacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niraudhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niraudhacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vausaggaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vausaggacariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimuttaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimutticariyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

chandaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa mūlaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa pādaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa padhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa ijjhanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa adhimaukkhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa paggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| chandassa dassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

vīriyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… cittaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… vīmaṃsaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya mūlaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya pādaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya padhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya ijjhanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya adhimaukkhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya paggahaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya avikkhaipaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vīmaṃsāya dassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

dukkhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dukkhassa  saṅkhataṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dukkhassa santāpaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dukkhassa vipariṇāmaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samudayaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhaṃ nidānaṭṭhaṃ sañ‍ñaugaṭṭhaṃ palibaudhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niraudhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhaṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ asaṅkhataṭṭhaṃ amataṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| maggaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| maggassa niyyānaṭṭhaṃ haituṭṭhaṃ dassanaṭṭhaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

tathaṭṭhaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anattaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sac‍caṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭivaidhaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| abhijānanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| parijānanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dhammaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dhātuṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ñātaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| sacchikiriyaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| phassanaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| abhisamayaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

naikkhammaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| abyāpādaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| avikkhaipaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dhammavavatthānaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ñāṇaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| pāmauj‍jaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… arahattamaggaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| arahattaphalasamāpattiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

adhimaukkhaṭṭhaina saddhindriyaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… dassanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| assaddhiyai akampiyaṭṭhaina saddhābalaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… avij‍jāya akampiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñābalaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭhaina upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| akampiyaṭṭhaina balaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| niyyānaṭṭhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| haituṭṭhaina maggaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina satipaṭṭhānaṃ bujjhantīti  – baujjhaṅgā| padahanaṭṭhaina sammappadhānaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhipādaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samathaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anupassanaṭṭhai vipassanaṃ  …pai॰… aikarasaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| anativattanaṭṭhaina yuganaddhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| muttaṭṭhaina vimaukkhaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭivaidhaṭṭhaina vij‍jaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina vimuttiṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samucchaidaṭṭhaina khayai ñāṇaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| paṭippassaddhaṭṭhaina anuppādai ñāṇaṃ bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

chandaṃ mūlaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| manasikāraṃ samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| phassaṃ samaudhānaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vaidanaṃ samausaraṇaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| samādhiṃ pamukhaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| satiṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina  bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| pañ‍ñaṃ tatuttaraṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| vimuttiṃ sāraṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā| amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyausānaṭṭhaina bujjhantīti – baujjhaṅgā|

20. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavau’’ti āvusauti (syā॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.185 passitabbā| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘sattimai, āvusau, baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, āvusau, satta baujjhaṅgā| imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharāmi| yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi majjhanhikasamayaṃ…pai॰… sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina sāyanhasamayaṃ  viharāmi| satisambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, ‘āvusau, hauti, appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti| tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ tiṭṭhantaṃ caraṃ (syā॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.185 passitabbā ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti| tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍakau pūrau assa| sau yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva  dussayugaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya| yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya majjhanhikasamayaṃ…pai॰… sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya| aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, āvusau, imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina  taina baujjhaṅgaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharāmi| yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi majjhanhikasamayaṃ…pai॰… sāyanhasamayaṃ  viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina sāyanhasamayaṃ viharāmi| satisambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti| tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti| tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi’’|

suttantaniddaisau

21. kathaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau? yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā satisambaujjhaṅgau| iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau yāvatā ac‍ci tāvatā vaṇṇau, yāvatā vaṇṇau tāvatā ac‍ci| aivamaiva yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā satisambaujjhaṅgau| iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ  appamāṇau iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau? pamāṇabaddhā pamāṇavantā (syā॰), aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā kilaisā, sabbai ca pariyuṭṭhānā, yai ca saṅkhārā paunaubhavikā appamāṇau niraudhau acalaṭṭhaina asaṅkhataṭṭhaina| yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā appamāṇau| iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ susamāraddhau iti cai mai hautīti – baujjhaṅgau? visamā kilaisā, sabbai  ca pariyuṭṭhānā, yai ca saṅkhārā paunaubhavikā, samadhammau niraudhau santaṭṭhaina paṇītaṭṭhaina| yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā susamāraddhau iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ ‘‘tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ tiṭṭhatī’’ti pajānāmi; sacaipi cavati ‘‘idappac‍cayā cavatī’’ti pajānāmi? katihākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati? katihākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati? aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati| aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati|

katamaihi  aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati? anuppādaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, uppādaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, appavattaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, pavattaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, animittaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, nimittaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, niraudhaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, saṅkhārai anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati|

katamaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati? uppādaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, anuppādaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, pavattaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, appavattaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, nimittaṃ āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, animittaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau  cavati, saṅkhārai āvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati, niraudhaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi satisambaujjhaṅgau cavati| aivaṃ  ‘‘tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ tiṭṭhatī’’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi cavati, ‘‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’’ti pajānāmi…pai॰…|

kathaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau? yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai hautīti baujjhaṅgau| saiyyathāpi tailappadīpassa jhāyatau yāvatā ac‍ci tāvatā vaṇṇau, yāvatā vaṇṇau tāvatā ac‍ci| aivamaiva yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ appamāṇau iti cai hautīti baujjhaṅgau? pamāṇabaddhā kilaisā, sabbai ca pariyuṭṭhānā, yai ca saṅkhārā paunaubhavikā appamāṇau niraudhau acalaṭṭhaina asaṅkhataṭṭhaina| yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā appamāṇau iti cai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ susamāraddhau iti cai hautīti baujjhaṅgau? visamā kilaisā, sabbai ca pariyuṭṭhānā, yai ca saṅkhārā paunaubhavikā samadhammau niraudhau  santaṭṭhaina paṇītaṭṭhaina| yāvatā niraudhūpaṭṭhāti tāvatā susamāraddhau iti cai hautīti baujjhaṅgau|

kathaṃ ‘‘tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ tiṭṭhatī’’ti pajānāmi; sacaipi cavati ‘‘idappac‍cayā  mai cavatī’’ti pajānāmi? katihākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati? katihākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati? aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati| aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati|

katamaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati? anuppādaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, uppādaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, appavattaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, pavattaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, animittaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, nimittaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, niraudhaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati, saṅkhārai anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tiṭṭhati|

katamaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati? uppādaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, anuppādaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, pavattaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, appavattaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, nimittaṃ āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, animittaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, saṅkhārai āvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati, niraudhaṃ anāvaj‍jitattā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau cavati| aivaṃ ‘‘tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ tiṭṭhatī’’ti pajānāmi; sacaipi cavati, ‘‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’’ti pajānāmi|

baujjhaṅgakathā niṭṭhitā|

4. maittākathā

22. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘maittāya, bhikkhavai, caitauvimuttiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya  paricitāya susamāraddhāya aikādasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai aikādasa? sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, manussānaṃ piyau hauti, amanussānaṃ piyau hauti, daivatā rakkhanti, nāssa aggi vā visaṃ vā satthaṃ vā kamati, tuvaṭaṃ cittaṃ samādhiyati, mukhavaṇṇau vippasīdati, asammūḷhau kālaṅkarauti, uttari uttariṃ (syā॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 11.15 passitabbā appaṭivijjhantau brahmalaukūpagau hauti| maittāya, bhikkhavai, caitauvimuttiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya – imai aikādasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’|

atthi anaudhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, atthi audhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, atthi disāpharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti| katihākāraihi anaudhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, katihākāraihi audhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, katihākāraihi disāpharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti? pañ‍cahākāraihi anaudhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, sattahākāraihi audhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti, dasahākāraihi disāpharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti|

katamaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anaudhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti? sabbai sattā avairā abyāpaj‍jā abyāpajjhā (syā॰) anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantu| sabbai pāṇā…pai॰… sabbai bhūtā…pai॰… sabbai puggalā…pai॰… sabbai attabhāvapariyāpan‍nā avairā abyāpaj‍jā  anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantūti| imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anaudhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti|

katamaihi sattahākāraihi audhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti? sabbā itthiyau avairā abyāpaj‍jā  anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantu| sabbai purisā…pai॰… sabbai ariyā…pai॰… sabbai anariyā…pai॰… sabbai daivā…pai॰… sabbai manussā…pai॰… sabbai vinipātikā avairā abyāpaj‍jā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantūti| imaihi sattahākāraihi  audhisau pharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti|

katamaihi dasahākāraihi disāpharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti? sabbai puratthimāya disāya sattā avairā abyāpaj‍jā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantu| sabbai pacchimāya disāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai uttarāya disāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai dakkhiṇāya disāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai puratthimāya anudisāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai pacchimāya anudisāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai uttarāya anudisāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai dakkhiṇāya anudisāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai haiṭṭhimāya disāya sattā…pai॰… sabbai uparimāya disāya sattā avairā abyāpaj‍jā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantu| sabbai puratthimāya disāya pāṇā…pai॰… bhūtā… puggalā… attabhāvapariyāpan‍nā… sabbā itthiyau… sabbai purisā… sabbai ariyā… sabbai anariyā… sabbai daivā… sabbai manussā… sabbai vinipātikā avairā abyāpaj‍jā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantu| sabbai pacchimāya disāya vinipātikā…pai॰… sabbai uttarāya disāya vinipātikā… sabbai dakkhiṇāya disāya vinipātikā… sabbai puratthimāya anudisāya vinipātikā… sabbai pacchimāya anudisāya vinipātikā… sabbai uttarāya anudisāya vinipātikā… sabbai dakkhiṇāya anudisāya vinipātikā… sabbai haiṭṭhimāya disāya vinipātikā… sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairā abyāpaj‍jā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantūti| imaihi dasahākāraihi  disāpharaṇā maittācaitauvimutti|

1. indriyavārau

23. sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ pīḷanaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apīḷanāya, upaghātaṃ vaj‍jaitvā anupaghātaina, santāpaṃ vaj‍jaitvā asantāpaina, pariyādānaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apariyādānaina, vihaisaṃ vaj‍jaitvā avihaisāya, sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu mā vairinau, sukhinau hauntu mā dukkhinau, sukhitattā hauntu mā dukkhitattāti – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi sabbai sattai maittāyatīti – maittā| taṃ dhammaṃ caitayatīti – caitau| sabbabyāpādapariyuṭṭhānaihi  vimuc‍catīti – vimutti| maittā ca caitau ca vimutti cāti – maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – saddhāya adhimuc‍cati| saddhindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – vīriyaṃ paggaṇhāti| vīriyindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| satindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – cittaṃ samādahati| samādhindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – pañ‍ñāya pajānāti| pañ‍ñindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imāni  pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti bhāvīyati| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bahulīkatā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti bahulīkarīyati| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā alaṅkārā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti svālaṅkatā hauti| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parikkhārā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparikkhatā hauti| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parivārā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparivutā hauti| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti, bhāvanā haunti, bahulīkatā haunti, alaṅkārā haunti, parikkhārā haunti, parivārā haunti, pāripūrī haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti, pakkhandanā haunti, pasīdanā saṃsīdanā (ka॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) haunti, santiṭṭhanā haunti, vimuc‍canā haunti, ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti phassanā haunti, yānīkatā haunti, vatthukatā haunti, anuṭṭhitā  haunti, paricitā haunti, susamāraddhā haunti, subhāvitā haunti, svādhiṭṭhitā haunti, susamuggatā haunti, suvimuttā haunti, nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

2. balavārau

24. sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – assaddhiyai na kampati| saddhābalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – kausaj‍jai na kampati| vīriyabalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – pamādai na kampati| satibalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – uddhac‍cai na kampati| samādhibalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – avij‍jāya na kampati| pañ‍ñābalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti bhāvīyati| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bahulīkatā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti bahulīkarīyati| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā alaṅkārā  haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti svālaṅkatā hauti| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parikkhārā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparikkhatā hauti| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parivārā  haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparivutā hauti| imāni pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti, bhāvanā haunti, bahulīkatā haunti, alaṅkārā haunti, parikkhārā haunti, parivārā haunti, pāripūrī haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti, pakkhandanā haunti, pasīdanā haunti, santiṭṭhanā haunti, vimuc‍canā  haunti, ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti phassanā haunti, yānīkatā  haunti, vatthukatā haunti, anuṭṭhitā haunti, paricitā haunti, susamāraddhā haunti, subhāvitā haunti, svādhiṭṭhitā haunti, susamuggatā haunti, suvimuttā haunti nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

3. baujjhaṅgavārau

25. sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| satisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – pañ‍ñāya pavicināti| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – vīriyaṃ paggaṇhāti| vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti  maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – pariḷāhaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| pītisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – duṭṭhul‍laṃ paṭippassambhaiti| passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – cittaṃ samādahati| samādhisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – ñāṇaina kilaisai paṭisaṅkhāti| upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvanā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti bhāvīyati| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya  caitauvimuttiyā bahulīkatā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti bahulīkarīyati| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā alaṅkārā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti svālaṅkatā hauti| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parikkhārā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparikkhatā hauti| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parivārā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi  maittācaitauvimutti suparivutā hauti| imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti, bhāvanā haunti, bahulīkatā haunti, alaṅkārā haunti, parikkhārā haunti, parivārā haunti, pāripūrī haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti, pakkhandanā haunti, pasīdanā haunti, santiṭṭhanā haunti, vimuc‍canā  haunti, ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti phassanā haunti, yānīkatā haunti, vatthukatā haunti, anuṭṭhitā haunti, paricitā haunti, susamāraddhā haunti, subhāvitā haunti, svādhiṭṭhitā haunti, susamuggatā haunti, suvimuttā haunti, nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

4. maggaṅgavārau

26. sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā passati| sammādiṭṭhiparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā abhiniraupaiti| sammāsaṅkappaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā pariggaṇhāti| sammāvācāparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā samuṭṭhāpaiti| sammākammantaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā vaudāpaiti| sammāājīvaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai  sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā paggaṇhāti| sammāvāyāmaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā upaṭṭhāpaiti| sammāsatiparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā samādahati| sammāsamādhiparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvanā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti bhāvīyati| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bahulīkatā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti bahulīkarīyati| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā alaṅkārā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti svālaṅkatā haunti| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parikkhārā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparikkhatā hauti| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā parivārā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparivutā hauti| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā  haunti, bhāvanā haunti, bahulīkatā haunti, alaṅkārā haunti, parikkhārā haunti, parivārā haunti, pāripūrī haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti, pakkhandanā haunti, pasīdanā haunti, santiṭṭhanā haunti, vimuc‍canā haunti, ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti phassanā haunti, yānīkatā haunti, vatthukatā haunti, anuṭṭhitā haunti, paricitā haunti, susamāraddhā haunti, subhāvitā haunti, svādhiṭṭhitā haunti, susamuggatā haunti, suvimuttā haunti nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

27. sabbaisaṃ pāṇānaṃ…pai॰… sabbaisaṃ bhūtānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ puggalānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ attabhāvapariyāpan‍nānaṃ… sabbāsaṃ itthīnaṃ… sabbaisaṃ purisānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ ariyānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ anariyānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ daivānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ manussānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ vinipātikānaṃ pīḷanaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apīḷanāya, upaghātaṃ vaj‍jaitvā anupaghātaina, santāpaṃ vaj‍jaitvā asantāpaina, pariyādānaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apariyādānaina, vihaisaṃ vaj‍jaitvā avihaisāya, sabbai vinipātikā avairinau hauntu mā  vairinau, sukhinau hauntu mā dukkhinau, sukhitattā hauntu mā dukkhitattāti – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi sabbai vinipātikai maittāyatīti – maittā| taṃ dhammaṃ caitayatīti – caitau| sabbabyāpādapariyuṭṭhānaihi vimuc‍catīti – vimutti| maittā ca caitau ca vimutti cāti – maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai vinipātikā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – saddhāya  adhimuc‍cati| saddhindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti…pai॰… nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

sabbaisaṃ puratthimāya disāya sattānaṃ…pai॰… sabbaisaṃ pacchimāya disāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uttarāya disāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ dakkhiṇāya disāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ puratthimāya anudisāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ pacchimāya anudisāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uttarāya anudisāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ dakkhiṇāya anudisāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ haiṭṭhimāya disāya sattānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uparimāya disāya sattānaṃ pīḷanaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apīḷanāya, upaghātaṃ vaj‍jaitvā anupaghātaina, santāpaṃ vaj‍jaitvā asantāpaina, pariyādānaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apariyādānaina, vihaisaṃ vaj‍jaitvā avihaisāya, sabbai uparimāya disāya sattā avairinau hauntu mā vairinau, sukhinau hauntu  mā dukkhinau, sukhitattā hauntu mā dukkhitattāti – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi sabbai uparimāya disāya sattai maittāyatīti – maittā| taṃ dhammaṃ caitayatīti – caitau| sabbabyāpādapariyuṭṭhānaihi vimuc‍catīti – vimutti| maittā ca caitau ca vimutti cāti – maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai uparimāya disāya sattā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – saddhāya adhimuc‍cati| saddhindriyaparibhāvitā  hauti maittācaitauvimutti…pai॰… nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

sabbaisaṃ puratthimāya disāya pāṇānaṃ…pai॰… bhūtānaṃ… puggalānaṃ  attabhāvapariyāpan‍nānaṃ… sabbāsaṃ itthīnaṃ… sabbaisaṃ purisānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ ariyānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ anariyānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ daivānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ manussānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ pacchimāya disāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uttarāya disāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ dakkhiṇāya disāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ puratthimāya anudisāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ  pacchimāya anudisāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uttarāya anudisāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ dakkhiṇāya anudisāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ haiṭṭhimāya disāya vinipātikānaṃ… sabbaisaṃ uparimāya disāya vinipātikānaṃ pīḷanaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apīḷanāya, upaghātaṃ vaj‍jaitvā anupaghātaina, santāpaṃ vaj‍jaitvā asantāpaina, pariyādānaṃ vaj‍jaitvā apariyādānaina, vihaisaṃ vaj‍jaitvā avihaisāya, sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairinau hauntu mā vairinau, sukhinau hauntu mā dukkhinau, sukhitattā hauntu mā dukkhitattāti – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikai maittāyatīti – maittā| taṃ dhammaṃ caitayatīti – caitau| sabbabyāpādapariyuṭṭhānaihi vimuc‍catīti – vimutti| maittā ca caitau ca vimutti cāti – maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – saddhāya adhimuc‍cati| saddhindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikā  avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – vīriyaṃ paggaṇhāti| vīriyindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

satiṃ  upaṭṭhāpaiti| satindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| cittaṃ samādahati| samādhindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| pañ‍ñāya pajānāti| pañ‍ñindriyaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imāni pañ‍cindriyāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati…pai॰… nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

sabbai  uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – assaddhiyai na kampati| saddhābalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| kausaj‍jai na kampati| vīriyabalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| pamādai na kampati| satibalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| uddhac‍cai na kampati| samādhibalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| avij‍jāya na kampati| pañ‍ñābalaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imāni  pañ‍ca balāni maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi pañ‍cahi balaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati…pai॰… nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

sabbai uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau  hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| satisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

pañ‍ñāya pavicināti| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| vīriyaṃ paggaṇhāti| vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| pariḷāhaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| pītisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| duṭṭhul‍laṃ paṭippassambhaiti| passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| cittaṃ samādahati| samādhisambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| ñāṇaina kilaisai paṭisaṅkhāti| upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imai satta baujjhaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati…pai॰… nibbattainti jautainti patāpainti|

sabbai  uparimāya disāya vinipātikā avairinau hauntu, khaiminau hauntu, sukhinau hauntūti – sammā passati| sammādiṭṭhi paribhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā abhiniraupaiti| sammāsaṅkappaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā pariggaṇhāti| sammāvācāparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā samuṭṭhāpaiti| sammākammantaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā vaudāpaiti| sammā ājīvaparibhāvitā hauti  maittācaitauvimutti| sammā paggaṇhāti| sammāvāyāmaparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā upaṭṭhāti| sammāsatiparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti| sammā samādahati| sammāsamādhiparibhāvitā hauti maittācaitauvimutti|

imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti āsaivīyati…pai॰… imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā paribhāvitā haunti| imaihi aṭṭhahi maggaṅgaihi maittācaitauvimutti suparivutā hauti| imai aṭṭha maggaṅgā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivanā haunti, bhāvanā haunti, bahulīkatā haunti, alaṅkārā haunti, parikkhārā haunti, parivārā haunti, pāripūrī  haunti, sahagatā haunti, sahajātā haunti, saṃsaṭṭhā haunti, sampayuttā haunti, pakkhandanā haunti, pasīdanā haunti, santiṭṭhanā haunti, vimuc‍canā haunti, ‘‘aitaṃ santa’’nti phassanā haunti, yānīkatā haunti, vatthukatā haunti, anuṭṭhitā haunti, paricitā haunti, susamāraddhā haunti, subhāvitā haunti, svādhiṭṭhitā haunti, susamuggatā haunti, suvimuttā haunti nibbattainti jautainti patāpaintīti|

maittākathā niṭṭhitā|

5. virāgakathā

28. virāgau  maggau, vimutti phalaṃ| kathaṃ virāgau maggau? sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā viraj‍jati, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca viraj‍jati, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi viraj‍jati| virāgau  virāgārammaṇau virāgagaucarau virāgai samudāgatau samupāgatau (syā॰) virāgai ṭhitau virāgai patiṭṭhitau|

virāgauti dvai virāgā – nibbānañ‍ca virāgau, yai ca nibbānārammaṇatājātā dhammā sabbai virāgā hauntīti – virāgā| sahajātāni sattaṅgāni virāgaṃ gacchantīti – virāgau maggau| aitaina maggaina buddhā ca sāvakā ca agataṃ disaṃ nibbānaṃ gacchantīti – aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| yāvatā puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ parappavādānaṃ maggā, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau vimaukkhau (syā॰), maukkhau (aṭṭha॰) ca uttamau ca pavarau cāti – maggānaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikau saiṭṭhau|

abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau micchāsaṅkappā viraj‍jati| pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācā micchāvācāya viraj‍jati| samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantau micchākammantā viraj‍jati| vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvau micchāājīvā viraj‍jati| paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmau micchāvāyāmā viraj‍jati| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina  sammāsati micchāsatiyā viraj‍jati| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi micchāsamādhitau viraj‍jati| tadanuvattakakilaisaihi  ca khandhaihi ca viraj‍jati| bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi viraj‍jati| virāgau virāgārammaṇau virāgagaucarau virāgai samudāgatau virāgai ṭhitau virāgai patiṭṭhitau|

virāgauti dvai virāgā – nibbānañ‍ca virāgau, yai ca nibbānārammaṇatājātā dhammā sabbai virāgā hauntīti – virāgā| sahajātāni sattaṅgāni virāgaṃ gacchantīti – virāgau maggau| aitaina maggaina buddhā ca sāvakā ca agataṃ disaṃ nibbānaṃ gacchantīti – aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| yāvatā puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ parappavādānaṃ maggā, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cāti – maggānaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikau saiṭṭhau|
