[ka] kathaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī viharati?  idhaikac‍cau pathavīkāyaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau; dukkhatau anupassati, nau sukhatau; anattatau anupassati, nau attatau; nibbindati, nau nandati; viraj‍jati, nau raj‍jati; niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti, paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, dukkhatau anupassantau sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, anattatau anupassantau attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, nibbindantau nandiṃ pajahati, viraj‍jantau rāgaṃ pajahati, niraudhaintau samudayaṃ pajahati, paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi kāyaṃ anupassati| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

idhaikac‍cau āpaukāyaṃ…pai॰… taijaukāyaṃ… vāyaukāyaṃ… kaisakāyaṃ… laumakāyaṃ… chavikāyaṃ… cammakāyaṃ… maṃsakāyaṃ… rudhirakāyaṃ… nhārukāyaṃ nahārukāyaṃ (syā॰) … aṭṭhikāyaṃ… aṭṭhimiñ‍jakāyaṃ  anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau; dukkhatau anupassati, nau sukhatau; anattatau anupassati, nau attatau; nibbindati, nau nandati; viraj‍jati, nau raj‍jati, niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti; paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, dukkhatau anupassantau sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, anattatau anupassantau attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, nibbindantau  nandiṃ pajahati, viraj‍jantau rāgaṃ pajahati, niraudhaintau samudayaṃ pajahati, paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi kāyaṃ anupassati| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

[kha] kathaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idhaikac‍cau sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi vaidanaṃ anupassati| vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā…pai॰… idhaikac‍cau dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ… sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ… nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ… sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ… nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ… sāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ… nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ… cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… sautasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… ghānasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… jivhāsamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… kāyasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ… manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi vaidanaṃ anupassati| vaidanā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ vaidanaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti  catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… aivaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati|

[ga] kathaṃ  cittai cittānupassī viharati?  idhaikac‍cau sarāgaṃ cittaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi cittaṃ anupassati| cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā|

idhaikac‍cau vītarāgaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… sadausaṃ cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ cittaṃ… samauhaṃ cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ cittaṃ… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi cittaṃ anupassati| cittaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina  ñāṇaina taṃ cittaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘cittai cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti catassau bhāvanā…pai॰… āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ cittai cittānupassī viharati|

[gha] kathaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idhaikac‍cau ṭhapaitvā kāyaṃ ṭhapaitvā vaidanaṃ ṭhapaitvā cittaṃ tadavasaisai dhammai anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau; dukkhatau anupassati, nau sukhatau; anattatau anupassati, nau attatau; nibbindati, nau nandati; viraj‍jati, nau raj‍jati; niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti; paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, dukkhatau anupassantau sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, anattatau anupassantau attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahati, nibbindantau nandiṃ pajahati, viraj‍jantau rāgaṃ pajahati, niraudhaintau samudayaṃ pajahati, paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati| imaihi sattahi ākāraihi tai dhammai anupassati| dhammā upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina tai  dhammai anupassati| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānā’’|

bhāvanāti  catassau bhāvanā – tattha jātānaṃ dhammānaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, indriyānaṃ aikarasaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, tadupagavīriyavāhanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā, āsaivanaṭṭhaina bhāvanā| aivaṃ  dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharatīti|

satipaṭṭhānakathā niṭṭhitā|

9. vipassanākathā

36. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.99-100 passitabbā saṅkhāraṃ nic‍catau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā arahattaphalaṃ vā (syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.98 passitabbā sacchikarissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasaṅkhārai anic‍catau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ sukhatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti  – naitaṃ  ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasaṅkhārai dukkhatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci dhammaṃ attatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbadhammai kañ‍ci dhammaṃ (syā॰), kiñ‍ci dhammaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.100 passitabbā anattatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nibbānaṃ dukkhatau samanupassantau  anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nibbānaṃ sukhatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; anulaumikāya  khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati’’|

37. katihākāraihi  anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati, katihākāraihi sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati? cattārīsāya ākāraihi anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati, cattārīsāya ākāraihi sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

katamaihi cattārīsāya ākāraihi anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati, katamahi cattārīsāya ākāraihi sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati? pañ‍cakkhandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau ītitau upaddavatau bhayatau upasaggatau calatau pabhaṅgutau pabhaṅgatau (syā॰) addhuvatau atāṇatau attāṇatau (syā॰) alaiṇatau asaraṇatau rittatau  tucchatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau ādīnavatau vipariṇāmadhammatau asārakatau aghamūlatau vadhakatau vibhavatau sāsavatau saṅkhatatau mārāmisatau jātidhammatau jarādhammatau byādhidhammatau maraṇadhammatau saukadhammatau paridaivadhammatau upāyāsadhammatau saṃkilaisikadhammatau|

38. pañ‍cakkhandhai anic‍catau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau nic‍caṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai dukkhatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau sukhaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai raugatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau āraugyaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai gaṇḍatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau agaṇḍaṃ nigaṇḍau (syā॰) nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai sal‍latau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau visal‍laṃ nissal‍laṃ (syā॰) nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai  aghatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anaghau nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai ābādhatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anābādhau nibbānanti  passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai paratau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau aparappac‍cayaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai palaukatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau apalaukadhammau nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai ītitau passantau  anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anītikaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai upaddavatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anupaddavaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai bhayatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau abhayaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai upasaggatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anupasaggaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai calatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau acalaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai pabhaṅgutau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau apabhaṅgu appabhaṅgaṃ (syā॰) nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai addhuvatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ  niraudhau dhuvaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai atāṇatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau tāṇaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai alaiṇatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau laiṇaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai asaraṇatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau saraṇaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai  rittatau passantau anulaumikaṃ  khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau arittaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai tucchatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau atucchaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai suñ‍ñatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau paramasuñ‍ñaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai anattatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau paramatthaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai ādīnavatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anādīnavaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai vipariṇāmadhammatau  passantau anulaumikaṃ  khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau avipariṇāmadhammaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai asārakatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau sāraṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai aghamūlatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anaghamūlaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai vadhakatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau avadhakaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai vibhavatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau avibhavaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai sāsavatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anāsavaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai saṅkhatatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau asaṅkhataṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ  auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai mārāmisatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau nirāmisaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai jātidhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau ajātaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ  auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai jarādhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ  niraudhau ajaraṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai byādhidhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau abyādhi abyādhidhammaṃ (syā॰) nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

pañ‍cakkhandhai maraṇadhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau amataṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai saukadhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau asaukaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai paridaivadhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau aparidaivaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai upāyāsadhammatau passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau anupāyāsaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati| pañ‍cakkhandhai saṃkilaisikadhammatau  passantau anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ niraudhau asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ nibbānanti passantau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

39. anic‍catauti, anic‍cānupassanā| dukkhatauti, dukkhānupassanā| raugatauti, dukkhānupassanā| gaṇḍatauti, dukkhānupassanā| sal‍latauti, dukkhānupassanā| aghatauti, dukkhānupassanā| ābādhatauti, dukkhānupassanā| paratauti, anattānupassanā| palaukatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| ītitauti, dukkhānupassanā|

upaddavatauti, dukkhānupassanā| bhayatauti, dukkhānupassanā| upasaggatauti, dukkhānupassanā| calatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| pabhaṅgutauti, anic‍cānupassanā| addhuvatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| atāṇatauti, dukkhānupassanā| alaiṇatauti, dukkhānupassanā| asaraṇatauti, dukkhānupassanā| rittatauti, anattānupassanā|

tucchatauti, anattānupassanā| suñ‍ñatauti, anattānupassanā| anattatauti, anattānupassanā| ādīnavatauti, dukkhānupassanā| vipariṇāmadhammatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| asārakatauti, anattānupassanā| aghamūlatauti, dukkhānupassanā| vadhakatauti, dukkhānupassanā| vibhavatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| sāsavatauti, dukkhānupassanā|

saṅkhatatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| mārāmisatauti, dukkhānupassanā| jātidhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| jarādhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| byādhidhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| maraṇadhammatauti, anic‍cānupassanā| saukadhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| paridaivadhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| upāyāsadhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā| saṃkilaisikadhammatauti, dukkhānupassanā|

imaihi cattālīsāya ākāraihi anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhati| imaihi cattālīsāya ākāraihi sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamati|

imaihi  cattālīsāya ākāraihi anulaumikaṃ khantiṃ paṭilabhantassa, imaihi cattālīsāya ākāraihi sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamantassa kati anic‍cānupassanā, kati dukkhānupassanā, kati anattānupassanā?

pañ‍cavīsati  anattānupassanā, pañ‍ñāsa anic‍cānupassanā|

sataṃ pañ‍cavīsati caiva, yāni dukkhai pavuc‍caraiti||

vipassanākathā niṭṭhitā|

10. mātikākathā

40. nicchātau  nicchātau muc‍catīti (syā॰), maukkhau vimaukkhau, vij‍jāvimutti, adhisīlaṃ, adhicittaṃ, adhipañ‍ñā, passaddhi, ñāṇaṃ, dassanaṃ visuddhi suddhi (syā॰) naikkhammaṃ, nissaraṇaṃ pavivaikau, vaussaggau, cariyā, jhānavimaukkhau, bhāvanā, adhiṭṭhānaṃ, jīvitaṃ|

41. nicchātauti  naikkhammaina kāmacchandatau nicchātau, abyāpādaina byāpādatau nicchātau…pai॰… paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇaihi nicchātau…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisaihi nicchātau|

maukkhau vimaukkhauti naikkhammaina kāmacchandatau muc‍catīti – maukkhau vimaukkhau| abyāpādaina byāpādatau muc‍catīti – maukkhau vimaukkhau…pai॰… paṭhamaina jhānaina nīvaraṇaihi muc‍catīti – maukkhau vimaukkhau…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisaihi  muc‍catīti – maukkhau vimaukkhau|

vij‍jāvimuttīti naikkhammaṃ vij‍jatīti vij‍jā, kāmacchandatau muc‍catīti vimutti| vij‍jantau muc‍cati, muc‍cantau vij‍jatīti – vij‍jāvimutti| abyāpādau abyāpādaṃ (syā॰) vij‍jatīti vij‍jā, byāpādatau vimuc‍catīti vimutti| vij‍jantau muc‍cati, muc‍cantau vij‍jatīti – vij‍jāvimutti…pai॰… arahattamaggau vij‍jatīti vij‍jā, sabbakilaisaihi muc‍catīti vimutti| vij‍jantau muc‍cati, muc‍cantau vij‍jatīti – vij‍jāvimutti|

adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ñāti naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ, saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi, dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| yau tattha saṃvaraṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā| yau tattha avikkhaipaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā| yau tattha dassanaṭṭhau, ayaṃ  adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai saṃvaraṭṭhaina sīlavisuddhi, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina cittavisuddhi| dassanaṭṭhaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| yau tattha saṃvaraṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā| yau tattha avikkhaipaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā| yau tattha dassanaṭṭhau, ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā|

passaddhīti naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ paṭippassambhaiti, abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ paṭippassambhaiti…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai paṭippassambhaiti|

ñāṇanti kāmacchandassa pahīnattā naikkhammaṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ; byāpādassa pahīnattā abyāpādau ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahīnattā arahattamaggau ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ|

dassananti kāmacchandassa pahīnattā naikkhammaṃ diṭṭhattā dassanaṃ| byāpādassa pahīnattā abyāpādau diṭṭhattā dassanaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ pahīnattā arahattamaggau diṭṭhattā dassanaṃ|

visuddhīti  kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammaina visujjhati| byāpādaṃ pajahantau abyāpādaina visujjhati…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggaina visujjhati|

naikkhammanti kāmānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ| rūpānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, yadidaṃ āruppaṃ| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ, niraudhau tassa naikkhammaṃ| byāpādassa abyāpādau naikkhammaṃ| thinamiddhassa ālaukasañ‍ñā naikkhammaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ arahattamaggau naikkhammaṃ|

nissaraṇanti kāmānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ| rūpānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, yadidaṃ āruppaṃ| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ, niraudhau tassa nissaraṇaṃ| kāmacchandassa naikkhammaṃ nissaraṇaṃ| byāpādassa abyāpādau nissaraṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ arahattamaggau nissaraṇaṃ|

pavivaikauti kāmacchandassa naikkhammaṃ pavivaikau  …pai॰… sabbakilaisānaṃ arahattamaggau pavivaikau|

vaussaggauti  naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ vaussaj‍jatīti – vaussaggau| abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ vaussaj‍jatīti – vaussaggau…pai॰… arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai vaussaj‍jatīti – vaussaggau|

cariyāti kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammaina carati| byāpādaṃ pajahantau abyāpādaina carati…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggaina carati|

jhānavimaukkhauti naikkhammaṃ jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| kāmacchandaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| jhāyantau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāpaintau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāyantīti dhammā| jhāpaintīti kilaisai| jhātai ca jhāpai ca jānātīti – jhānajhāyī jhānavimaukkhau (syā॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā| abyāpādau jhāyatīti jhānaṃ| byāpādaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ…pai॰… ālaukasañ‍ñā jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ…pai॰… arahattamaggau  jhāyatīti – jhānaṃ| sabbakilaisai jhāpaitīti – jhānaṃ| jhāyantau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāpaintau muc‍catīti – jhānavimaukkhau| jhāyantīti – dhammā| jhāpaintīti – kilaisai| jhātai ca jhāpai ca jānātīti – jhānajhāyī|

42. bhāvanā adhiṭṭhānaṃ jīvitanti kāmacchandaṃ pajahantau naikkhammaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau| naikkhammavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau| svāyaṃ aivaṃ bhāvanāsampan‍nau adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau  samaṃ jīvati, nau visamaṃ; sammā jīvati, nau micchā; visuddhaṃ jīvati, nau kiliṭṭhanti – ājīvasampan‍nau| svāyaṃ aivaṃ bhāvanāsampan‍nau adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau ājīvasampan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau bhāvanāsampan‍nau adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau ājīvasampan‍nau|

byāpādaṃ pajahantau abyāpādaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… thinamiddhaṃ pajahantau ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ pajahantau avikkhaipaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… vicikicchaṃ pajahantau dhammavavatthānaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… avij‍jaṃ pajahantau vij‍jaṃ bhāvaitīti  – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… aratiṃ pajahantau pāmauj‍jaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… nīvaraṇai pajahantau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau…pai॰… sabbakilaisai pajahantau arahattamaggaṃ bhāvaitīti – bhāvanāsampan‍nau| arahattamaggavasaina cittaṃ adhiṭṭhātīti – adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau| svāyaṃ aivaṃ bhāvanāsampan‍nau  adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau samaṃ jīvati, nau visamaṃ; sammā jīvati, nau micchā; visuddhaṃ jīvati, nau kiliṭṭhanti – ājīvasampan‍nau| svāyaṃ aivaṃ bhāvanāsampan‍nau adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau ājīvasampan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā  hi sau bhāvanāsampan‍nau adhiṭṭhānasampan‍nau ājīvasampan‍nauti|

mātikākathā niṭṭhitā|

pañ‍ñāvaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

pañ‍ñā  iddhi abhisamayau, vivaikau cariyapañ‍camau|

pāṭihāri samasīsi, satipaṭṭhānā vipassanā|

tatiyai pañ‍ñāvaggamhi, mātikāya ca tai dasāti||

mahāvaggau yuganaddhau, pañ‍ñāvaggau ca nāmatau|

tayauva vaggā imamhi tivaggau yassa vikkhaipau (syā॰ ka॰), paṭisambhidāpakaraṇai||

anantanayamaggaisu, gambhīrau sāgarūpamau|

nabhañ‍ca tārakākiṇṇaṃ, thūlau jātassarau yathā|

kathikānaṃ visālāya, yaugīnaṃ ñāṇajautananti||

paṭisambhidāmaggapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
